# Targum Jonathan on Numbers

תרגום יונתן על במדבר

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers)

תרגום מרכזי על ספרי הנביאים שנקרא בציבור בבית הכנסת בתקופת התלמוד, ועדיין נקרא היום בקהילות של יהודי תימן.

Primary Targum on the books of the Prophets, read publicly in synagogues in talmudic times and still today by Yemenite Jews.

Sections: Chapter-Verse

Location: Tanakh/Targum/Targum Jonathan/Torah

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Targum Jonathan on Numbers (Segments: 1288, License: Public Domain)

Version (En): The Targum of Jonathan ben Uzziel, trans. J. W. Etheridge, London, 1862 (Segments: 1284)


## Chapter 1

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:1)


וידבר וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְסִינַי בְּמַשְׁכַּן זִימְנָא בְּחַד לְיַרְחָא דְאִיָיר הוּא יַרְחָא תִּנְיָינָא דְמִן שַׁתָּא תִּנְיֵיתָא לִזְמַן מִפַּקְהוֹן מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם לְמֵימָר


AND the Lord spake with Mosheh in the wilderness of Sinai, in the tabernacle of ordinance, on the first of the month Ijar, which was the second month of the second year from the time of their coming forth from the land of Mizraim, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:2)


קַבִּילוּ יַת חוּשְׁבַּן כָּל כְּנִישְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לִגְנִיסַתְהוֹן לְבֵית אֲבָהַתְהוֹן בְּמִנְיַן שְׁמָהָן כָּל דְכוּרָא לְגֻלְגְלַתְהוֹן


Take the account of the whole congregation of the Beni Israel, according to the families of their fathers' house, by the number of the names of all the males by their capitations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:3)


מִבַּר עַשְרִין שְׁנִין וּלְעֵילָא כָּל נָפִיק חֵילָא בְּיִשְרָאֵל תִּמְנוּן יַתְהוֹן לְחֵילֵיהוֹן אַנְתְּ וְאַהֲרן


From each son twenty years and upwards, every one going out in the host in Israel; thou and Aharon number them by their hosts.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:4)


וְעִמְכוֹן יְהוֹן גְבַר גְבַר לְשִׁבְטָא גְבַר רֵישׁ לְבֵית אַבְהָתוֹי הוּא


And let there be with you a man of each tribe, a chief of his father's house.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:5)


וְאִלֵין שְׁמָהָיַית גוּבְרַיָא דִיקוּמוּן עִמְכוֹן לִרְאוּבֵן אַמַרְכּוֹל אֱלִיצוּר בַּר שְׁדֵיאוּר


And these are the names of the men who shall stand with you. Of Reuben, Elizur bar Shedeur:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:6)


לְשִׁמְעוֹן אַמַרְכּוֹל שְׁלֻמִיאֵל בַּר צוּרִי שַׁדָי


of Shemeon, the leader, Shelumiel bar Zuri-Shaddai:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:7)


לִיהוּדָה אַמַרְכּוֹל נַחְשׁוֹן בַּר עַמִינָדָב


the prince of Jehudah, Nachshon bar Amminadab:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:8)


לִישָשׁכָר אַמַרְכּוֹל נְתַנְאֵל בַּר צוּעָר


the prince of Issakar, Nathaniel bar Zuar:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:9)


לִזְבוּלֻן אַמַרְכּוֹל אֱלִיאָב בַּר חֵילוֹן


the prince of Zebulon, Eliab bar Chelon:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:10)


לִבְנֵי יוֹסֵף לְאֶפְרַיִם אַמַרְכּוֹל אֱלִישָׁמָע בַּר עַמִיהוּד לִמְנַשֶׁה אַמַרְכּוֹל גַמְלִיאֵל בַּר פְּדָהצוּר


the prince of the Beni Joseph, of Ephraim, Elishama bar Ammihud: the prince of Menasheh, Gamliel bar Pedazur:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:11)


לְבִנְיָמִין אַמַרְכּוֹל אֲבִידָן בַּר גִדְעוֹנִי


the prince of Benjamin, Abidan bar Gideoni:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:12)


לְדָן אַמַרְכּוֹל אֲחִיעֶזֶר בַּר עַמִישַׁדָי


the prince of Dan, Achiezer bar Ammi-shadai:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:13)


לְאָשֵׁר אַמַרְכּוֹל פַּגְעִיאֵל בַּר עָכְרָן


the prince of Asher, Pagiel bar Achran:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:14)


לְגָד אַמַרְכּוֹל אֶלְיָסָף בַּר דְעוּאֵל


the prince of Gad, Eljasaph bar Dehuel:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:15)


לְנַפְתָּלִי אַמַרְכּוֹל אֲחִירַע בַּר עֵינָן


the prince of Naphtali, Achira bar Enan.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:16)


אִלֵין מְזַמְנֵי עַם כְּנִישְׁתָּא רַבְרְבֵי שִׁבְטַיָא דְאַבְהַתְהוֹן רֵישֵׁי אַלְפַיָא דְיִשְרָאֵל הִינוּן


These (were) the notables of the congregation of the people, chiefs of their fathers' tribes, heads of thousands in Israel these.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:17)


וּדְבַר משֶׁה וְאַהֲרן יַת גוּבְרַיָא הָאִלֵין דְאִתְפְּרָשׁוּ בִּשְׁמָהָן


And Mosheh and Aharon took these men who are expressed by their names:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:18)


וְיַת כָּל כְּנִישְׁתָּא כְּנָשׁוּ בְּחַד יוֹמָא לְיַרְחָא דְאִיָיר הוּא יַרְחָא תִּנְיָינָא וְאִתְיַיחְסוּ עַל זַרְעֲיַתְהוֹן לְבֵית אַבְהַתְהוֹן בְּמִנְיַן שְׁמָהָן מִבַּר עַשְרִין שְׁנִין וּלְעֵילָא לְגוּלְגְלַתְהוֹן


and they assembled all the congregation on the first day of the month Ijar, which is the second month, and recensed them by the families of their fathers' house, by the number of their names, from twenty years old and upward by their capitations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:19)


הֵיכְמָא דְפַקֵיד יְיָ יַת משֶׁה וּמְנָנוּן בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְסִינָי


As the Lord commanded Mosheh, they numbered them in the wilderness of Sinai.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:20)


וַהֲווֹ בְנֵי רְאוּבֵן בּוּכְרָא דְיִשְרָאֵל יִחוּסֵיהוֹן לִגְנִיסַתְהוֹן לְבֵית אַבְהַתְהוֹן בְּמִנְיַן שְׁמָהָן לְגוּלְגְלַתְהוֹן כָּל דְכוּרָא מִבַּר עַשְרִין שְׁנִין וּלְעֵילָא כָּל נָפִיק חֵילָא


And the sons of Reuben, the first-born of Israel by the families of the generations of their fathers' house, in the number of their names by their polls, every male from twenty years old and upward, all going forth in the host,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:21)


סְכוּמְהוֹן לְשִׁבְטָא דִרְאוּבֵן אַרְבְּעִין וְשִׁית אַלְפִין וַחֲמֵשׁ מְאָה


the sum of the tribe of Reuben, forty-six thousand Five hundred.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:22)


לִבְנוֹי דְשִׁמְעוֹן יִחוּסֵיהוֹן לִגְנִיסַתְהוֹן לְבֵית אַבְהַתְהוֹן בְּמִנְיַן שְׁמָהָן לְגֻלְגְלַתְהוֹן כָּל דְכוּרָא מִבַּר עַשְרִין שְׁנִין וּלְעֵילָא כָּל נָפִיק חֵילָא


Of the families of the Beni Shemeon, after the generations of their fathers' house in the number of their names by their polls, every male from twenty years and upward, every one going forth in the host,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:23)


סְכוּמְהוֹן לְשִׁבְטָא דְשִׁמְעוֹן חַמְשִׁין וְתִשְׁעָה אַלְפִין וּתְלַת מְאָה


the sum of the tribe of Shemeon, fifty-nine thousand three hundred:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:24)


לִבְנוֹי דְגָד יִחוּסֵיהוֹן לִגְנִיסַתְהוֹן לְבֵית אַבְהַתְהוֹן בְּמִנְיַן שְׁמָהָן לְגוּלְגְלַתְהוֹן כָּל דְכוּרָא מִבַּר עַשְרִין שְׁנִין וּלְעֵילָא כָּל נָפִיק חֵילָא


of the family of the Beni Gad, after the generation of their fathers' house, in the number of their names by their polls, every male from twenty years and upward,

סְכוּמְהוֹן לְשִׁבְטָא דְגָד אַרְבְּעִין וְחַמְשָׁא אַלְפִין וְשִׁית מְאָה וְחַמְשִׁין


the sum of the tribe of Dan, forty-five thousand six hundred and fifty.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:26)


לִבְנוֹי דִיהוּדָה יִחוּסֵיהוֹן לִגְנִיסַתְהוֹן לְבֵית אַבְהַתְהוֹן בְּמִנְיַן שְׁמָהָן לְגוּלְגְלַתְהוֹן כָּל דְכוּרָא מִבַּר עַשְרִין שְׁנִין וּלְעֵילָא כָּל נָפִיק חֵילָא


of the family of the Beni Jehudah, after the generation of their fathers' house, in the number of their names by their polls, every male from twenty years and upward,

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:27)

The sum of the tribe of Jehudah seventy-four thousand six hundred:

The sum of the tribe of Zebulon, fifty-seven thousand four hundred:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:32)


לִבְנוֹי דְיוֹסֵף לִבְנוֹי דְאֶפְרַיִם יִחוּסֵיהוֹן לִגְנִיסַתְהוֹן לְבֵית אַבְהַתְהוֹן בְּמִנְיַן שְׁמָהָן לְגוּלְגְלַתְהוֹן כָּל דְכוּרָא מִבַּר עַשְרִין שְׁנִין וּלְעֵילָא כָּל נָפִיק חֵילָא


of the family of the Beni Joseph of the family of the Beni Ephraim, after the generation of their fathers' house, in the number of their names by their polls, every male from twenty years and upward,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:33)

The sum of the tribe of Ephraim, forty thousand five hundred:

לִבְנוֹי דִמְנַשֶׁה יִחוּסֵיהוֹן לִגְנִיסַתְהוֹן לְבֵית אַבְהַתְהוֹן בְּמִנְיַן שְׁמָהָן לְגוּלְגְלַתְהוֹן כָּל דְכוּרָא מִבַּר עַשְרִין שְׁנִין וּלְעֵילָא כָּל נָפִיק חֵילָא


of the family of the Beni Menasheh, after the generation of their fathers' house, in the number of their names by their polls, every male from twenty years and upward,

The sum of the tribe of Menasheh, thirty-two thousand two hundred:

of the family of the Beni Benjamin, after the generation of their fathers' house, in the number of their names by their polls, every male from twenty years and upward,

The sum of the tribe of Benjamin, thirty five thousand four hundred:

of the family of the Beni Dan, after the generation of their fathers' house, in the number of their names by their polls, every male from twenty years and upward,

סְכוּמְהוֹן לְשִׁבְטָא דְאָשֵׁר אַרְבְּעִין וְחַד אַלְפִין וַחֲמֵשׁ מְאָה

of the family of the Beni Naphtali, after the generation of their fathers' house, in the number of their names by their polls, every male from twenty years and upward,

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:43)


סְכוּמְהוֹן לְשִׁבְטָא דְנַפְתָּלִי חַמְשִׁין וּתְלָתָא אַלְפִין וְאַרְבַּע מְאָה


The sum of the tribe of Naphtali, fifty-three thousand four hundred.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:44)


אִלֵין סְכוּמֵי מִנְיָינַיָא דִמְנָא משֶׁה וְאַהֲרן וְרַבְרְבֵי יִשְרָאֵל תְּרֵיסַר גוּבְרִין גַבְרָא חָד לְבֵית אַבְהָתוֹי הֲוָון


These are the sums of the numbered ones which Mosheh and Aharon, and the princes of Israel, twelve men, a man for each house of their fathers, did number.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:45)


וַהֲווֹ כָּל סְכוּמֵי מִנְיָינֵי בְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְבֵית אַבְהַתְהוֹן מִבַּר עַשְרִין שְׁנִין וּלְעֵילָא כָּל נָפִיק חֵילָא בְּיִשְרָאֵל


And all the sums of the numbered of Beni Israel, according to the house of their fathers, from twenty years old and upward, of every one who goeth forth with the host in Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:46

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:46](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:46)


וַהֲווֹ כָּל סְכוּמֵי שִׁית מְאָה וּתְלַת אַלְפִין וַחֲמֵשׁ מְאָה וְחַמְשִׁין


And all the sums (of the numbered of the Beni Israel) were six hundred and three thousand five hundred and fifty.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:47

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:47](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:47)


וְלֵיוָאֵי לְשִׁבְטָא דַאֲבָהַתְהוֹן לָא אִתְמְנִיאוּ בֵּינֵיהוֹן


But the Levites after their father's tribe were not numbered among them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:48

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:48](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:48)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


For the Lord had spoken with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:49

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:49](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:49)


בְּרַם יַת שִׁבְטָא דְלֵוִי לָא תִמְנֵי וְיַת חוּשְׁבַּנְהוֹן לָא תְקַבֵּל בְּגוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


Nevertheless, the tribe of Levi thou shalt not number, nor take their sum among the children of Israel:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:50

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:50](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:50)


וְאַנְתְּ מַנֵי יַת לֵוָאֵי עַל מַשְׁכְּנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא וְעַל כָּל מָנוֹי וְעַל כָּל דִילֵיהּ הִינוּן יִטְלוּן יַת מַשְׁכְּנָא וְיַת כָּל מָנוֹי וְהִינוּן יְשַׁמְשׁוּנֵיהּ וַחֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר לְמַשְׁכְּנָא יִשְׁרוּן


but thou shalt appoint the Levites over the tabernacle of the testimony, and over all its vessels, and whatever things pertain unto it. They shall carry the tabernacle and all its vessels, and do service in it; and round about the tabernacle shall they dwell.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:51

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:51](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:51)


וּבְמֵיטַל מַשְׁכְּנָא יִפְרְקוּן יָתֵיהּ לֵיוָאֵי וּבְמֵישְׁרֵי מַשְׁכְּנָא יְקִימוּן יָתֵיהּ לֵיוָאֵי וְחִילוֹנַי דְיִקְרַב יִתְקְטֵיל בְּאֵישָׁא מְצַלְהֲבָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ


And when the tabernacle is to go forward, the Levites shall take it apart; and when the tabernacle is to be stationary, the Levites are to uprear it: the common person who draweth near will be slain by a flaming fire from before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:52

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:52](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:52)


וְיִשְׁרוּן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל גְבַר עַל בֵּית מֵשְׁרוֹי וּגְבַר עַל טִקְסֵיהּ לְחֵילֵיהוֹן


And the sons of Israel shall encamp every one by the place of his own company, every one under his standard according to their hosts.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:53

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:53](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:53)


וְלֵיוָאֵי יִשְׁרוּן חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר לְמַשְׁכְּנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא וְלָא יְהֵי רוּגְזָא עַל כְּנִישְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְיִטְרוּן לֵיוָאֵי יַת מַטְרַת מַשְׁכְּנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא


But the Levites shall encamp round about the tabernacle of the testimony, that there may not be wrath upon the congregation of the children of Israel; and the Levites shall keep charge of the tabernacle of testimony.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 1:54

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:54](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/1:54)


וַעֲבָדוּ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל כְּכָל דְפַקֵיד יְיָ יַת משֶׁה הֵיכְדֵין עָבָדוּ


And the sons of Israel did according to all that the Lord commanded Mosheh, so did they.



## Chapter 2

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/2:1)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה וְעִם אַהֲרן לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh and with Aharon, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/2:2)


גְבַר עַל טִיקְסֵיהּ בְּאַתְוָן דְמִסְתַּמְנִין עַל טִיקְסֵיהוֹן לְבֵית אֲבָהַתְהוֹן יִשְׁרוּן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מִלְקָבֵיל סְחוֹר סְחוֹר לְמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא יִשְׁרוּן


Every man of the Beni Israel shall encamp by his standard, by the ensign which is signified upon the standards of their fathers' house over against the tabernacle of ordinance shall they encamp round about.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/2:3)


מַשְׁרִיתָא דְיִשְרָאֵל הֲוָה אוּרְכָּא תְּרֵיסַר מִילִין וּפוּתְיָהּ תְּרֵיסַר מִילִין וְדִישְׁרַן קִידוּמָא מַדִינְחָא טֵיקַס מַשְׁרִיתָא יְהוּדָה לְחֵילֵיהוֹן בְּאַרְבַּעְתֵּי מִילִין מְרַבְּעִין וְטִיקְסֵיהּ הֲוָה מִמֵילַת תְּלַת גְוָונִין כָּל קְבֵיל תְּלַת מַרְגַלְיָתָא דִבְחוּשְׁנָא סְמוּקָא וְיָרוּקָא וּבְרוֹקָא וּבֵיהּ חָקִיק וּמְפָרֵשׁ שְׁמָהַת תְּלַת שִׁבְטַיָא יְהוּדָה יִשָשׁכָר וּזְבוּלוּן וּבִמְצִיעוּתֵיהּ כְּתִיב יְקוּם יְיָ וְיִתְבַּדְרוּן סַנְאָךְ וְיִעַרְקוּן בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבָךְ מִן קֳדָמָךְ וּבֵיהּ הֲוָה חָקִיק צוּרַת בַּר אַרְיְוָן מְטוֹל דְרַבָּא לִבְנֵי יְהוּדָה נַחְשׁוֹן בַּר עַמִינָדָב


The length of the camp of Israel shall be twelve miles, and its breadth twelve miles. And they who encamp eastward to the sunrise shall be of the standard of the camp of Jehudah by their hosts, spreading over four miles. And his standard shall be of silk, of three colours, corresponding with (those of) the precious stones which are in the breastplate,--sardius, topaz, and carbuncle; and upon it shall be expressed and set forth the names of the three tribes of Jehudah, Issakar, and Zebulon; and in the midst shall be written : Arise, O Lord, and let Thine enemies be scattered, and Thine adversaries be driven away before Thee; and upon it shall be set forth the figure of a young lion. And the Rabba of the Beni Jehudah shall be Nachshon bar Amminadab.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/2:4)


וְחֵילֵיהּ וּסְכוּמְהוֹן דְשִׁבְטַיָא שׁוּבְעִין וְאַרְבָּעָה אַלְפִין וְשִׁית מְאָה


And the sum of the hosts of that tribe was seventy-four thousand six hundred.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/2:5)

And they who encamp next by him shall be the tribe of Issakar, and the Rabba appointed over the host of the Beni Issakar, Nathaniel bar Zuar:

כָּל מִנְיָינַיָא לְמַשִׁרְיַת יְהוּדָה מְאָה וּתְמָנָן וְשִׁתָּא אַלְפִין וְאַרְבַּע מְאָה לְחֵילְוַותְהוֹן בְּקַדְמֵיתָא נַטְלִין


All the numbered ones of the camp of Jehudah, one hundred and eighty-six thousand four hundred, by their hosts; they shall go forward in front.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/2:10)


טֵיקַס מַשִׁרְיַת רְאוּבֵן דָרוֹמָא יִשְׁרוּן לְחֵילֵיהוֹן בְּאַרְבַּעְתֵּי מִילִין מְרַבְּעָן וְטִיקְסֵיהּ הֲוָה מִמֵילַת תְּלַת גַוְונִין כָּל קְבֵל תְּלַת מַרְגַלְיָיתָא דִבְחוּשְׁנָא אַזְמוֹרַד וְשַׁבְזִיז וְסַבְהֲלוֹם וּבֵיהּ חָקִיק וּמְפָרֵשׁ שְׁמָהַת תְּלַת שִׁבְטַיָא רְאוּבֵן שִׁמְעוֹן גָד וּבִמְצִיעוּתֵיהּ כְּתִיב שְׁמַע יִשְרָאֵל יְיָ אֱלָהָנָא יְיָ אֶחָד וּבֵיהּ הֲוָה חָקִיק צוּרַת בַּר אַיָלָא וַהֲוָה חָמֵי לְמֶהֱוֵי בֵּיהּ צוּרַת בַּר תּוֹרֵי בְּרַם משֶׁה נְבִיאָה חַלְפֵיהּ מְטוֹל דְלָא יִדְכַּר לְהוֹן חוֹבַת עֵיגְלָא וְרַבָּא דַהֲוָה מְמַנֵי עַל חֵילְוַת שִׁבְטָא דִרְאוּבֵן אֱלִיצוּר בַּר שְׁדֵיאוּר


(By) the standard of the camp of Reuben they, shall encamp southward by their hosts, spreading over four miles. And his standard shall be of silk, of three colours, corresponding with (those of) the precious stones that are in the breastplate, azmorad, sapphire, and adamant: on it shall be expressed and set forth the names of the three tribes of Reuben, Shemeon, Gad; and in the midst of it be written, Hear, Israel, the Lord our God is One; and upon it shall be set forth the figure of a stag. Some would have thought there should have been upon it the figure of a young ox; but Mosheh the prophet altered it, that the sin of the calf might not be remembered against them. And the Rabba set over the host of the tribe Reuben was Elizur bar Shedeur.

טֵיקַס מַשִׁרְיַית אֶפְרַיִם לְחֵילֵיהוֹן מַעַרְבָא יִשְׁרוּן וּמַשִׁירְיַתְהוֹן בְאַרְבַּעְתֵּי מִילִין מְרַבְּעַן וְטִיקְסֵיהּ הֲוָה מִמֵילַת תְּלַת גְוָונִין כָּל קְבֵיל תְּלַת מַרְגַלְיָיתָא דִבְחוּשְׁנָא קַנְכִּירִין טַרְקִין וְעֵינְעֵיגַל וּבֵיהּ חָקִיק וּמְפָרָשׁ שְׁמָהַת תְּלַת שִׁבְטַיָא אֶפְרַיִם וּמְנַשֶׁה וּבִנְיָמִין וּבִמְצִיעוּתֵיהּ כְּתִיב וַעֲנָנָא דַיְיָ עֲלֵיהוֹן בִּימָמָא בְּמַטְלֵיהוֹן מִן מַשְׁרִיתָא וּבֵיהּ הֲוָה חָקִיק צוּרַת רִיבָא וְרַבָּא דַהֲוָה מְמַנֵי עַל חֵילְוַת שִׁבְטָא דִבְנֵי אֶפְרַיִם אֱלִישָׁמָע בַּר עַמִיהוּד


The camp of Ephraim by the standard of their hosts shall pitch on the west; their camp spreadeth over four miles; and their standard is of silk of three colours, corresponding with the precious stones in the breastplate, ligure, agate, and amethyst; and upon it expressed and set forth the names of the three tribes, Ephraim, Menasheh and Benjamin, having written in the midst, And the Cloud of the Lord was over them, in the going forward of the host; and upon it was set forth the figure of a young man. And the Rabba set over the tribe of Ephraim, Elishama bar Ammihud;

טֵיקַס מַשִׁרְיַת דָן לְצִפּוּנָא לְחֵילֵיהוֹן וּבֵית מַשִׁירְיַתְהוֹן בְּאַרְבַּעְתֵּי מִילִין מְרַבְּעַן וְטִקְסֵיהּ הֲוָה מִמֵילַת תְּלַת גְוָונִין כָּל קְבֵיל תְּלַת מַרְגַלְיָיתָא דִבְחוּשְׁנָא כְּרוּם יַמָא וּבִירְלְוַות חַלָא וְאַפַּנְטוֹר וּבֵיהּ חָקִיק וּמְפָרַשׁ שְׁמָהַת תְּלַת שִׁבְטַיָא דָן וְנַפְתָּלִי וְאָשֵׁר וּבִמְצִיעוּתֵיהּ חָקִיק וּמְפָרַשׁ וּבְמֵישְׁרוֹי יֵימַר תּוּב יְיָ שְׁרֵי בִיקָרָךְ בְּגוֹ רִיבְוַות אַלְפַיָא דְיִשְרָאֵל וּבֵיהּ הֲוָה חָקִיק צוּרַת חֵיוֵי חָרְמַן וְרַבָּא דַהֲוָה מְמַנֵי עַל חֵילְוַת שִׁבְטָא דִבְנֵי דָן אֲחִיעֶזֶר בַּר עַמִישַׁדָי


The standard of the camp of Dan shall be to the north with their hosts; and the space of their camp shall spread over four miles. His standard shall be of silk of three colours, corresponding with the stones in the breastplate, chrysolite, beryl, and jasper; in it shall be expressed and set forth the names of the three tribes, Dan, Naphtali, and Asher; and upon it shall be expressed: And in his encampment shall he say, Return, O Lord, and dwell in Thy glory in the midst of the myriads of Israel; and upon it shall also be set forth the figure of a basilisk serpent. (Gen. xlix. 17.) The Rabba set over the hosts of Dan was Achiezer bar Aminishaddai,

and the numbers forty-one thousand five hundred.

Of the tribe Naphtali, the Rabba, Achira bar Enan;

The sum of the hosts of Dan was one hundred and fifty-seven thousand six hundred: and these went forward last with their ensigns.

This is the amount of the numbers of the Beni Israel, according to the house of their fathers; all the sums of the camps by their hosts were six hundred and three thousand five hundred and fifty.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/2:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/2:33)

But the Levites were not numbered among the sons of Israel, as the Lord commanded Mosheh.

And the sons of Israel did according to all that the Lord commanded Mosheh; so did they encamp by their standards, and so went they forward, every man with his family by the house of his fathers.

These are the generations of Aharon and Mosheb, who were genealogized in the day that the Lord spake with Mosheh in the mountain of Sinai.

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/3:3)

And these are the names of the Beni Aharon the priests, the disciples of Moses, the Rabbi of Israel; and they were called by his name in the day that they were anointed to minister in offering their oblations.

But Nadab and Abihu died by the flaming fire at the time of their offering the strange fire from their own tents; and they had no children. And Elazar and Ithamar ministered before Aharon their father.

For every first-born among the sons of Israel is Mine, from the day when. I slew every first-born in the land of Mizraim; I have sanctified before Me every first-born in Israel; from man to animal, they are Mine: I am the Lord.

And the Lord spake with Mosheh in the wilderness of Sinai, saying:

## Chapter 5

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:1)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:2)


פַּקֵיד יַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְיִפְטְרוּן מִן מַשְׁרִיתָא כָּל דִמְצוֹרָע וְכָל דְדָאִיב וְכָל דִמְסָאָב לִטְמֵי נַפְשָׁא דְמִית


Command the sons of Israel to send away from the camp every one who is leprous, or who hath an issue, or is unclean by having defiled himself (by touching) the dead.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:3)


מִדְכוּרָא וְעַד נוּקְבָא תְּשַׁלְחוּן לְמִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא תִּפְטוּרוּנוּן וְלָא יְסַאֲבוּן יַת מַשִׁרְיַיתְהוֹן דִשְׁכִינַת קוּדְשָׁא שַׁרְיָא בֵּינֵיהוֹן


From a male to a female thou shalt send them away, and separate them without the camp, that they may not defile their tents; for the Shekinah of My Holiness dwelleth among you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:4)


וַעֲבָדוּ כֵן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וּפַטְרוּ יַתְהוֹן לְמִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא הֵיכְמָא דְמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה הֵיכְדֵין עֲבָדוּ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


And the sons of Israel did so, and sent them away from the camp; as the Lord had commanded Mosheh, so did the sons of Israel.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:5)

And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:6)


מַלֵיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל גְבַר אוֹ אִיתְּתָא דִי יַעֲבְדוּן מִכָּל חוֹבֵי אֱנָשָׁא לִמְשַׁקְרָא שְׁקַר קֳדָם יְיָ וְיִתְחַיֵיב בַּר נַשׁ הַהוּא


Say to the children of Israel: A man or a woman who committeth any human sin, in acting perversely before the Lord, and hath become guilty;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:7)


וְיוֹדוּן יַת חוֹבֵיהוֹן דְעָבָדוּ אִין מָמוֹנָא אֲנַס לְחַבְרֵיהּ וְיָתוּב יַת חוֹבְתֵיהּ בְּרֵישֵׁיהּ וְחוֹמֶשׁ דְמוֹי יוֹסֵף עֲלוֹי וְיִתֵּן קַרְנָא וְחוּמְשָׁא לְמַאן דְאִתְחַיֵיב לֵיהּ


they shall make confession of their sins which they have committed. If he hath extorted money from his neighbour, he shall restore (the amount of) his sin in the principal thereof, and add to it a fifth of its value, and give (both) principal and fifth to him against whom he hath sinned.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:8)


וְאִין לָא אִית לְגַבֵּיהּ פָּרִיק לְאָתָבָא חוֹבְתֵיהּ לֵיהּ חוֹבְתֵיהּ דִמְיֵיתַב קֳדָם יְיָ לְכַהֲנָא יִתֵּן בַּר מִדְכַר כִּפּוּרַיָא דִי יְכַפֵּר בֵּיהּ עֲלוֹי


And if the man (hath died and) hath no kinsman to whom the debt may be rendered, the debt to be restored (shall he render) before the Lord; he shall give it to the priest, besides the ram for his atonement, by which atonement is to be made for him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:9)


וְכָל אַפְרָשׁוּתָא לְכָל קוּדְשַׁיָא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל דִיקַרְבוּן לְכַהֲנָא דִילֵיהּ יְהוֹן


And every separation of all consecrated things of the children of Israel which they bring to the priest shall be his.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:10)


וּגְבַר יַת מַעֲשֵר קוּדְשׁוֹי דִילֵיהּ יְהוֹן וְלָא חַסְרִין נִכְסוֹי גְבַר מַה דְיִתֵּן לְכַהֲנָא דִילֵיהּ יְהֵי


The consecrated tithe, also, of any man shall be his, that his substance may not fail; whatever a man giveth unto the priest shall be his.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:11)

And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:12)


מַלֵיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן גְבַר גְבַר אֲרוּם תִסְטֵי אִנְתְּתֵיהּ וּתְשַׁקֵר בֵּיהּ שְׁקָר


Speak with the sons of Israel, and say to them: If the wife of any man go astray and commit wrongness against him,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:13)


וִישַׁמֵשׁ גְבַר חוֹרָן עִמָהּ תַּשְׁמִישׁ דְעָרִיס וִיהֵי מְכַסֵי מֵעֵינֵי בַּעֲלָהּ וּמִטַמְרָא וְהִיא מְסָאֲבָא וְסַהֲדוּ בְּרִיר לֵית דְמַסְהִיד בָּהּ וְהִיא לָא אִתְחָדָת


and another man lie with her, and it be hidden from her husband's eyes, and be concealed, and she be contaminated: or, if the testimony be not clear which is witnessed against her, and she be not convicted;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:14)


וִיעִבַּר עֲלוֹי רוּחַ קִנְאָתָא וִיקַנֵי יַת אִתְּתֵיהּ וְהִיא אִסְתְּאָבַת אוֹ עָבַר עֲלוֹי רוּחַ קִנְאֵיתָא וִיקַנֵא יַת אִתְּתֵיהּ וְהִיא לָא אִסְתְּאָבָת


or, if the spirit of jealousy come upon him, and he be jealous of his wife, that she hath been defiled, or the spirit of jealousy come upon him, and he be jealous of his wife, though she hath not been defiled;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:15)


וּמְטוֹל דְלָא אַיְיתִי גַבְרָא הַהוּא אַפְרָשׁוּתָא וּמַעַשְרָא אִסְתְּקַף עֲלוֹי דְיַיתֵי יַת אִנְתְּתֵיהּ לְכַהֲנָא וּמְטוֹל דְאִיהִי אַטְעִימַת לְגִיוֹרָא תַפְנוּקִין אִסְתְּקַף עֲלָהּ דְיַיְתֵי יַת קוּרְבָּנָהּ דְקַיִץ עֲלָהּ מִדִילָהּ חַד מִן עַשְרָא בִּתְלַת סְאִין קִמְחָא דְשַעֲרֵי דְהִינוּן מֵיכְלָא דִבְעִירֵי לָא יְרִיק עֲלָהּ מִשְׁחָא וְלָא יִתֵּן עֲלָהּ לְבוֹנְתָּא אֲרוּם מִנְחַת קִנְאֵיתָא הִיא מִנְחַת דוּכְרָנָא מַדְכְּרַת חוֹבִין


and though that man may have not brought separation or tythe, there is constraint upon him to bring his wife unto the priest. Now, because she may have brought delicacies to the adulterer, she ought to bring an appointed oblation of her own, a tenth of three sata of barley flour, that being the food of beasts: he shall not pour oil, nor put frankincense thereon; for it is a mincha (on account) of jealousy, a mincha of a memorial which calleth guilt to mind.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:16)


וִיקָרֵב יָתָהּ כַּהֲנָא וִיקִימִינָהּ קֳדָם יְיָ


And the priest shall bring her near, and cause her to stand before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:17)


וְיִסַב כַּהֲנָא מַיִין קַדִישִׁין מִן כִּיוֹרָא בְּנַטְלָא וְיִתְּנִינוּן בְּמָאן דַחֲסַף מְטוֹל דְאִיהִי אַשְׁקְיַית לְגִיוֹרָא חֲמַר בָּסִים בְּמָאנִין יַקִירִין וּמִן עַפְרָא דִי יְהֵא בְּשִׁפּוּלֵי מַשְׁכְּנָא מְטוֹל דְסוֹף כָּל בִּישְרָא לְעַפְרָא יִסַב כַּהֲנָא וְיִתֵּן לְמַיָא


And the priest shall take holy water from the laver with an ewer, and pour it into an earthen vessel; because she may have brought the adulterer sweet wine to drink in precious vases; and he shall take of the dust that is upon the ground of the tabernacle,-because the end of all flesh is dust,- and put it into the water.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:18)


וְיוֹקִים כַּהֲנָא יַת אִיתְּתָא קֳדָם יְיָ וְיֶאֱסוֹר עַל חַרְצָא אַשְׁלָא מֵעִילוֹי חַדְיָיהָא מְטוֹל דְאִיהִי אַסְרַת חַרְצָהָא בְּצִילְצוֹלִין וְיִפְרַע יַת רֵישָׁא דְאִיתְּתָא מְטוֹל דְאִיהִי קַלְעַת שְעַר רֵישָׁא וְיִתֵּן עַל יְדָהָא יַת מִנְחַת דוּכְרָנָא מִנְחַת קִנְאֵיתָא הִיא וּבִידָא דְכַהֲנָא יְהוֹן מַיָא מְרִירַיָא בְּדוּקַיָא


And the priest shall cause the woman to stand before the Lord, and bind a cord over her loins and upon her breast,- because she should have bound her loins with a girdle; and he shall uncover the woman's head, because she had tied a fillet upon her hair. And he shall put the mincha of memorial, the mincha of jealousy, into her hand; while in the hand of the priest shall be the bitter water of the trial.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:19)


וְיוֹמֵי יָתָהּ כַּהֲנָא בִּשְׁבוּעַת שְׁמָא רַבָּא וְיַקִירָא וְיֵימַר כַּהֲנָא לְאִתְּתָא אִין לָא סָטִית לְאִסְתְּאָבָא בְּתַּשְׁמִישׁ דְעָרִיס בַּר מִן רְשׁוּתָא דְבַעֲלִיךְ תֶּהֱוֵי זַכָּאָה מִמַיָא מְרִירַיָא בְּדוּקַיָא הָאִלֵין


And the priest shall adjure her by the adjuration of the Great and Glorious Name, and shall say to the woman: If thou hast not turned aside, to defile thyself by acting against the right of thy husband, be thou unhurt by these bitter waters of trial.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:20)


וְאַנְתְּ אֲרוּם סַטְיָת בַּר מִן רְשׁוּתָא דְבַעֲלִיךְ וַאֲרוּם אִסְתְּאָבַת בְּתַּשְׁמִישׁ דְעָרִיס וִיהַב גְבַר בִּיךְ יַת תַּשְׁמִישֵׁיהּ בַּר מִן רְשׁוּתָא דְבַעֲלִיךְ


But if thou hast turned aside against the right of thy husband, and art defiled in having shared the bed with a man against thy husband's right:-


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:21)


וְיוֹמֵי כַּהֲנָא יַת אִתְּתָא בְּקִיוּם זְנוּמְתָּא וְיֵימַר כַּהֲנָא לְאִתְּתָא יִתֵּן יְיָ יָתִיךְ לִלְוַט וְלִמְמוֹמֵי בְּגוֹ בְּנֵי עַמִיךְ בִּדְיִתֵּן יְיָ יַת יְרֵכוֹנֵךְ מִתְמַסְיָן וְיַת כַּרְסִיךְ מְנַפְחָא


Then shall the priest adjure the woman by the oath of malediction, and say to the woman,-The Lord make thee a curse and an execration among the children of thy people, in causing thy thigh to corrupt, and thy belly to swell;.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:22)


וְיֵיעָלוּן מַיָא בְּדוּקַיָא הָאִלֵין בִּמְעַיְיכִי לִמְנַפְחָא כְּרֵיסָן וּלְמַסְיָא יַרְכִינוּן וְתַעֲנֶה אִתְּתָא וְתֵימַר אָמֵן אִין אִסְתְּאָבִית כַּד מֵאַרְסָא אָמֵן אִין אִסְתְּאָבִית כַּד נְסִיבְתָּא


and may these waters of trial enter into thy bowels, to cause thy belly to swell, and thy thigh to corrupt. And the woman shall answer and say: Amen, if I was polluted when betrothed; Amen, if I have been polluted since my marriage.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:23)


(וְתִכְתּוֹב) [צ"ל וְיִכְתּוֹב] יַת לְוָטַיָא הָאִלֵין כַּהֲנָא עַל מְגִילְתָּא וְיִמְחוֹק לְמַיָא בְּדוּקַיָא


And the priest shall write these maledictions upon a parchment, and wash it out with the water of trial,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:24)


וְיַשְׁקֵי יַת אִתְּתָא יַת מַיָא מְרִירַיְתָא בְּדוּקַיָא וְיֵעָלוּן בָּהּ מַיָא בְּדוּקַיָא לִלְוָט


and cause the woman to drink the bitter trial water: the trial water of malediction shall be received by her.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:25)


וְיִסַב כַּהֲנָא מִידָא דְאִתְּתָא יַת מִנְחָתָא דְקַנִיאֲתָא וְיָרִים יַת מִנְחָתָא קֳדָם יְיָ וִיקָרֵב יָתָהּ עַל גַבֵּי מַדְבְּחָא


But the priest shall (first) take from the woman's hand the mincha of jealousy, and uplift the mincha before the Lord, and lay it on the side of the altar.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:26)


וְיִקְמוֹץ כַּהֲנָא מִן מִנְחָתָא יַת צְרִיד אַדְכַּרְתָּא וְיַסִיק לְמַדְבְּחָא וּמִבָּתַר כֵּן יַשְׁקֵי יַת אִתְּתָא יַת מַיָא


And the priest shall take a handful of the portion for its memorial, and burn it at the altar; and after that the woman shall drink the water.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:27)


וְיַשְׁקִינָהּ יַת מַיָא וִיהֵי אִין אִסְתְּאָבַת בְּתַשְׁמִישׁ דְעָרִיס וְשַׁקְרַת שְׁקָר בְּבַעֲלָהּ וְיֵעָלוּן בָּהּ מַיָא בְּדוּקַיָא לִלְוָט וּתְנָפַח כְּרֵיסָהּ וְתִתְמְסֵי יַרְכָּהּ וּתְהֵי אִתְּתָא לִלְוָטָא בְּגוֹ בְּנֵי עַמָהּ בְּרַם לְגִיוֹרָא בַּדְקִין מַיָא בְּדוּקַיָא הָאִינוּן בְּכָל אַתְרָא דְהוּא תַּמָן


And when he hath caused her to drink the water, it will be that if she hath been defiled by adultery, and hath acted with wrongness against her husband, those proving waters will enter into her with a curse, and her belly will swell, and her thigh become corrupt, and the woman will be an execration among the children of her people. The adulterer as well will be detected by these waters of probation, in whatever place he may be.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:28)


וְאִין לָא אִסְתְּאָבַת אִנְתְּתָא בְּתַשְׁמִישׁ דְעָרִיס וּדְכִיתָא הִיא וְתִיפּוֹק זַכְיָא נְזִיוָהּ מַנְהֵר וְתִשְׁכַּח רַחְמִין קֳדָם בַּעֲלָהּ וְתִתְעַבַּר בְּבַר דְכָר


But if the woman hath not been defiled by adultery, but is innocent, they will enter without harm, and her brightness will shine forth, and she will find affection before her husband, and become the mother of a son.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:29)


דָא אַחְוָיַת אוֹרַיְיתָא קִנְאָתָא דְתִסְטֵי אִתְּתָא בַּר מִן רְשׁוּתָא דְבַעֲלָהּ וְתִסְתָּאֵב בְּתַּשְׁמִישׁ דְעָרִיס


This is the declaration of the law of jealousy, when a woman hath fallen away from the right of her husband, and become defiled by adultery;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:30)


אוֹ גְבַר דְיִתְעַבָּר עֲלוֹי רוּחַ קִנְאֵיתָא וִיקַנֵי יַת אִתְּתֵיהּ וְיָקִים יַת אִיתְּתָא קֳדָם יְיָ וִיעַבַּד לָהּ כַּהֲנָא יַת כָּל אוֹרַיְתָא הֲדָא


or when the spirit of jealousy cometh upon a man, that he be so jealous of his wife as to make her stand before the Lord, then shall the priest perform all this law.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 5:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/5:31)


וְאִין זַכַּאי גַבְרָא מֵחוֹבִין אִתְּתָא הַהִיא תְּקַבֵּל יַת חוֹבָאָהּ


But if the man be innocent of transgressions, then let that woman bear her iniquity.



## Chapter 6

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:1)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:2)


מַלֵיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן גְבַר אוֹ אִתְּתָא אֲרוּם חַמְיַין סְטִיתָא בְּקִלְקוּלָהּ וְיַפְרִישׁ מִן חַמְרָא אוֹ עַל שׁוּם מִדַעַם יִדוֹר נֶדֶר נְזִירוּ לְמִפְרַשׁ לִשְׁמָא דַיְיָ


Speak with the children of Israel, and say to them: When a man or, woman, seeing her who had gone astray in her corruption, shall (resolve to) become abstinent from wine, or for any other cause shall make the vow of a Nazir in separating one'- self unto the Name of the Lord,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:3)


מִן חֲמַר חֲדַת וְעַתִּיק יִפְרֵשׁ חֲלָא דַחֲמַר חֲדַת וַחֲלָא דַחֲמַר עַתִּיק לָא יִשְׁתֵּי וְכָל שִׁיקְיָינֵיהּ דְאִיתְרוּ בֵּיהּ עִנְבֵי לָא יִשְׁתֵּי וְעִנְבִין רְטִיבִין וּצְמוּקִין לָא יֵיכוֹל


he shall abstain from wine, new and old, he shall drink neither vinegar of old wine or new; neither may he drink liquor in which grapes have been crushed, nor eat of grapes either fresh or dried.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:4)


כָּל יוֹמֵי נִזְרֵיהּ מִכָּל דְמִתְעֲבֵיד מִגוּפְנָא דְחַמְרָא מִגוּפְנִין מִקְלוּפִין וְעַד זַגִין גַיוָואִין דְעִנְבָא לָא יֵיכוֹל


All the days of his vow he shall not eat of the tree which maketh wine, from the husks of grapes even to the kernels within them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:5)


כָּל יוֹמֵי נְדַר נִזְרֵיהּ גְלָב לָא יְעִבַּר עַל רֵישֵׁיהּ עַד זְמַן מִשְׁלַם יוֹמַיָא דְיִפְרֵשׁ לִשְׁמָא דַיְיָ קַדִישׁ יְהֵי מַרְבֶּה פֵּרוּעַ שְעַר רֵישֵׁיהּ


All the days of his nazir-vow the razor shall not pass upon his head until the time when the days of his separation to the Name of the Lord be fulfilled; he shall be consecrate, letting the hair of his head grow.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:6)


כָּל יוֹמִין דְיַפְרֵישׁ לִשְׁמָא דַיְיָ עַל בַּר נַשׁ דְמִית לָא יֵיעִיל


All the days of his separation to the Name of the Lord he shall not go in where there is a dead man.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:7)


לְאָבוּי וּלְאִמֵיהּ לְאָחוֹי וּלְאַחְתֵיהּ לָא יִסְתָּאַב לְהוֹן בְּמוֹתְהוֹן אֲרוּם כְּלִילָא דֶאֱלָהֵיהּ עַל רֵישֵׁיהּ


For his father, or his mother, his brother, or his sister, he shall not make himself unclean through their decease; for the crown of Eloah is upon his head;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:8)


כָּל יוֹמֵי נִזְרֵיהּ קָדוֹשׁ הוּא קֳדָם יְיָ


all the days in which he is a Nazir lie shall be sacred before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:9)


וַאֲרוּם יְמוּת מִיתָא עֲלוֹי בִּתְכּוֹף שְׁלוּ וְיִסְאָב רֵישׁ נִזְרֵיהּ וִיגַלַח רֵישֵׁיהּ בְּיוֹם דְכוּתֵיהּ בְּיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה יְגַלְחִינֵיהּ


But if a person die near him suddenly, and he unawares defile the head of his vow, let him shave his head on the day of his purification; on the seventh day let him shave it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:10)


וּבְיוֹמָא תְמִינָאָה יַיְתֵי תְּרֵין שַׁפְנִינָן אוֹ תְּרֵין גוֹזְלִין בְּנֵי יוֹנָן לְוַת כַּהֲנָא לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא


And on the eighth day let him bring two turtle doves, or two young pigeons, unto the priest at the door of the tabernacle of ordinance.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:11)


וְיַעֲבֵד כַּהֲנָא חַד חַטָאתָא וְחַד לַעֲלָתָא וִיכַפֵּר עֲלוֹי מַאן דְחַב עַל דְאִסְתָּאַב לְמִיתָא וִיקַדֵשׁ יַת רֵישֵׁיהּ בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא


And the priest shall make one a sin offering, and one a burnt offering, and atone for him on account of that in which he hath sinned, in defiling himself by the dead: and he shall consecrate his head on that day.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:12)


וְיַפְרֵשׁ קֳדָם יְיָ יַת יוֹמֵי נִזְרֵיהּ וְיַיְתֵי אִמַר בַּר שַׁתֵּיהּ לְאַשָׁמָא וְיוֹמַיָא קַדְמָאֵי יְבַטְלוּן אֲרוּם אִסְתָּאֵב נִזְרֵיהּ


And let him dedicate before the Lord the days of his nazirate (afresh), and bring a lamb of the year for a trespass offering; but the former days will have been in vain, because he had defiled his nazirate.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:13)


וְדָא אַחֲוָויַת אוֹרַיְתָא נִזְרָא בְּיוֹם מִשְׁלַם יוֹמֵי אַפְרָשׁוּתָא יַמְטֵי יַת גַרְמֵיהּ לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא


And this is the law of the Nazir on the day when his separation days are fulfilled: Let him present himself at the door of the tabernacle of ordinance,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:14)


וְיַקְרִיב יַת קוּרְבָּנֵיהּ קֳדָם יְיָ אִימַר בַּר שַׁתֵּיהּ שְׁלִים חָד לַעֲלָתָא וְאִימַרְתָּא חֲדָא בְּרַת שַׁתָּא שְׁלֵימְתָּא לְחַטָאתָא וּדְכַר חַד שְׁלִים לְנִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָא


and bring his oblation before the Lord, one lamb of the year unblemished for a burnt offering, and one ewe Iamb of the year unblemished for a sin offering, and one ram unblemished for the consecrated oblation;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:15)


וְסַלָא דְפַטִירֵי סְמִידָא גְרִיצָן בִּמְשַׁח זֵיתָא וְעֵרוּכִין פַּטִירִין דִמְשִׁיחִין בִּמְשַׁח זֵיתָא וּמִנְחַתְהוֹן וְנִסְכֵּיהוֹן


and a basket of unleavened cakes of flour with olive oil, and unleavened wafers anointed with olive oil, their minchas and libations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:16)


וִיקָרֵב כַּהֲנָא קֳדָם יְיָ וְיֶעֱבַד יַת חַטָאתֵיהּ וְיַת עֲלָתֵיהּ


And the priest shall offer before the Lord, and perform the sin offering, and the burnt sacrifice;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:17)


וְיַת דִיכְרָא יַעֲבֵד נִכְסַת קוּדְשִׁין קֳדָם יְיָ עַל סַלָא דְפַטִירַיָא וְיֶעֱבַד כַּהֲנָא יַת מִנְחָתֵיהּ וְיַת נִסְכֵּיהּ


and make the ram a consecrated victim (peace offering) before the Lord, with the basket of unleavened; and the priest shall make its mincha and its libation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:18)


וִיגַלַח נְזִירָא יַת רֵישׁ נִזְרֵיהּ לְבָרָא בָּתַר דִנְכִיסוּ יַת נִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָא בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְיִסַב יַת שְעַר רֵישׁ נִזְרֵיהּ וְיִתֵּן עַל אֵשָׁתָא דִתְחוֹת דוּדָא דְנִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָא


And the Nazir shall shave his consecrated head, without, after the offering of the holy oblations at the door of the tabernacle of ordinance, and take the hair of his consecrated head, and lay it on the fire that is under the cauldron of the peace offering.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:19)


וְיִסַב כַּהֲנָא יַת אֶדְרוֹעָא דְמִיבַשְׁלָא שְׁלֵימָא מִן דִיכְרָא וּגְרִיצְתָּא פַּטִירְתָּא חֲדָא מִן סַלָא וְעָרוּךְ פַּטִיר חָד וְיִתֵּן עַל יְדֵי נְזִירָא בָּתַר דְיִגְלַח יַת נִזְרֵיהּ


And the priest shall take the shoulder that is boiled, entire from the ram, and one unleavened cake from the basket, and one unleavened wafer, and put upon the hands of the Nazirite, after he hath shaved his head of the consecration.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:20)


וְיָרִים יַתְהוֹן כַּהֲנָא אֲרָמָא קוּדְשָׁא הוּא לְכַהֲנָא עַל חַדְיָא דַאֲרָמוּתָא וְעַל שׁוֹקָא דְאַפְרָשׁוּתָא וּמִבָּתַר כְּדֵין יִשְׁתֵּי נְזִירָא חַמְרָא


And the priest shall uplift them for an elevation. It is sacred; it belongeth to the priest, with the breast of the elevation, and the separated shoulder. And after that the Nazirite may drink wine.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:21)


דָא אַחְוָיַית אוֹרַיְיתָא נְזִירָא דִי יִדַר קוּרְבָּנֵיהּ קֳדָם יְיָ עַל נִזְרֵיהּ בַּר מִן מַה דְתִדְבַּק יְדֵיהּ כְּמֵסַת נִדְרֵיהּ לְאַיְיתָאָה מִן דִי יִדַר הֵיכְדֵין יַעֲבֵד עַל אוֹרַיְיתָא נִזְרֵיהּ


This is the declaration of the law of the Nazir who shall have vowed his oblation before the Lord for his separation, besides what may come into his hand according to the rule (or measure) of his vow, to bring that which he had vowed; so shall he do according to the law of his nazirate.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:22)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:23)


מַלֵיל עִם אַהֲרן וְעִם בְּנוֹי לְמֵימָר כִּדְנָא תְבָרְכוּן יַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל בְּמִפְרַסְהוֹן יְדֵיהוֹן עַל דוּכְנָא בַּהֲדֵין לִישָׁן יֵימְרוּן לְהוֹן יְבָרֶכְךָ יְיָ וְיִשְׁמְרֶךָ. יָאֵר יְיָ פָּנָיו אֵלֶיךָ וִיחֻנֶךָּ. יִשָא יְיָ פָּנָיו אֵלֶיךָ וְיָשֵם לְךָ שָׁלוֹם


Speak with Aharon and his sons, saying: Thus shall you bless the children of Israel, while spreading forth the hands from the high place; in this tongue.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:24)


יְבָרְכִינָךָ יְיָ בְּכָל עִסְקָךְ וְיִטְרִינָךְ מִן לִילֵי וּמְזַיְיעֵי וּבְנֵי טִיהַרְרֵי וּבְנֵי צַפְרִירֵי וּמַזִיקֵי וּטְלוֹי


The Lord bless thee and keep thee. The Lord make His face to shine upon thee, and gracious unto thee. The Lord lift up His countenance upon thee, and grant thee peace. The Lord bless thee in all thy business, and keep) thee from demonsof the night, and things that cause terror, and from demons of the noon and of the morning, and from malignant spirits and phantoms.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:25)


יַנְהַר יְיָ סְבַר אַפּוֹי לָךְ בְּמֵעַסְקָךְ בְּאוֹרַיְיתָא וְיִגְלֵי לָךָ טְמִירָן וְיֵיחוֹס עֲלָךְ


The Lord make His face to shine upon thee, when occupied in the law, and reveal to thee its secrets, and be merciful unto thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:26)


יַסְבַּר יְיָ סְבַר אַפּוֹי לָךְ בִּצְלוּתָךְ וִישַׁוֵי עֲלָךְ שְׁלַם בְּכָל תְּחוּמָךְ


The Lord lift up His countenance upon thee in thy prayer, and grant thee peace in thy end.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 6:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/6:27)


וִישַׁווּן יַת בִּרְכַּת שְׁמִי עַל בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וַאֲנָא בְּמֵימְרִי אֲבָרְכִינוּן


And they shall bestow the benediction of My Name upon the children of Israel, and I, by My Word, will bless them.



## Chapter 7

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:1)


וַהֲוָה בְּיוֹם רֵישׁ יַרְחָא דְנִיסָן פְּסַק משֶׁה לְמִקְמָא יַת מַשְׁכְּנָא דְלָא פַּרְקֵיהּ תּוּב וְרַבֵּי יָתֵיהּ וְקַדִישׁ יָתֵיהּ וְיַת כָּל מָנוֹי וְיַת מַדְבְּחָא וְיַת כָּל מָנוֹי וְרַבִּינוּן וְקַדִישׁ יַתְהוֹן


And it was on the day which begins the month of Nisan, when Mosheh had finished to uprear the tabernacle, he took it not in pieces again, but anointed and consecrated it and all its vessels, the altar and all the vessels thereof, and he anointed them and hallowed them;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:2)


וְקָרִיבוּ אֲמַרְכְּלַיָא דְיִשְרָאֵל רֵישֵׁי בֵּית אַבְהַתְהוֹן הִינוּן רַבְרְבֵי שִׁבְטַיָא הִינוּן דְמִתְמַנָן בְּמִצְרַיִם אֲמַרְכּוֹלִין עַל מִנְיָנַיָא


then the leaders of Israel, who were the chiefs of the house of their fathers, brought their offerings. These were they who had been appointed in Mizraim chiefs over the numbered,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:3)


וְאַיְתִיוּ יַת קָרְבַּנְהוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ שִׁית עֶגְלָן כַּד מְחַפָּן וּמְטַקְסָן וּתְרֵיסַר תּוֹרִין עֶגְלָתָא בֵּינֵי תְרֵין אֲמַרְכְּלִין וְתוֹרָא לְחַד וְלָא צְבָא משֶׁה לְמִסַב מִנְהוֹן וּקְרִיבוּ יַתְהוֹן קֳדָם מַשְׁכְּנָא


and they brought their offering before the Lord; six wagons covered and fitted up, and twelve oxen; one wagon for two princes and one ox for each. But Mosheh was not willing to receive them, and they brought them before the tabernacle.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:4)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:5)


קַבֵּל מִנְהוֹן וִיהוֹן צִיבִי לִצְרוֹךְ סִדוּרָא וְתוֹרִין וְעִיגְלִין יְהוֹן לְמִפְלַח יַת פּוּלְחַן מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְתִתֵּן יַתְהוֹן לְלֵיוָאֵי גְבַר כְּמֵיסַת פּוּלְחָנֵיהּ


Take them, and let them be used for the need of the appointed (work), and let the oxen and the wagons be for the work of the service of the tabernacle of ordinance, and give them to the Levites, to each according to the measure of his work.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:6)


וּנְסֵב משֶׁה יַת עֶגְלָתָא וְיַת תּוֹרֵי וִיהַב לְלֵיוָאֵי


And Mosheh took the wagons and the oxen, and gave them to the Levites.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:7)


יַת תַּרְתֵּין עֶגְלָתָא וְיַת אַרְבַּעַת תּוֹרֵי יְהַב לִבְנֵי גֵרְשׁוֹן כְּמֵיסַת פּוּלְחַנְהוֹן


Two wagons and four oxen he gave to the sons of Gershon, according to the amount of their service,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:8)


וְיַת אַרְבַּע עֶגְלָן וְיַת תַּמְנָן תּוֹרֵי יְהַב לִבְנֵי מְרָרִי כְּמֵיסַת פּוּלְחַנְהוֹן בִּידָא דְאִיתָמָר בַּר אַהֲרן כַּהֲנָא


and four wagons and eight oxen gave be to the sons of Merari, according to the measure of their service, by the hand of Ithamar bar Aharon the priest.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:9)


וְלִבְנֵי קְהָת לָא יְהַב עֶגְלָן וְתוֹרִין אֲרוּם פּוּלְחַן קוּדְשָׁא רַמְיָא עֲלֵיהוֹן בְּכַתְפָא נַטְלִין


But to the sons of Kehath he gave neither wagons nor oxen, because on them was laid the service of the sanctuary, to be carried on their shoulders.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:10)


וּקְרִיבוּ רַבְרְבַיָא יַת חֲנוּכַּת רְבוּתָא דְמַדְבְּחָא בְּיוֹמָא דְרַבֵּי יָתֵיהּ וּקְרִיבוּ רַבְרְבַיָא יַת קָרְבַּנְהוֹן לִקְדָם מַדְבְּחָא


And the princes offered at the dedication of the altar by anointing, on the day that he anointed it did the princes present their oblations before the altar.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:11)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה אֲמַרְכּוֹל חָד לְיוֹמָא אֲמַרְכּוֹל חָד לְיוֹמָא יִקְרְבוּן יַת קוּרְבַּנְהוֹן לַחֲנוּכַּת רְבוּתָא דְמַדְבְּחָא


And the Lord said unto Mosheh, Let the princes offer each, one prince on one day, their oblations at the dedication of the altar by anointing.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:12)


וַהֲוָה דִמְקָרֵב בְּיוֹמָא קַמָאָה יַת קוּרְבָּנֵיהּ נַחְשׁוֹן בַּר עַמִינָדָב רַב בֵּית אַבָּא לְשִׁבְטָא דִיהוּדָה


He who on the first day presented his oblation was Nachshon bar Amminadab, prince of the house of the fathers of the tribe Jehudah:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:13)


וְקוּרְבָּנֵיהּ דְמִקְרַב פְּיוֹלֵי דִכְסַף חֲדָא גִילְדָא סְמִיךְ מְאָה וּתְלָתִין סִלְעִין בְּסִלְעֵי בֵּית קוּדְשָׁא הֲוַת מַתְקְלָא מַזִירְקָא חָד דִכְסַף דְגִילְדָא קַלִישָׁא שׁוּבְעִין סִלְעִין בְּסִלְעֵי בֵּית קוּדְשָׁא תְּרֵין מָאנַיָא הָאִילֵין קְרֵיב יַתְהוֹן מַלְיָן סְמִידָא מִן אַפְרָשׁוּתָא פְּתִיכָא בִּמְשַׁח זֵיתָא לְמִנְחָתָא


and his oblation which he offered was one silver bowl, thickly embossed, (or, crusted,) in weight one hundred and thirty shekels, in shekels of the sanctuary; one silver vase, slightly embossed, of seventy shekels, in shekels of the sanctuary; both of these vessels he brought filled with flour of the separation, sprinkled with olive oil for a mincha;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 7:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:14)


בָּזִיכָא חֲדָא מַתְקְלָא עֲשַר סִלְעִין דִכְסַף וְהִיא הֲוַת דִדְהַב טָב קָרִיב יָתָהּ מַלְיָא קְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמָנִין טָבִין מִן אַפְרָשׁוּתָא

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:15)


תּוֹר חָד בַּר תּוֹרִין בַּר תְּלַת שְׁנִין דְכַר חָד בַּר תַּרְתֵּין שְׁנִין וְאִימַר חָד בַּר שַׁתֵּיהּ תְּלָתֵיהוֹן קְרַב רַב שִׁבְטָא דִיהוּדָה לַעֲלָתָא


one young bullock of three years, one ram of two years, and one lamb of the year. These three did the chief of the tribe Jehudah bring for a burnt offering;

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:16)

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:17)


וּלְנִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָא תּוֹרִין תְּרֵין דִכְרִין חַמְשָׁא בַּרְחֵי חַמְשָׁא אִמְרִין בְּנֵי שְׁנָה חַמְשָׁא דֵין סֵדֶר קוּרְבָּנָא דִי יְקָרֵיב מִנִכְסוֹי נַחְשׁוֹן בַּר עַמִינָדָב


and for consecrated victims, two oxen, five rams, five goats, lambs of the year five: this is the order of the oblation which Nachshon bar Amminadab offered of his wealth.

מְאָה וּתְלָתִין סִלְעִין הֲוֵי מַתְקְלָא דְפַיְילָתָא חֲדָא דְכַסְפָּא כָּל קֳבֵיל שְׁנִין דַהֲוָת יוֹכֶבֶד כַּד יְלִידַת יַת משֶׁה וְשׁוּבְעִין סִלְעִין הֲוֵי מַתְקְלָא דְמַזִירְקָא חַד כָּל קֳבֵיל שׁוּבְעִין סָבֵי סַנְהֶדְרִין רַבָּא כָּל כְּסַף מָאנַיָא תְּרֵין אַלְפִין וְאַרְבַּע מְאָה סִלְעִין בְּסִלְעֵי בֵּית קוּדְשָׁא


One hundred and thirty shekels was the weight of each silver bowl, answering to the years of Jokebed when she bare Mosheh; and seventy shekels was the weight of each vase, answering to the seventy elders of the great Sanhedrin: all the silver vessels, two thousand four hundred shekels, in shekels of the sanctuary.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 7:86

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:86](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:86)


בָּזִיכֵי דְדַהֲבָא תְּרֵי סְרֵי כָּל קֳבֵיל רַבְרְבֵי יִשְרָאֵל מַלְיָן קְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמִין טָבִין מַתְקַל עֲשַר סִלְעִין הֲוָה מַתְקְלָא דְבָזִיכָא בְּסִלְעֵי בֵּית קוּדְשָׁא כָּל קֳבֵיל עֲשֵירְתָּא דִבְרַיָא כָּל דְהַב בָּזִיכַיָא מְאָה וְעֶשְרִין כָּל קֳבֵיל שְׁנַיָא דְחַיָא בְּהוֹן משֶׁה נְבִיאָה


The golden pans were twelve, answering to the princes of Israel, full of good sweet incense; the weight of ten shekels was the weight of each pan, answering to the Ten Words; all the gold of the pans, one hundred and and twenty (shekels), answering to the years lived by Mosheh the prophet.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 7:87

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:87](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:87)


כָּל תּוֹרֵי לַעֲלָתָא תְּרֵיסַר תּוֹרֵי לְרַב בֵּית אַבָּא דִיכְרָא דִיכְרִין תְּרֵיסַר מְטוּל דִיהוֹבְדוּן תְּרֵיסַר רַבְרְבֵי יִשְׁמָעֵאל אִמְרִין בְּנֵי שָׁנָה תְּרֵיסַר מְטוּל דִיהוֹבְדוּן תְּרֵיסַר רַבְרְבֵי פָּרַס וּמִנְחַתְהוֹן מְטוּל דְיַעֲדֵי כַפְנָא מִן עַלְמָא וּצְפִירֵי עִזִין תְּרֵיסַר לְחַטָאתָא מְטוּל לְכַפָּרָא עַל חוֹבֵי תְּרֵיסַר שִׁבְטַיָא


All the bullocks for the burnt offering, twelve, a bullock for a prince of the house of the fathers; twelve rams, because the twelve princes of Ishmael would perish; twelve lambs of the year, because the twelve princes of Persia would perish; and their minchas, that famine might be removed from the world; and twelve kids of the goats for the sin offering, to atone for the sins of the twelve tribes.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 7:88

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:88](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:88)


וְכָל תּוֹרֵי לְנִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָא עֶשְרִין וְאַרְבַּע תוֹרִין כָּל קֳבֵיל עֶשְרִין וְאַרְבַּע מַטַרְתָּא דִכְרֵי שִׁתִּין כָּל קֳבֵיל שִׁתִּין שְׁנִין דַהֲוָה יִצְחָק כַּד יְלִיד יַת יַעֲקב בָּרְחֵי שִׁתִּין כָּל קֳבֵיל שִׁתִּין אַפִּין דְבִרְכַת כַּהֲנַיָא אִמְרִין בְּנֵי שָׁנָה שִׁתִּין לְכַפָּרָא עַל שִׁתִּין רִיבְוָן דְיִשְרָאֵל דָא חֲנֻכַּת רְבוּת מַדְבְּחָא בְּיוֹמָא דְרַבִּיוּ יָתֵיהּ


And all the oxen for consecrated victims, twenty-four, answering to the twenty-four orders (of the priests); the rams, sixty, answering, to the sixty years which Izhak had lived when he begat Jakob; the goats, sixty, answering to the sixty letters in the benediction of the priests; lambs of the year, sixty, to atone for the sixty myriads of Israel. This was the dedication of the altar by anointment on the day that they anointed it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 7:89

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:89](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/7:89)


וְכַד עָלֵיל משֶׁה לְמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לְמַלָלָא עִמֵיהּ וּשְׁמַע יַת קַל רוּחָא דְמִתְמַלֵיל עִמֵיהּ כַּד נָחִית מִן שְׁמֵי שְׁמַיָא עִילַוֵי כַפּוּרְתָּא דְעַל אֲרוֹנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא מִבֵּין תְּרֵין כְּרוּבַיָא וּמִתַּמָן הֲוָה דִבּוּרָא מִתְמַלֵל עִמֵיהּ


And when Mosheh entered into the tabernacle of ordinance to speak with Him, he heard the voice of the Spirit who spake with him descending from the heaven of heavens upon the Mercy Seat which was upon the Ark of the Testimony between the two Cherubim, and from thence was the Oracle {Word; Dibbera} speaking with him.



## Chapter 8

And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:2)


מַלֵיל עִם אַהֲרן וְתֵימַר לֵיהּ בִּזְמַן אַדְלָקוּתָךְ יַת בּוֹצִינַיָא כָּל קֳבֵיל אַפֵּי מְנַרְתָּא יְהוֹן מְנַהֲרִין שִׁבְעָתֵי בּוֹצִינַיָא תְּלַת לִקֳבֵיל רוּחַ מַעַרְבָא וּתְלַת לִקֳבֵל רוּחַ מַדִינְחָא וּשְׁבִיעָא בִּמְצִיעָאָה


Speak with Aharon, and say to him: At the time when thou dost kindle the lamps upon the candelabrum, (all) the seven lamps shall be alight; three on the western side, and three on the eastern side, and the seventh in the midst.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:3)


וַעֲבַד כֵּן אַהֲרן כָּל קֳבֵיל אַפֵּי מְנַרְתָּא אַדְלֵיק בּוֹצִינָהָא הֵיכְמָא דְפַקֵיד יְיָ יַת משֶׁה


And Aharon did so; at the face of the candelabrum he lit the lamps thereof, as the Lord commanded Mosheh.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:4)


וְדֵין עוֹבַד מְנַרְתָּא מִינָא קַשְׁיָא דִדְהַב עַד בָּסִיס דִידָהּ וְעַד שׁוֹשַׁנְיָיהָא עוֹבַד אוּמַן בְּקוּרְנְסָא הִיא מִתְעַבְדָא הֵי כְחֵיזוּ דְאַחֲזֵי יְיָ יַת משֶׁה הֵיכְדֵין עָבַד בְּצַלְאֵל יַת מְנַרְתָּא


And this was the work of the candelabrum, which was of beaten gold, from its foundations unto its lilies, the work of the artificer, with the hammer was it wrought: according to the vision which the Lord had showed Mosheh, so did Bezalel make the candelabrum.

קָרֵיב יַת לֵיוָאֵי מִגוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וּתְדַכֵּי יַתְהוֹן


Bring the Levites out from among the sons of Israel, and purify them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:7)


וּכְדֵין תַּעֲבֵיד לְהוֹן לְדַכּוֹאֵיהוֹן אֲדֵי עֲלֵיהוֹן מַיָא דְחַטָאתָא וְיַעַבְרוּן גְלַב עַל כָּל בִּשְרֵיהוֹן וִיחַוְורוּן לְבוּשֵׁיהוֹן וְיִדְכּוּן בְּאַרְבְּעִין סָוִוין דְמַיָא


And this shalt thou do to purify them. Sprinkle upon them the water for uncleanness through sin (chattatha), and let the razor pass over all their flesh, and let them wash their raiment, and wash themselves in forty savan of water.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:8)


וְיִסְבּוּן תּוֹר בַּר תּוֹרֵי וּמִנְחָתֵיהּ סוּלְתָּא פְּתִיכָא בִּמְשַׁח זֵיתָא וְתוֹר תִּנְיַין בַּר תּוֹרֵי תִּיסַב לְחַטָאתָא


And they shall take a young bullock, and his mincha of flour sprinkled with olive oil; and take thou a second young bullock for a sin offering.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 8:9

וְתַקְרֵיב יַת לֵיוָאֵי קֳדָם מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְתִכְנוֹשׁ יַת כָּל כְּנִישְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


And thou shalt bring the Levites before the tabernacle of ordinance, and gather together also all the congregation of the sons of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 8:10

Thou shalt bring the Levites before the Lord, and the sons of Israel shall lay their hands upon the Levites.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 8:11

And Aharon shall present the Levites, (as) an elevation before the Lord from the sons of Israel, and they shall be for the work of the service of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 8:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:12)


וְלֵיוָאֵי יִסְמְכוּן יַת יְדֵיהוֹן עַל רֵישׁ תּוֹרֵי וְעִיבַד יַת חַד חַטָאתָא וְיַת חַד עֲלָתָא קֳדָם יְיָ לְכַפָּרָא עַל לֵיוָאֵי


And the Levites shall lay their hands upon the head of the bullocks, and make one a sin offering and one a burnt offering before the Lord, to atone for the Levites.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:13)

And thou shalt place the Levites before Aharon and his sons, and present them (as) an elevation before the Lord;

וְתַפְרֵישׁ יַת לֵיוָאֵי מִגוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וִיהוֹן מְשַׁמְשִׁין קֳדָמַי לֵיוָאֵי


and thus shalt thou separate the Levites from among the sons of Israel, that the Levites may be ministers before Me.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:15)

And afterward the Levites may enter to fulfil the service of the tabernacle of ordinance, when thou shalt have purified them and presented them (as) an elevation;

for separated they are separate before Me from among the sons of Israel, instead of every one who openeth the womb; the first-born of all who are of the sons of Israel have I taken (to be) before Me.

For every first-born of the sons of Israel is Mine, whether of man or of beast: in the day that I slew all the first-born in the land of Mizraim, I sanctified them before Me;
