and I have taken the Levites instead of all the first-born of the sons of Israel,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 8:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:19)


וִיהָבִית יַת לֵיוָאֵי יְהִיבִין לְאַהֲרן וְלִבְנוֹי מִגוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְמִפְלַח יַת פּוּלְחַן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל בְּמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וּלְכַפָּרָא עַל בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְלָא יְהֵי בִּבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מוֹתָא בִּזְמַן מִקְרַב בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְקוּדְשָׁא


and have given the Levites (as) gifts unto Aharon and to his sons from among the sons of Israel, to minister the service of the children of Israel in the tabernacle of ordinance, and to atone for the children of Israel, lest there be mortality among the children of Israel at the time when they approach the sanctuary.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 8:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:20)


וַעֲבַד משֶׁה וְאַהֲרן וְכָל כְּנִישְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְלֵיוָאֵי הֵיכְמָא דְפַקֵיד יְיָ יַת משֶׁה עַל לֵיוָאֵי הֵיכְדֵין עָבָדוּ לְהוֹן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


And Mosheh and Aharon and all the congregation of the Beni Israel did unto the Levites according to all that the Lord had commanded Mosheh concerning the Levites, so did the sons of Israel to them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 8:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:21)


וְאִדְכִּיאוּ לֵיוָאֵי וְחַוָרוּ לְבוּשֵׁיהוֹן וַאֲרֵים אַהֲרן יַתְהוֹן אֲרָמָא קֳדָם יְיָ וְכַפֵּר עֲלֵיהוֹן אַהֲרן לְדַכּוֹאֵיהוֹן


And the Levites were purified, and they washed their raiment; and Aharon presented them as an elevation before the Lord. And Aharon made atonement for them to purify them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 8:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:22)


וּמִבָּתַר כְּדֵין אָעֲלוּ לֵיוָאֵי לְמִפְלַח יַת פּוּלְחַנְהוֹן בְּמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לִקֳדָם אַהֲרן וְלִקֳדָם בְּנוֹי הֵיכְמָא דְפַקֵיד יְיָ יַת משֶׁה עַל לֵיוָאֵי הֵיכְדֵין עָבָדוּ לְהוֹן


And afterward the Levites went in to fulfil their ministry in the tabernacle of ordinance, before Aharon and his sons: as the Lord had commanded Mosheh concerning the Levites, so did they unto them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 8:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:23)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 8:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:24)


דָא אַחְוַויְיתָא דִי לְלֵיוָאֵי דְלָא מוּפְסָלִין בְּמוּמְהוֹן בְּרַם מִבַּר עֶשְרִין וְחָמֵשׁ שְׁנִין וּלְעֵילָא יֵיתֵי לְחַיְילָא חֵילָא בְּפוּלְחַן מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא


This is the instruction for the Levites who are not disqualified (profaned) by their blemishes: from one of twenty-five years and upward, he shall come, according to his company, to the service of the tabernacle of ordinance


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 8:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:25)


וּמִבַּר חַמְשִׁין שְׁנִין יֵיתוּב מֵחֵיל פּוּלְחָנָא וְלָא יִפְלַח תּוּב


and from fifty years of age he shall return from the band of the service, and serve no more.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 8:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/8:26)


וִישַׁמֵשׁ עִם אָחוֹי בְּמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לְמִטַר מַטְרָא וּפוּלְחָנָא לָא יִפְלַח הֵיכְדֵין תַּעֲבֵיד לְלֵיוָאֵי בְּמַטְרַתְהוֹן


Yet he may minister with his brethren at the tabernacle of ordinance in keeping the watch; but he shall not do any of the service. So shall the Levites act in their charge.



## Chapter 9

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:1)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְסִינַי בְּשַׁתָּא תִּנְיֵיתָא לִזְמַן מִפַּקְהוֹן מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם בְּיַרְחָא קַדְמָאָה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh in the wilderness of Sinai, in the second year from the time of their going forth from the land of Mizraim, in the first month, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:2)


וְיַעַבְדוּן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל יַת נִכְסַת פִּסְחָא בֵּינֵי שִׁמְשְׁתָא בִּזְמַנֵיהּ


Let the children of Israel perform the sacrifice of the Pascha between the suns at its time.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:3)


בְּאַרְבֵּיסַר יוֹמָא בְּיַרְחָא הָדֵין בֵּין שִׁמְשְׁתָא תַּעַבְדוּן יָתֵיהּ בִּזְמַנֵיהּ כְּכָל קְיָימוֹי וּכְכָל דִינוֹי תַּעַבְדוּן יָתֵיהּ


On the fourteenth day of this mouth, between the suns, they shall perform it in its time; according to all its rites and all its statutes shall they do it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:4)


וּמַלֵיל משֶׁה עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְמֶעֱבַּד יַת נִכְסַת פִּסְחָא


And Mosheh spake with the children of Israel to perform the sacrifice of the Pascha.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:5)


וַעֲבָדוּ יַת פִּסְחָא בְּאַרְבֵּיסַר יוֹמִין לְיַרְחָא בֵּינֵי שִׁמְשְׁתָא בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְסִינַי כְּכָל מַה דִי פַקֵיד יְיָ יַת משֶׁה הֵיכְדֵין עָבָדוּ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


They performed the Pascha, therefore, on the fourteenth day of the month, between the suns in the wilderness of Sinai; after all that the Lord had commanded Mosheh, so did the children of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:6)


וַהֲווֹ גוּבְרַיָא דְהַווּ מְסָאֲבִין לִטְמֵי נְפַשׁ בַּר נַשׁ דְמִית עֲלֵיהוֹן בִּתְכֵּף דְפוּקְדָנֵיהּ רַמְיָא עֲלֵיהוֹן וְלָא יְכִילוּ לְמֶעֱבַד פִּסְחָא בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא דְהַוָה יוֹם שְׁבִיעָאָה לְסוֹאֲבוּתְהוֹן וּקְרִיבוּ לִקֳדָם משֶׁה וְקָדָם אַהֲרן בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא


But certain men, who were unclean, having been defiled by the body of a man who had died near them suddenly; as the commandment (of the Pascha) came upon them, could not perform it on that day, which was the seventh of their uncleanness. And they came before Mosheh and Aharon on that day;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:7)


וְאָמָרוּ גוּבְרַיָא הָאִינוּן לֵיהּ אֲנַחְנָא אִסְתְּאַבְנָא לְבַר נַשׁ דְמִית עֲלָנָא לְמָה כְעַן נִתְמְנַע בְּגִין דְלָא לְמֵיכוֹס פִּסְחָא וּלְמִזְרוֹק אַדְמָא דְקוּרְבָּנָא דַיְיָ עַל מַדְבְּחָא בִּזְמַנֵיהּ וּבִשְרֵיהּ יֵיכְלוּן דַכְיָין בְּגוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


and these men said to him, We are unclean, on account of a man who died with us: therefore we are hindered from killing the Pascha, and shedding the blood of the Lord's oblation upon the altar at its time, that we may eat its flesh, being clean, among the children of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:8)


דֵין הוּא חַד מֵאַרְבָּעָה דִינִין דְעָלוּ קֳדָם משֶׁה נְבִיָא וְדָן יַתְהוֹן עַל מֵימְרָא דְקוּדְשָׁא בִּקְצַת מִנְהוֹן הֲוָה משֶׁה מָתִין מִן בִּגְלַל דַהֲווֹ דִינֵי נַפְשָׁתָא וּבִקְצַתְהוֹן הֲוָה משֶׁה זָרִיז מִן בִּגְלַל דַהֲווֹ דִינֵי מָמוֹנָא בְּאִלֵין וּבְאִלֵין אָמַר משֶׁה לָא שַׁמְעִית מִן בִּגְלַל לְמַלְפָא לְרֵישֵׁי סַנְהֶדְרַיְתָא דַעֲתִידִין לְמֵקוּם מִן בַּתְרֵיהּ דִיהוֹן מְתוּנִין בְּדִינֵי נַפְשָׁתָא וּזְרִיזִין בְּדִינֵי מָמוֹנָא וְלָא יְבַהֲתוּן לִמְשַׁיְילֵי דִינָא דְמִתְקַשֵׁי לְהוֹן אֲרוּם משֶׁה דְהַוָה רַבְּהוֹן דְיִשְרָאֵל צָרִיךְ דְיֵימַר לָא שְׁמָעִית בְּגִין כֵּן אָמַר לְהוֹן משֶׁה אוֹרִיכוּ עַד דְאֶשְׁמַע מַה דְאִתְפְּקִיד מִן קֳדָם יְיָ עַל דִלְכוֹן


This is one of four matters of judgment brought before Mosheh the prophet, which he decided according to the Word of the Holy One: in some of which Mosheh was deliberate, because they were judgments about life; but in the others Mosheh was prompt, they being (only) judgments concerning money: but in those (the former) Mosheh said, I have not heard; that he might teach the princes of the Sanhedrin who should arise after him to be deliberate in judgements regarding life, but prompt in judgments about money; and not to be ashamed to ask counsel in things too hard for them, inasmuch as Mosheh himself, the Rabbi of Israel, had need to say, I have not yet heard. Therefore, said Mosheh to them, Wait until I have heard what will be commanded from before the Lord concerning your case.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:9)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:10)


מַלֵיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְמֵימָר גְבַר טְלֵי אוֹ גְבַר סִיב אֲרוּם יְהֵי מְסָאָב לִטְמֵי בַּר נַשׁ דְמִית אוֹ דַיֵב אוֹ סְגִיר דִמְרָחֵק בְּאוֹרַח עַלְמָא בְּקִרְיוּת לֵילְיָא וַהֲוָה בַּר מִן סְקוּף מַשְׁכְּנָא לְכוֹן גַרְמֵיכוֹן אוֹ לְדָרֵיכוֹן וְיִדְחֵי לְמֶעֱבַד פִּסְחָא קֳדָם יְיָ


Speak with the sons of Israel, saying: A man, whether young or old, when unclean by defilement from the dead, or an issue, or the leprosy, or who is hindered in the way of the world by the accidents of the night, or who shall be at a distance from the threshold of his house: if such things happen to you, or to your generations, then may he defer to perform the Pascha before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 9:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:11)


בְּיַרְחָא תִנְיָינָא הוּא יַרְחָא דְאִיָיר בְּאַרְבֵּיסַר יוֹמָא בֵּינֵי שִׁמְשָׁתָא יַעַבְדוּן יָתֵיהּ עַל פַּטִיר וּמְרָרִין יֵיכְלוּנֵיהּ


But in the second month, which is the month of Ijar, on the fourteenth day of the month, between the suns they shall perform it; with unleavened bread and with bitters they shall eat it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 9:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:12)


לָא יְשַׁיְירוּן מִנֵיהּ עַד צַפְרָא וְגַרְמָא לָא יִתְבְּרוּן בֵּיהּ כְּכָל אַחְוָיַית גְזֵירַת פִּסְחָא דְנִיסָן יַעַבְדוּן יָתֵיהּ בְּרַם בְּפִסְחָא דְנִיסָן הִינוּן יֵיכְלוּן פַּטִירֵי וְקוּרְבַּן פִּסְחָא לָא יַעַבְדוּן מְטוֹל דְסוֹאָבוּתְהוֹן בְּהוֹן וּבְפִסְחָא דְאִיָיר יִידְכּוּן וְיִקְרְבוּן יָתֵיהּ


They shall not leave of it till the morning, and a bone in it shall not be broken; according to every instruction in the decree of the Pascha in Nisan, they shall perform it. In the Pascha of Nisan (such persons) may eat unleavened bread, but not perform the oblation of the Pascha on account of their defilement; but in the Pascha of Ijar being purified they shall offer it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 9:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:13)


וְגַבְרָא דְהוּא דְכֵי וּבְאוֹרַח עַלְמָא לָא אִסְתָּאֵב וּמִבָּרָא לְסִיקוּף מַשְׁכְּנֵיהּ לָא הֲוָה וּפְסַק מִלְמֶעֱבַד קוּרְבַּן פִּסְחָא דְנִיסָן וְיִשְׁתֵּיצֵי בַּר נְשָׁא הַהוּא מֵעַמֵיהּ אֲרוּם קוּרְבָּנָא דַיְיָ לָא קְרֵיב בְּזִמְנֵיהּ חוֹבֵיהּ יְקַבֵּיל גַבְרָא הַהוּא


But the man who, being clean and undefiled by the way of the world, and not at a distance from the threshold of his home, neglecteth to perform the oblation of the Pascha of Nisan, that man shall be cut off from his people, because he hath not offered the Lord's oblation in its season; that man shall bear his sin.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 9:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:14)


וַאֲרוּם אִין יִתְגַיֵיר עִמְכוֹן גִיוֹרָא וְיַעֲבֵיד פִּסְחָא קֳדָם יְיָ כְּאַחְוָיַית גְזֵירַת פִּסְחָא וְכִדְחָזֵי לֵיהּ הֵיכְדֵין יַעֲבֵיד קְיָמָא חֲדָא יְהֵי לְכוֹן וּלְדִיוֹרָא וּלְיַצִבָא דְאַרְעָא


And if the stranger who is sojourning with you will perform the Pascha before the Lord, he shall do it after the proper manner of the Paschal decree, according to its form so shall he do it. You shall have one statute, both for the sojourner and for the native of the land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 9:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:15)


וּבְיוֹמָא דְאִתְקַם יַת מַשְׁכְּנָא חָפָא עֲנַן יְקָרָא יַת מַשְׁכְּנָא וַהֲוָה מְטַלֵל בִּימָמָא לְמַשְׁכְּנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא וּבְרַמְשָׁא הֲוֵי עַל מַשְׁכְּנָא הֵי כְּחֵיזוּ אֵישָׁא עַד צַפְרָא


And on the day on which the tabernacle was reared the Cloud of Glory covered the Tabernacle; it overspread the Tabernacle of Testimony by day, and at evening, it was over the Tabernacle like a vision of Fire until the morning.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 9:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:16)


כְּדֵין הֲוֵי תְדִירָא עֲנַן יְקָרָא חָפֵי לֵיהּ בִּימָמָא וְחֵיזוּ אֵשָׁתָא בְּלֵילְיָא


So was it continually, a Cloud of Glory covering it by day, and a vision of Fire by night.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 9:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:17)


וּלְפוּם אִישׁוֹן אִסְתַּלְקוּת עֲנָנֵי יְקָרָא מֵעִלַוֵי מַשְׁכְּנָא וּבָתַר כֵּן נַטְלִין בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וּבְאַתְרֵי דְשָׁרֵי תַמָן עֲנָנָא תַמָן שָׁרָן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


And what time the Cloud of Glory was uplifted from the Tabernacle, then the children of Israel went forward; and at the place where the Cloud rested, there did the children of Israel rest.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 9:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:18)


עַל פּוּם מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ נַטְלִין בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְעַל פּוּם מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ שָׁרָן כָּל יוֹמִין דְשָׁרֵי עֲנַן יְקָרָא עַל מַשְׁכְּנָא בְּרַם הִינוּן שָׁרָן


By the mouth of the Word of the Lord the children of Israel went forward, and by the Word of the Lord they rested. All the days that the Cloud of Glory abode upon the Tabernacle, (so long) did they abide.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 9:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:19)


וּבְאוֹרְכוּת עֲנָנָא עַל מַשְׁכְּנָא יוֹמִין סַגִיאִין וְיִנְטְרוּן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל יַת מַטְרַת מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ וְלָא נַטְלִין


And if the Cloud tarried over the Tabernacle many days the children of Israel observed the watch of the Word of the Lord, and did not proceed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 9:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:20)


וְאִית זְמַן דְהַוֵי עֲנַן יְקָרָא יוֹמִין דְמִנְיַן הִינוּן שִׁבְעָתֵי יוֹמִין שִׁבְעָתָא עַל מַשְׁכְּנָא עַל פּוּם מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ שָׁרָן וְעַל פּוּם מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ נַטְלִין


If for the time of a number of days, suppose the seven days of the week, the Cloud of Glory was upon the Tabernacle, by the mouth of the Word of the Lord they rested, and by the mouth of the Word of the Lord they went forward.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 9:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:21)


וְאִית זְמַן דְהַוֵי עֲנַן יְקָרָא מִן רַמְשָׁא וְעַד צַפְרָא וּמִסְתַּלֵק עֲנַן יְקָרָא בְּצַפְרָא וְנַטְלִין אוֹ יֵימָם וְלֵילֵי וּמִסְתַּלֵק עֲנָנָא וְנַטְלִין


Or, if the Cloud of Glory (rested only) from evening until morning, and was uplifted in the morning, then went they onward; whether by day or by night, when the Cloud was lifted up they went forward;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 9:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:22)


אוֹ תְּרֵין יוֹמִין אוֹ יַרְחָא אוֹ שַׁתָּא שְׁלֵימְתָּא בְּאוֹרְכוּת עֲנַן יְקָרָא עַל מַשְׁכְּנָא לְמֵישְׁרֵי עֲלוֹי שָׁרָן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְלָא נַטְלִין וּבִזְמַן אִסְתַּלָקוּתֵיהּ נַטְלִין


whether it was two days, or a month, or a year complete, while the Cloud of Glory made stay over the Tabernacle, abiding on it, the children of Israel abode, and journeyed not, and at the time of its uplifting they went forward.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 9:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/9:23)


עַל פּוּם מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ שָׁרָן וְעַל פּוּם מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ נַטְלִין יַת מַטְרַת מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ נַטְרִין עַל פּוּם מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ בִּידָא דְמשֶׁה


By the mouth of the Word of the Lord they encamped, and by it they journeyed; they kept the observance of the Word of the Lord, by the mouth of the Word of the Lord through Mosheh.



## Chapter 10

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:1)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:2)


עִיבַד לָךְ מִדִילָךְ תַּרְתֵּין חֲצוֹצְרַן דִכְסַף מִמִינָא קַשְׁיָא עוֹבַד אוּמַן תַּעֲבֵד יַתְהוֹן וִיהוֹן לָךְ לְעַרְעָא כְנִשְׁתָּא וּלְאַטְלָא יַת מַשִׁרְיָתָא


Make for thee, of thine, two trumpets of silver of solid material, the work of the artificer shalt thou make them; and let them be thine, with which to convoke the assembly, and for the removing of the camps.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:3)


וְיִתְקְעוּן בְּהוֹן וְיִזְדַמְנוּן לְוָתָךְ כָּל כְּנִישְׁתָּא לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא


And thou shalt blow upon them, and bring together to thee all the congregation at the door of the tabernacle of ordinance.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:4)


וְאִין בְּחָדָא יִתְקְעוּן וְיִזְדַמְנוּן לְוָתָךְ רַבְרְבַיָא רֵישֵׁי אַלְפַיָא דְיִשְרָאֵל


If they blow upon one (only), the princes of the heads of the thousands of Israel shall assemble to thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:5)


וְתִתְקְעוּן יַבֶּבְתָּא וְיִטְלוּן מַשִׁרְיָתָא דְשָׁרַן קִידוּמָא


But when you blow an alarm, then the camps which are on the east are to go forward;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:6)


וְתִתְקְעוּן יַבֶּבְתָּא תִּנְיָינוּת וְיִטְלוּן מַשִׁרְיָתָא דְשָׁרַן דָרוֹמְתָּא יַבֶּבְתָּא יִתְקְעוּן לְמַטְלָנֵיהוֹן


and when they blow a second alarm, the camps on the south shall go forward; they shall blow the alarm for their journeys.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:7)


וּבִזְמַן מַכְנַשׁ יַת קְהָלָא תִּתְקְעוּן וְלָא תְיַבְּבוּן


And at the time of assembling the congregation you shall blow, but not an alarm.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:8)


וּבְנֵי אַהֲרן כַּהֲנַיָא שְׁלֵימַיָא יִתְקְעוּן בַּחֲצוֹצְרָתָא וִיהוֹן לְכוֹן לִקְיַם עֲלָם לְדָרֵיכוֹן


The sons of Aharon, the priests only, shall blow with the trumpets, which shall be to you, for a perpetual statute for your generations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 10:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:9)


וַאֲרוּם תְּעוּלוּן לִמְסַדְרָא סִדְרֵי קְרָבָא בְּאַרְעֲכוֹן עַל מְעִיקֵי דִמְעִיקִין לְכוֹן וּתְיַבְּבוּן בַּחֲצוֹצְרָתָא וְיֵיעוּל דוּכְרַנְכוֹן לְטָבָא קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן וְתִתְפָּרְקוּן מִבַּעֲלֵי דְבָבֵיכוֹן


And when you enter upon the order of the line of battle for your country, with oppressors who oppress you, then shall you blow the alarm on the trumpets, that the remembrance of you may come up for good before the Lord your God, that you may be delivered from your enemies.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 10:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:10)


וּבְיוֹם חֶדְוַותְכוֹן וּמוֹעֲדֵיכוֹן וּבְרֵישֵׁי יַרְחֵיכוֹן וְתִתְקְעוּן בַּחֲצוֹצְרָתָא עַל עֲלָוַותְכוֹן וְעַל נִכְסַת קוּדְשֵׁיכוֹן וִיהוֹן לְכוֹן לְדוּכְרָנָא טָבָא קֳדָם אֱלָהָכוֹן בְּרַם סַטָנָא מִתְעַרְבֵב לְקַל יַבְּבוּתְכוֹן אֲנָא הוּא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן


And in the day of your rejoicings, and in your solemnities, and at the beginning of your months, you shall blow with the trumpets over your burnt offerings and your consecrated victims, and they shall be for a good memorial to you before the Lord your God; for Satana shall be troubled at the sound of your jubel notes: I am the Lord your God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 10:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:11)


וַהֲוָה בְּשַׁתָּא תִנְיֵיתָא בְּיַרְחָא תִּנְיֵיתָא הוּא יַרְחָא דְאִיָיר בְּעֶשְרִין לְיַרְחָא אִסְתַּלֵק עֲנַן יְקָרָא מֵעִילַוֵי מַשְׁכְּנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא


And it was in the second year, the second month, which is the month of Ijar, the twentieth day of the month, that the Cloud of Glory was uplifted from above the Tabernacle of Testimony;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 10:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:12)


וּנְטָלוּ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְמַטְלָנֵיהוֹן מִמַדְבְּרָא דְסִינַי וּשְׁרָא עֲנַן יְקָרָא בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְפָּארָן


and the children of Israel went forward upon their journeys from the wilderness of Sinai, and the Cloud of Glory rested in the wilderness of Pharan.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 10:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:13)


וּנְטָלוּ בְּקַדְמֵיתָא עַל פּוּם מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ בִּידָא דְמשֶׁה


And they went forth at the first by the mouth of the Word of the Lord through Mosheh.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 10:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:14)


וּנְטָלוּ טֵיקַס מַשִׁרְיַית בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה לְחֵילֵיהוֹן וְרַבָּא דְהַוָה מְמַנֵי עַל חֵילְוַות שִׁבְטָא דִבְנֵי יְהוּדָה נַחְשׁוֹן בַּר עַמִינָדָב


The standard of the camps of the Beni Jehudah went forward by their hosts, and the Rabba who was appointed over the host of the tribe of the Beni Jehudah was Nachshon bar Amminadab;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 10:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:15)


וְרַבָּא דְהַוָה מְמַנֵי עַל חֵילְוַות שִׁבְטָא דִבְנֵי יִשָשכָר נְתַנְאֵל בַּר צוּעָר

and the Rabba of the Beni Naphtali, Ahira bar Enan.

These are the journeys of the children of Israel by their hosts; the Cloud of Glory was lifted up from above the tabernacle, and they went forward.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 10:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:29)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְחוֹבָב בַּר רְעוּאֵל מִדְיָנָאָה חֲמוֹי דְמשֶׁה נַטְלִין אֲנַחְנָא מִיכָּא לְאַתְרָא דַאֲמַר יְיָ יָתֵיהּ אֶתֵּן לְכוֹן אִיתָא עִמָנָא וְנוֹטִיב לָךְ אֲרוּם יְיָ מַלֵיל לְאוֹטָבָא לְגִיוֹרָא עַל יִשְרָאֵל


And Mosheh said unto Hobab bar Reuel the Midianite, father-in-law of Mosheh, We are journeying from hence to the place of which the Lord hath said, I will give it to you: come with us, and we will do thee good; for the Lord hath spoken to do good unto the sojourner with Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 10:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:30)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ לָא אִיזֵיל אֱלָהֵין לְאַרְעִי וּלְיַלְדוּתִי אִיזֵיל


But he answered him, I will not go (with you) but to my (own) land and to my kindred will I go.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 10:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:31)


וַאֲמַר לָא כְדוּן תִּשְׁבּוֹק יָתָנָא אֲרוּם בְּגִין כֵּן דִידַעְתְּ כַּד הֲוֵינָן שְׁרַן בְּמַדְבְּרָא לְמִידַן וְאַלִיפַת לָנָא עֵיסַק דִינָא וַהֲוָיַת חָבִיב עֲלָן כְּבָבַת עֵינָנָא


But he said, Do not now leave us; for when we were encamped in the wilderness, thou knewest how to judge, and didst teach us the method (or business) of judgment, and thou art dear to us as the apple of our eyes.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 10:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:32)


וִיהֵי אֲרוּם תֵזִיל עִמָנָא וִיהֵי טָבָא הַהוּא דְיוֹטֵב יְיָ עִמָנָא וְנוֹטִיב לָךְ בְּפִלוּג אַרְעָא


And it shall be that if thou wilt go on with us, with the good that the Lord shall benefit us will we benefit thee, in the division of the land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 10:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:33)


וּנְטָלוּ מִטוּרָא דְאִתְגְלֵי עֲלוֹי אִיקַר שְׁכִינְתָּא דַיְיָ מַהֲלַךְ תְּלָתָא יוֹמִין וַאֲרוֹן קְיָימָא דַיְיָ מְטַיֵיל קֳדָמֵיהוֹן תְּלָתִין וְשִׁית מִלִין הֲלִיךְ בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא וְהוּא הֲוָה מִידַבֵּר קֳדָם מַשִׁרְיָיתָא דְיִשְרָאֵל מַהֲלַךְ תְּלָתָא יוֹמִין לְאַתְקָנָא לְהוֹן אֲתַר בֵּית מֵשְׁרוֹי


And they went forward from the mountain where the glory of the Shekinah of the Lord had been revealed, going three days; and the Ark of the Lord's covenant went before them. Thirty and six miles it went that day; it preceded the camp of Israel, going three days, to provide for them a place to encamp in.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 10:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:34)


וַעֲנַן אִיקַר שְׁכִינְתָּא דַיְיָ מְטַלֵיל עִילַוֵיהוֹן בִּימָמָא בְּמֵטַלְהוֹן מִן מַשְׁרִיתָא


And the Cloud of the Lord's Shekinah overshadowed them by day in their going out from the encampment.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 10:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:35)


וַהֲוָה כַּד הֲוָה בָּעֵי לְמֵיטַל אֲרוֹנָא הֲוָה עֲנָנָא מְקַפֵּל וְקָאִים וְלָא הֲוָה נָטִיל עַד דְמשֶׁה הֲוָה קָאֵי בִּצְלוֹ מַצְלֵי וּבָעֵי רַחֲמִין מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וְכֵן אָמַר אִתְגְלֵי כְּדוֹן מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ בִּתְקוֹף רוּגְזָךְ וְיִתְבַּדְרוּן בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבֵיהוֹן דְעַמָךְ וְלָא יֶהֱוֵי לְסַנְאֵיהוֹן רֵיגַל לְמֵיקוּם קֳדָמָךְ


And it was when the ark should go forward, the Cloud gathered itself together and stood still, not going on, until Mosheh, standing in prayer, prayed and supplicated mercy from before the Lord, and thus spake: Let the Word of the Lord be now revealed in the power of Thy anger, that the adversaries of Thy people may be scattered; and let not the banner of those who hate them be uplifted before Thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 10:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/10:36)


וְכַד הֲוָה בָּעֵי לְמִשְׁרֵי אֲרוֹנָא הֲוָה עֲנָנָא מְקַפֵּל וְקָאִים וְלָא הֲוָה פָּרִיס עַד דְהַוָה משֶׁה קָאִים בִּצְלוֹ וּמַצְלֵי וּבָעֵי רַחֲמִין מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וְכֵן אָמַר תּוּב כְּדוֹן מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ בְּרַחֲמָךְ טַבְיָא וּדְבַר יַת עַמָךְ יִשְרָאֵל וְאַשְׁרֵי אִיקַר שְׁכִינָתָךְ בֵּינֵיהוֹן וְרַחֵם יַת רִבְוָותָא דְבֵית יַעֲקב מִנְיַן אַלְפַיָא דְיִשְרָאֵל


But when the ark should rest, the Cloud gathered itself to ether and stood, but did not overspread, until Mosheh, standing in prayer, prayed and besought mercy from before the Lord, thus speaking: Return now, Thou Word of the Lord, in the goodness of Thy mercy, and lead Thy people Israel, and let the glory of Thy Shekinah dwell among them, and (Thy) mercy with the myriads of the house of Jakob, and with the multitudes of the thousands of Israel.



## Chapter 11

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:1)


וַהֲווֹ רַשִׁיעֵי עַמָא כְּמִצְטַעֲרִין מְכַוְונִין וְהַגְיָאן בִּישׁ קֳדָם יְיָ וּשְׁמִיעַ קֳדָם יְיָ וּתְקֵיף רוּגְזֵיהּ וּדְלֵיקַת בְּהוֹן אֵישָׁא מְצַלְהֲבָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וְשֵׁצִיאַת מִן רַשִׁיעַיָא דְבִסְיָיפֵי מַשְׁרִיתָא דְבֵית דָן דְהַוָה פִּיסְלָא עִמְהוֹן


But there were wicked men of the people, who, being discontent, devised and imagined evil before the Lord; and it was heard before the Lord, whose displeasure was moved; and a flaming fire was kindled among them from the Lord, which destroyed some of the wicked in the outskirts of the house of Dan, with whom was a graven image.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:2)


וְצָוַח עַמָא עַל משֶׁה דְיִבְעֵי עֲלֵיהוֹן וְצַלֵי משֶׁה קֳדָם יְיָ וְאִשְׁתְּקָעַת אֵישָׁתָא בְּאַתְרָא


And the people cried to Mosheh to pray for them; and Mosheh did pray before the Lord, and the fire was extinguished where it was.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:3)


וּקְרָא שְׁמֵיהּ דְאַתְרָא הַהוּא דְלֵיקְתָּא אֲרוּם דְלֵיקַת בְּהוֹן אֵישָׁתָא מְצַלְהֲבָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ


And he called the name of that place Enkindlement, because the flaming fire had been enkindled there from before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:4)


וְגִיוֹרַיָא דְאִתְכְּנָשׁוּ בֵּינֵיהוֹן שְׁאִילוּ שְׁאֵילְתָּא וְתָבוּ וּבְכוּ בְּרַם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְאָמְרוּ מַאן יֵיכְלִינָנָא בִּשְרָא


And the strangers who had gathered together among them demanded with demand, and they turned and wept; and the sons of Israel said, Who will give us flesh to eat?


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 11:5

Alas for the people whose food is bread from the heavens! And so murmured they, because the manna was like coriander-seed, round, when it came down from the heavens, and when it had been sanctified its appearance was as the likeness of Bedilcha.

And the wicked people looked about, and collected, and ground it in the mill. But he who would, bruised it in the mortar, or dressed it in the pot, or made cakes of it; and the taste of it was like the taste of cream covered with oil.

Have I made or borne all this people as from the womb? are they my children, that Thou saidst to me in Mizraim, Bear the toil of them with thy strength, as the instructor of youth beareth, until they be carried into the land which Thou hast sworn unto their fathers?

And I will be revealed in the glory of My Shekinah, and will speak with thee there, and will amplify the spirit of prophecy that is upon thee, and bestow it upon them; and they shall sustain with thee the burden of the people, that thou mayest not bear it alone.

And say thou to the people, Make ready against the morrow that you may eat flesh; because you have lamented before the Lord, saying, Who will give us flesh to eat? for it was better with us in Mizraim. The Lord therefore will give you flesh that you may eat.

for a month of days, until the smell of it cometh forth from your nostrils, and it become a loathing to you; because you have been contemptuous against the Word of the Lord, whose glorious Shekinah dwelleth among you, and because you have wept before Him, saying, Why should we have come out from Mizraim?

Then Mosheh went forth from the tabernacle, the house of the Shekinah, and told the people the words of the Lord. And he called together the seventy men, the elders of Israel, and placed them around the tabernacle.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 11:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:25)

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 11:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:26)


וְאִשְׁתְּיָירוּ תְּרֵין גוּבְרִין בְּמַשְׁרִיתָא שְׁמֵיהּ דְחָד אֶלְדָד וּשְׁמֵיהּ דְתִנְיַין מֵידָד בְּנוֹי דְאֶלְצָפָן בַּר פַּרְנָךְ דִילֵידַת לֵיהּ יוֹכֶבֶד בְּרַת לֵוִי בִּזְמַן דְפַּטְרָהּ עַמְרָם גַבְרָהּ וְאִתְנְסִיבַת לֵיהּ עַד דְלָא יְלֵידַת יַת משֶׁה וּשְׁרַת עֲלֵיהוֹן רוּחַ נְבוּאָה אֶלְדָד הֲוָה מִתְנַבֵּי וַאֲמַר הָא משֶׁה מִתְכְּנִישׁ מִן עַלְמָא וִיהוֹשֻׁע בַּר נוּן מְשַׁמֵשׁ מַשְׁרִיתָא קָאִי מִן בַּתְרֵיהּ וּמְדַבֵּר עַמָא בֵּית יִשְרָאֵל וּמֵיעַל יַתְהוֹן לְאַרַע כְּנַעֲנָאֵי וּמַחֲסִין יָתָהּ לְהוֹן מֵידָד הֲוָה מִתְנַבֵּי וַאֲמַר הָא סִלְוֵי סַלְקִין מִן יַמָא וְחַפְיָין כָּל מַשְׁרִיתָא דְיִשְרָאֵל וְיֶהֱוֵי לְעַמָא לְתוּקְלָא בְּרַם תְּרֵיהוֹן כַּחֲדָא מִתְנַבִּין וְאָמְרִין הָא מַלְכָּא סְלִיק מִן אַרְעָא דְמָגוֹג בְּסוֹף יוֹמַיָא וּמַכְנַשׁ מַלְכִין קִטְרֵי תַגִין וְאַפַרְכִין לוֹבְשֵׁי שִרְיוֹנִין וְכָל עַמְמַיָא יִשְׁתַּמְעוּן לֵיהּ וּמְסַדְרִין קְרָבָא בְּאַרְעָא דְיִשְרָאֵל עַל בְּנֵי גַלְוָתָא בְּרַם קִירִיס אִיטִימוֹס לְהוֹן בְּשַׁעַת אֲנִיקִין וּמִקְטַל כֻּלְהוֹן בִּיקִידַת נִשְׁמָתָא בְּשַׁלְהוֹבִית אֵשָׁתָא דְנַפְקָא מִתְּחוֹת כּוּרְסֵי יְקָרָא וְנָפְלִין פִּגְרֵיהוֹן עַל טַוְורַיָא דְאַרְעָא דְיִשְרָאֵל וְיֵיתוּן כָּל חֵיוַת בָּרָא וְצִיפְרֵי שְׁמַיָא וְיֵיכְלוּן גוּשְׁמֵיהוֹן וּמִבָּתַר כְּדֵין יֵיחוֹן כָּל מֵתַיָא דְיִשְרָאֵל וְיִתְפַּנְקוּן מִן טַוְורָא דְאִצְטְנַע לְהוֹן מִן שֵׁירוּיָא וִיקַבְּלוּן אֲגַר עוֹבָדֵיהוֹן וְהִינוּן הֲווֹן מִן סָבַיָא דִסְלִיקוּ בִּפְטִיקַיָא כְּתוֹבַיָא וְלָא נַפְקָן לְמַשְׁכְּנָא דְאִיטַמָרוּ לְמֵעֲרוֹק מִן רַבָּנוּתָא וְאִתְנַבִּיאוּ בְּמַשְׁרִיתָא


But two men had remained in the camp; the name of the one Eldad, and the name of the second Medad, the sons of Elizaphan bar Parnak, whom Jokebed the daughter of Levi bare to him when Amram her husband had put her away; and to whom she had been espoused before she gave birth to Mosheh. And the Spirit of prophecy resting upon them, Eldad prophe­sied, and said: Behold, Mosheh shall be gathered from the world; and Jehoshua bar Nun, the minister of the camps, will be established after him, and will lead the people of the house of Israel into the land of Kenaan, and make it their inheritance. Medad prophesied, and said: Behold, quails come up from the sea, and cover all the camp of Israel; but they will be to the people (a cause of) an offence. And both of them prophesied together, and said: Behold, a king will arise from the land of Magog, at the end of the days, and will assemble kings crowned with crowns, and captains wearing armour, and him will all nations obey. And they will set battle in array in the land of Israel against the children of the captivity; but already is it provided that in the hour of distresses all of them shall perish by the burning blast of the flame that cometh forth from beneath the Throne of Glory; and their carcases shall fall upon the mountains of the land of Israel, and the wild beasts of the field and the fowls of the sky shall come and consume their dead bodies. And afterward will all the dead of Israel live (again), and be feasted from the ox which hath been set apart for them from the beginning, and they shall receive the reward of their works. And they were of the elders who stood in the registers among them; but they had not gone forth to the tabernacle, but had hidden to escape from the dignity; yet they prophesied in the camp.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 11:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:27)


וּרְהַט טַלְיָא חַד וּתְנֵי לְמשֶׁה וַאֲמַר אֶלְדָד וּמֵידָד מִתְנַבְּיָין הָכֵין בְּמַשְׁרִיתָא


And a certain young man ran, and told to Mosheh, and said: Eldad and Medad are prophesying thus in the camp.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 11:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:28)


וְאָתֵיב יְהוֹשֻׁעַ בַּר נוּן מְשׁוּמְשָׁנֵיהּ דְמשֶׁה וַאֲמַר רִבּוֹנִי משֶׁה בָּעֵי רַחֲמִין מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וּכְלִי מִנְהוֹן רוּחַ נְבוּאָתָא


And Jehoshua bar Nun, the minister of Mosheh, answered and said: Ribboni Mosheh, pray for mercy before the Lord, that the Spirit of prophecy may be withheld from them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 11:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:29)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ משֶׁה הֲמִן בִּגְלַל דְאִתְנַבּוּ עָלַי דַאֲנָא מִתְכְּנַשׁ מִן עַלְמָא וְאַנְתְּ מְשַׁמֵשׁ מִן בַּתְרַיי אַנְתְּ מְקַנֵי לִי רְעֵינָא פּוֹן דִיהוֹן כָּל עַמָא דַיְיָ נְבִייָן אֲרוּם יִתֵּן יְיָ יַת רוּחַ נְבִיאוּתֵיהּ עֲלֵיהוֹן


But Mosheh said to him, Because they prophesy concerning me that I am to be gathered from the world, and that thou art to minister after me, art thou jealous for my sake? I would that all the Lord's people were prophets, and that He would bestow the Spirit of prophecy upon them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 11:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:30)


וְאִתְכְּנַשׁ משֶׁה לְמַשְׁרִיתָא הוּא וְכָל סָבֵי יִשְרָאֵל


And Mosheh proceeded to the camp, he and all the elders of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 11:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:31)


וְרוּחָא דְעַלְעוּלָא נְפָקַת וּנְטָלַת בְּרוּגְזָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וּבְעָא לְמִשְׁטְפֵיהּ לְעַלְמָא אִילוּלֵי זְכוּתָא דְמשֶׁה וְאַהֲרן וּנְתֵיב בְּיַמָא רַבָּא וְאַפְרַח שַלְוֵי מִן יַמָא רַבָּא וּשְׁרָא עַל דֵין דִזְעֵיר בְּמַשְׁרִיָתָא כְּמַהֲלַךְ יוֹמָא לְצִיפּוּנָא וּכְמַהֲלַךְ יוֹמָא לְדָרוֹמָא וּכְרוּם תַּרְתֵּין אַמִין הֲוָה פָּרַח עַל אַנְפֵּי אַרְעָא וַהֲווֹ מְהַלְכִין בְּהוֹן עַד פְּרַתְהוֹן מְטוּל דְלָא יִלְעוּן בִּזְמַן מְכַנְשְׁהוֹן יַתְהוֹן


And the wind of a tempest went forth, and came violently from before the Lord, so as to have swept the world away, but for the righteousness of Mosheh and Aharon: and it blew over the Great Sea, and made the quails fly from the Great Sea, and settle wherever there was place in the camp, as a day's journey northward and southward, and at the height as of two cubits; they flew upon the face of the ground, and went upon their bellies, so that (the people) were not wearied while they collected them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 11:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:32)


וְקָמוּ מְחַסְרֵי הֵימְנוּתָא דִי בְּעַמָא כָּל יוֹמָא הַהוּא וְכָל לֵילְיָא וְכָל יוֹמָא דְבַתְרוֹי וּכְנָשׁוּ יַת סַלְוֵי דִקְטִיעַ וּדְחַגִיר כְּנַשׁ עַשְרָא כִּרְוַויַן וּשְׁטָחוּ לְהוֹן מַשְׁטִיחִין חִזְרָנוּת מַשְׁרִיתָא


And they who had been wanting in faith arose: and all that day, and all the night, and all the day that followed, they gathered the quails; even he who was lame and infirm gathered ten korin, and they spread them abroad round about the camps.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 11:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:33)


רַשִׁיעַיָא הֲווֹן אָכְלִין מִן בִּשְרָא וּלְמַאן דְיַהֲבִית לְהוֹן לָא הֲווֹן מְכָרְכִין בִּישְרָא עַד כְּדוֹן הֲוָה בֵּינֵי כַּכֵיהוֹן עַד לָא פָסַק וְרוּגְזָא דַיְיָ תַּקִיף בְּרִשְׁעֵי עַמָא וּקְטֵיל יְיָ בְּעַמָא קְטוֹל סַגִי לַחֲדָא


The wicked ate of the flesh, yet offered no thanksgiving to Him who had given it to them: but while the flesh was between their teeth, and not consumed, the anger of the Lord waxed strong against the evil people, and the Lord slew the people with a very great mortality.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 11:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:34)


וּקְרָא יַת שׁוּם אַתְרָא הַהוּא קִבְרֵי דִמְשַׁיְילֵי בִּישְרָא אֲרוּם תַּמָן קְבָרוּ יַת עַמָא דְשַׁיְילוּ בִּישְרָא


And he called the name of that place, The Graves of the Desirers of Flesh; for there they buried the people who had desired flesh.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 11:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/11:35)


וּמִן קִבְרֵי דִמְשַׁיְילֵי בִּישְרָא נְטָלוּ עַמָא לַחֲצֵרוֹת וַהֲווֹן בַּחֲצֵרוֹת


And from the Graves of the Desirers the people journeyed to Hatseroth, and they were in Hatseroth.



## Chapter 12

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:1)


וְאִשְׁתְּעִיוּ מִרְיָם וְאַהֲרן בְּמשֶׁה פִּתְגָמִין דְלָא מְהַגְנִין עַל עֵיסַק אִתְּתָא כּוּשְׁיָיתָא דְאַסְבוֹהִי כוּשָׁאֵי לְמשֶׁה בְּמֵיעַרְקֵיהּ מִן קֳדָם פַּרְעה וְרִיחְקָהּ אֲרוּם לְאִיתָא אַסְבוֹהִי יַת מַלְכְּתָא דְכוּשׁ וְרָחִיק מִינָהּ


And Miriam and Aharon spake against Mosheh words that were not becoming with respect to the Kushaitha whom the Kushaee had caused Mosheh to take when he had fled from Pharoh, but whom he had sent away because they had given him the queen of Kush, and he had sent her away.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:2)


וְאָמְרוּ הַלְחוֹד בְּרַם עִם משֶׁה מַלֵיל יְיָ דְאִתְפְּרַשׁ מִתַּשְׁמִישׁ דְעָרִיס הֲלָא אוֹף עִמָנָא מַלֵיל וּשְׁמִיעַ קֳדָם יְיָ


And they said, Hath the Lord spoken only with Mosheh, that he should be separated from the married life? Hath He not spoken with us also? And it was heard before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:3)


וְגַבְרָא משֶׁה עִנְוְותָן בְּדַעֲתֵּיהּ לַחֲדָא מִן כָּל בְּנֵי נְשָׁא דְעַל אַנְפֵּי אַרְעָא וְלָא חָשׁ לְמִלֵיהוֹן


But the man Mosheh was more bowed down in his mind than all the children of men upon the face of the earth; neither cared he for their words.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:4)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה וּלְאַהֲרן וּלְמִרְיָם פּוּקוּ תְּלָתֵיכוֹן לְמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וּנְפָקוּ תְּלָתֵיהוֹן


And the Lord said to Mosheh, to Aharon, and to Miriam, Come forth, you three, to the tabernacle. And those three went forth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:5)


וְאִתְגְלֵי יְקָרָא דַיְיָ בְּעַמוּדָא דַעֲנַן יְקָרָא וְקָם בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכְּנָא וּקְרָא אַהֲרן וּמִרְיָם וּנְפָקוּ תַּרְוֵיהוֹן


And the Glory of the Lord was revealed in the Cloud of Glory, and He stood at the door of the tabernacle, and called Aharon and Miriam: and those two came forth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:6)


וַאֲמַר שִׁמְעוּ בְּבָעוּ פִּתְגָמָי עַד דַאֲמַלֵיל אִין יֶהֱווֹן כָּל נְבִיָא דְקָמוּ מִן יוֹמַת עַלְמָא מִתְמַלֵיל עִמְהוֹן הֵיכְמָא דְמִתְמַלֵיל עִם משֶׁה דְמֵימְרָא דַיְיָ בְּחֵזְיוּ לְוַתְהוֹן מִתְגְלֵי בְּחֶלְמָא מְמַלֵילְנָא עִמְהוֹן


And He said, Hear now My words, while I speak. Have any of the prophets who have arisen from the days of old been spoken with as Mosheh hath been? To those (prophets) the Word of the Lord hath been revealed in apparition, speaking with them in a dream.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 12:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:7)


לָא כְדֵין אָרְחָא דְמשֶׁה עַבְדִי בְּכָל בֵּית יִשְרָאֵל עַמִי מְהֵימָן הוּא


Not so is the way with Mosheh My servant; in all the house of Israel My people he is faithful.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 12:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:8)


מַמְלַל עִם מַמְלַל מַלֵילִית עִמֵיהּ דְיִתְפְּרַשׁ מִתַּשְׁמִישׁ דְעָרִיס וְחֵיזוּ וְלָא בְּטוּמְרָא מִתְגְלֵינָא לֵיהּ בְּאַסָנָא וּדְמוּ דְבָתַר שְׁכִינְתִּי חָזֵי וּמָא דֵין לָא דְחַלְתּוּן לְאִשְׁתְּעוֹיֵי כַּהֲלֵין מִלַיָא בְּעַבְדִי בְמשֶׁה


Speaker with speaker have I spoken with him, who hath separated himself from the married life; but in vision, and not with mystery, revealed I Myself to him at the bush, and he beheld the likeness of My Shekinah. And why have you not feared to speak such words of My servant Mosheh?


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 12:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:9)


וְאִסְתַּלֵק אִיקַר שְׁכִינְתָּא דַיְיָ מִנְהוֹן וְאָזִיל


And the glory of the Lord's Shekinah ascended, and went.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 12:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:10)


וַעֲנַן אִיקַר שְׁכִינְתָּא דַיְיָ אִסְתַּלֵק מֵעִילַוֵי מַשְׁכְּנָא וְהָא מִרְיָם לָקַת בְּצוּרְעָא וְאִסְתַּכֵּל אַהֲרן לְוַת מִרְיָם וְהָא לָקַת בְּצוּרְעָא


And the glorious Cloud of the Lord's Shekinah went up from above the tabernacle; and, behold, Miriam was seized with the leprosy. And Aharon looked upon Miriam, and, behold, she had been smitten with leprosy.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 12:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:11)


וַאֲמַר אַהֲרן לְמשֶׁה בְּמָטוּ מִינָךְ רִבּוֹנִי לָא תְשַׁוֵי עֲלָנָא חוֹבָא דֵי אִיטַפַּשְׁנָא וְדִי סְרַחֲנָא


And Aharon said to Mosheh, I beseech of thee, my lord, not to lay upon us the sin we have foolishly committed, and by which we have transgressed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 12:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:12)


בְּבָעוּ מִינָךְ לָא תֶהֱוֵי מִרְיָם אַחֲתָן מְצָרְעָא מְסָאֲבָא בְּאָהֵילָא כְּמֵיתָא דְהִיא מְדַמְיָא לְוַלְדָא דְאִישְׁלַם בִּמְעֵי אִמֵיהּ תִּשְׁעָא יַרְחִין וְכֵיוָן דְיִמְטָא קִיצָא לְמִפּוֹק לְעַלְמָא וְאִתְאָכִיל פַּלְגוּת בִּישְרֵיהּ בִּזְמַן דְיַתְבָא אִמֵיהּ עַל מַתְבְּרָא וִימוּת וַלְדָא וּמַפְקָא יָתֵיהּ חַיְתָא כַּד מְחַתֵּיךְ הֵיכְדֵין כַּד הֲוֵינָן בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם הֲוַת מִרְיָם אַחֲתָן חַמְיָא יָתָן בְּגָלוּתָן וּבְטִלְטוּלָן וּבְשִׁעֲבּוּדָן וּכְדוּ מְטַת זִמְנָא לְמִפַּק וּלְמֵירוּת יַת אַרְעָא דְיִשְרָאֵל הָא כְדוּן הִיא מִתְמְנָעָא מִינָן בְּמָטוּ מִינָךְ רִבּוֹנִי צַלִי כְעַן עֲלָהּ וְלָא נוֹבְדָא זְכוּתָא מִגוֹ קְהָלָא


I entreat thee that Miriam, our sister, may not be defiled with leprosy in the tent, as the dead, for it is with her as with the infant which, having well fulfilled the time of the womb, perishes at the birth: so Miriam was with us in the land of Mizraim, seeing us in our captivity, our dispersion, our servitude; but now, when the time hath come for our going forth to possess the land of Israel behold she is kept back from us. I entreat thee, my lord, to pray for her, that her righteousness may not come to nought among the congregation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 12:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:13)


וְצַלִי משֶׁה וּבְעָא רַחֲמִין קֳדָם יְיָ לְמֵימָר בְּבָעוּ בְּרַחֲמִין אֱלָהָא רַחֲמָנָא בְּבָעוּ אֱלָהָא דְשַׁלִיט בְּנִשְׁמַת כָּל בִּישְרָא אַסֵי בְּבָעוּ לָהּ


And Mosheh did pray, and seek mercy before the Lord, saying: I pray through the compassions of the merciful God, O Eloha, who hast power over the life of all flesh, heal her, I beseech thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 12:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:14)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה וְאָבִיהָ אִילוּ מַנְזִיף נְזַף בְּאַנְפָּהּ הֲלָא הֲוָה מִכְסְפָה וּמִיטַרְדָא שִׁבְעָתֵי יוֹמִין וּכְדֵין דְנַזְפָה בָּהּ מִן דִינָא דְתִכְסוֹף אַרְבֵּיסַר יוֹמִין אֱלָהֵן מִסְתְּיָה דְתִטְרַד שִׁבְעָתֵי יוֹמִין מִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא וַאֲנָא מְעַכֵּב בְּגִין זְכוּתָךְ עֲנָנֵי יְקָרִי וּמַשְׁכְּנָא וַאֲרוֹנָא וְכָל יִשְרָאֵל עַד זְמַן דְתִיתְּסֵי וּמִבָּתַר כְּדֵין תִּתְכְּנַשׁ


And the Lord said to Mosheh, If her father had corrected her, would she not have been disgraced, and secluded seven days? But today, when I correct her, much more right is it that she should be dishonoured fourteen days: yet shall it suffice to seclude her seven days without the camp; and for thy righteousness will I make the Cloud of My Glory, the tabernacle, the ark, and all Israel, tarry until the time that she is healed, and then re-admitted.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 12:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:15)


וְאִיטְרְדַת מִרְיָם מִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא שִׁבְעָתֵי יוֹמִין וְעַמָא לָא נָטַל עַד זְמַן דְאִתַּסְיַת מִרְיָם


And Miriam was kept apart without the camp for seven days, and the people went not forward until the time that Miriam was healed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 12:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/12:16)


אַף עַל גַב דְאִתְחַיָיבַת מִרְיָם נְבִיאֲתָא לְמִלְקֵי בְּצוֹרְעָא בְּעַלְמָא הָדֵין אוּלְפַן סַגִי אִית לְהוּ לְעַלְמָא דְאָתֵי לְצַדִיקַיָא וּלְנַטְרֵי פִּקוּדֵי אוֹרַיְיתָא לְפוּם דְאִתְיְעָדַת מִרְיָם נְבִיאֲתָא שָׁעָא זְעֵירָא לְמִידוֹעַ מָה הֲוָה בְסֵיפֵיהּ דְמשֶׁה בְּהַהוּא זְכוּתָא הֲווֹן כָּל יִשְרָאֵל אַשִׁתִּין רִבְוָון דְהִינוּן סְכוֹם תְּמַנְיָן לִגְיוֹנִין וַעֲנָנֵי יְקָרָא וּמַשְׁכְּנָא וּבֵירָא לָא זַיְיעִין וְלָא נַטְלִין עַד זְמַן דְאִיתְסִיאַת מִרְיָם נְבִיאֲתָא וּמִן בָּתַר כְּדֵין נְטָלוּ עַמָא מֵחֲצֵרוֹת וּשְׁרוֹן בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְפָארָן


But though Miriam the prophetess had made herself liable to be stricken with leprosy in this world, the doctrine is ample that in the world to come (there remaineth a reward) for the just, and for them who keep the commandments of the law. And because Miriam the prophetess had watched for a little hour to know what would be the fate of Mosheh; (Exod. ii) for the sake of that merit all Israel, numbering sixty myriads, being eighty legions, and the Cloud of Glory, the tabernacle, and the well, went not, nor proceeded, till the time that she was healed: and afterward the people journeyed from Hatseroth, and encamped in the wilderness of Pharan.
