אִלֵין שְׁמָהָן גוּבְרַיָא דְשָׁדַר משֶׁה לְאַלְלָא יַת אַרְעָא וּכְדִי חֲמָא משֶׁה עִנְוָותָנוּתֵיהּ קָרָא משֶׁה לְהוֹשֵׁעַ בַּר נוּן יְהוֹשֻׁעַ


These are the names of the men whom Mosheh sent to explore the land; and when Mosheh saw his humility, he called Hoshea bar Nun Jehoshua.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 13:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:17)


וְשָׁדַר יַתְהוֹן משֶׁה לְאַלְלָא יַת אַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן סָקוּ בַּהֲדֵין צִטְרָא בִּדְרוֹמָא וְתִסְקוּן לְטוּרָא


And Mosheh sent them to survey the land of Kenaan, and said to them, Go up on this side by the south, and ascend the mountain,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 13:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:18)


וְתֵיחֲמוּן יַת אַרְעָא מָה הִיא וְיַת עַמָא דְיָתֵיב עֲלָהּ הֲתַקִיף הוּא אִין חֲלָשׁ הַזְעֵיר הוּא אִין סַגִי


and survey the country, what it is, and the people who dwell in it; whether they be strong or weak, few or many;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 13:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:19)


וּמָה אַרְעָא דְהוּא יָתֵיב בָּהּ הֲטַבְתָּא הִיא אִין בִּישְׁתָּא וּמָה קִרְוַיָא דְהוּא יָתֵיב בְּהוֹן הַבִכְּרַכֵּי פַּצִיחֵי חַנְיָן אִין בְּחַקְרִין


what the land is in which they dwell, whether good or bad; what cities they inhabit, whether they live in towns that are open or walled;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 13:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:20)


וּמָה שְׁבַח אַרְעָא הַשְׁמִינִין אִינוּן פִּרְיָהּ אִין פַּתְרָנִין הַאִית בָּהּ אִילָנִין דְמֵיכַל אִין לָא וְתַעַבְדוּן חֶזְקָתָא וְתִסְבּוּן מֵאִבָּא דְאַרְעָא וְיוֹמַיָא דִי אֲזָלוּ בְּעֶשְרִין וְתִשְׁעָה לְיַרְחָא דְסִיוָן יוֹמֵי דְמִן בִּכּוּרֵי עִנְבִין


and what the reputation of the land, whether its productions are rich or poor, and the trees of it fruitful or not. And do valiantly, and bring back some of the fruit of the land. And the day on which they went was the nineteenth of the month of Sivan, (about) the days of the first grapes.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 13:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:21)


וּסְלִיקוּ וְאַלִילוּ יַת אַרְעָא מִן מַדְבְּרָא דְצִין עַד פַּלְטִיוֹת מֵעַלָךְ לְאַנְטוּכְיָא


They went up, therefore, and explored the country, from the wilderness of Zin, unto the roads by which thou comest unto Antiochia.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 13:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:22)


וּסְלִיקוּ מִן צְטַר דָרוֹמָא וְאָתוֹ עַד חֶבְרוֹן וְתַמָן אֲחִימָן שֵׁשַׁי וְתַלְמַי מַרְבְּיָנֵי דַעֲנָק גִבָּרָא וְחֶבְרוֹן שְׁבַע שְׁנִין אִתְבַּנְיַת קֳדָם עַד לָא תִתְבְּנֵי טָאנֵיס דְמִצְרַיִם


They went up from the side of the south and came to Hebron, where were Achiman, Sheshai, and Talmai, sons of Anak the giant Now Hebron was built seven years before Tanis in Mizraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 13:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:23)


וְאָתוּ עַד נַחְלָא דְאַתְכְּלָא וְקָצוּ מִתַּמָן עוֹבַרְתָּא וְאִתְכַּל דְעַנְבִין חַד וְסוֹבְרוֹהִי בְּאַסְלָא בִּכְּתֵיף תְּרֵין מִנְהוֹן וְכֵן מִן רוּמָנַיָא וְכֵן מִן תֵּינַיָא


They came then to the stream of the grapes (or bunches, ethkala), and cut down from thence a branch with one cluster of grapes, and carried it on a staff on the shoulders of two of them, and also took they of the pomegranates and the figs.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 13:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:24)


לְאַתְרָא הַהוּא קָרוּ נַחְלָא דְאַתְכְּלָא עַל עֵיסַק עוֹבַרְתָּא דְקָצִיצוּ מִתַּמָן בְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וַהֲוָה חַמְרָא נָטֵיף מִנֵיהּ כְּנַחְלָא


Now that place they call the stream of the cluster, from the branch which the sons of Israel cut down there; and wine was dropping from it like a stream.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 13:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:25)


וְתָבוּ מִלְאַלְלָא יַת אַרְעָא בִתְמַנְיָא יוֹמִין בְּיַרְחָא דְאָב מִסוֹף אַרְבְּעִין יוֹמִין


And they returned from exploring the land on the eighth day of the month Ab, at the end of forty days.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 13:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:26)


וַאֲזָלוּ וְאָתוּ לְוַת משֶׁה וּלְוַת אַהֲרן וּלְוַת כָּל כְּנִישְׁתָּא דְיִשְרָאֵל לְמַדְבְּרָא דְפָּארָן לִרְקַם וְאָתִיבוּ לְהוֹן פִּתְגָמָא וּלְוַת כָּל כְּנִישְׁתָּא וְחָמִיאוֹנוּן יַת אִבָּא דְאַרְעָא


And they came to Mosheh and Aharon, and all the congregation of the children of Israel in the wilderness of Pharan, at Rekem, and returned them word, to them and the whole congregation, and showed them the fruit of the land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 13:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:27)


וְאִשְׁתָּעִיוּ לֵיהּ וְאָמָרוּ אָתָאנָא לְאַרְעָא דְשָׁדַרְתָּנָא וְאוּף עַבְדָא חֲלַב וּדְבָשׁ הִיא וְדֵין אִבָּהּ


And they recounted to him, and said: We went into the country to which thou didst send us; and it indeed produceth milk and honey, and this is the fruit of it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 13:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:28)


לְחוֹד אֲרוּם תַּקִיף עַמָא דְיָתֵיב בְּאַרְעָא וְקִירְוַיָא כְּרִיכִין חַנְיָין רַבְרְבָן לַחֲדָא וְאוּף מַרְבְּיָנֵי דַעֲנָק גִבָּרָא חֲמֵינָא תַמָן


But the people who inhabit the country are strong, and the fortified cities they inhabit very great; and we saw also there the sons of Anak the giant.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 13:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:29)


עֲמַלְקָאֵי יַתְבִין בְּאַרַע דָרוֹמָא וְחִתָּאֵי וִיבוּסָאֵי וֶאֱמוֹרָאֵי יַתְבִין בְּטַוְורָא וּכְנַעֲנָאֵי יַתְבִין עַל יַמָא וְעַל תְּחוּם יוֹרְדְנָא


The Amalekites dwell in the south, the Hittites, Jebusites, and Amorites in the mountains; but the Kenaanites dwell by the sea, and by the bank of the Jordan.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 13:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:30)


וּשְׁתִיק כָּלֵב יַת עַמָא וְאֵיצֵתִינוּן לְוַת משֶׁה וַאֲמַר מֵיסַק נֵיסַק וְנִירַת יָתָהּ אֲרוּם מֵיכַל נֵיכוֹל לָהּ


And Kaleb stilled the people, and made them listen to Mosheh, and said: Let us go up and possess it, for we are able to take it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 13:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:31)


וְגוּבְרַיָא דְסַלִיקוּ עִמֵיהּ אָמְרוּ לָא נֵיכוֹל לְמֵיסוֹק לְוַת עַמָא אֲרוּם תַּקִיף הוּא מִנָנָא


But the men who had gone up with him said, We are not able to go up to the people, for they are stronger than we.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 13:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:32)


וְאַפִּיקוּ טִיב בִּישׁ עַל אַרְעָא דְאַלִילוּ יָתָהּ לְוַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְמֵימָר אַרְעָא דְעָבַרְנוּ בָהּ לְאַלְלָא יָתָהּ אַרְעָא מְקַטְלָא יַתְבָהָא הִיא בְמַרְעִין וְכָל עַמָא דְגַוָהּ גַבְרַיָא מָרֵי מִיכְלַן בִּישִׁין


And they brought out an evil report about the land which they had surveyed, to the sons of Israel, saying, The country through which we have passed to explore it is a land that killeth its inhabitants with diseases; and all the people who are in it are giants, masters of evil ways.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 13:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/13:33)


וְתַמָן חָמֵינָא יַת גַבְרַיָא בְנֵי עֲנָק מִגְנִיסַת גוּבְרַיָא וַהֲוֵינָא דַמְיַין בְּאַנְפֵּי נַפְשָׁתָנָא הֵי כְקַמְצָן וְהֵיכְדֵין הֲוֵינָא דַמְיַין בְּאַנְפֵּי נַפְשֵׁיהוֹן


And there we saw the giants, the sons of Anak, of the race of the giants; and we appeared to ourselves to be as locusts; and so we appeared to them.



## Chapter 14

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:1)


וַאֲרֵימַת כָּל כְּנִישְׁתָּא וִיהָבוּ יַת קָלֵיהוֹן וּבְכוֹ עַמָא בְּלֵילְיָא הַהוּא וְאִתְקְבִיעַת לְהוֹן הַהוּא לֵילְיָא לְבָכוּתָא לְדָרֵיהוֹן


And all the congregation lifted up and gave forth their voice, and the people wept that night: and it was confirmed (as a punishment) that they should weep on that night in their generations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:2)


וְאִתְרַעֲמוּ עַל משֶׁה וְעַל אַהֲרן כָּל בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן כָּל כְּנִישְׁתָּא לְוַאי דְמִתְנָא בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם אוֹ בְּמַדְבְּרָא הָדֵין לְוַאי דְמִתְנָא


And all the sons of Israel murmured against Mosheh and Aharon, and said: Would that we had died in the land of Mizraim, or that we may die in this wilderness!


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:3)


וּלְמָא יְיָ מָעִיל יָתָנָא לְאַרְעָא הֲדָא לְמֵיפַּל בְּחַרְבָּא דִכְנַעֲנָאֵי נְשָׁנָא וְטַפְלָנָא יְהוֹן לְמֵיבַז הֲלָא טַב לָנָא דְנָתוּב לְמִצְרָיִם


Why is the Lord bringing us into this land, to fall by the sword of the Kenaanaah, and our wives and little ones to become a prey? Will it not be better to return into Mizraim?


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:4)


וַאֲמָרוּ גְבַר לְאָחוֹי נְמַנֵי עֲלָן מְלִיךְ לְרֵישׁ וְנָתוּב לְמִצְרַיִם


And one man said to his brother, Let us appoint a king over us for a chief, and return to Mizraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:5)


וְאִתְרְכִינוּ משֶׁה וְאַהֲרן עַל אַפֵּיהוֹן קֳדָם כָּל כְּנִישְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


And Mosheh and Aharon bowed upon their faces before all the congregation of the sons of Israel;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:6)


וִיהוֹשֻׁעַ בַּר נוּן וְכָלֵב בַּר יְפֻנֶה מִן מְאַלְלֵי יַת אַרְעָא בְּזָעוּ לְבוּשֵׁיהוֹן


and Jehoshua bar Nun and Kaleb bar Jephunneh of the explorers of the land rent their clothes,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:7)


וַאֲמָרוּ לְכָל כְּנִישְׁתָּא לְמֵימָר אַרְעָא דְעָבַרְנָא בָּהּ לְאַלָלָא יָתָהּ טָבָא אַרְעָא לַחֲדָא לַחֲדָא


and spake to the congregation, saying: The land we went to see is an exceedingly good land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:8)


אִין רַעֲוָא דַיְיָ אִית בָּנָא וְאָעֵיל יָתָנָא לְאַרְעָא הָדָא וְיִתְּנִינָהּ לָנָא אַרְעָא דְהִיא עַבְדָא חֲלַב וּדְבָשׁ


If the Lord hath pleasure in us, He will bring us into this land, and give it us, a land producing milk and honey.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:9)


בְּרַם בְּפִיקוּדַיָא דַיְיָ לָא תְבַטְשׁוּן וְאַתּוּן לָא תְדַחֲלוּן מִן עַמָא דְאַרְעָא אֲרוּם בִּידָנָא מְסִירִין הִינוּן תַּשׁ חֵיל גְבוּרַתְהוֹן מֵעֲלֵיהוֹן וּמֵימְרָא דַיְיָ בְּסַעֲדָנָא לָא תִדַחֲלוּן מִנְהוֹן


Only do not rebel against the commandments of the Lord, and you need not fear the people of the land, for they are delivered into our hands; the strength of their power hath failed from them, but the Word of the Lord will be our helper; fear them not.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:10)


וַאֲמָרוּ כָּל כְּנִישְׁתָּא לְאַטָלָא יַתְהוֹן בְּאַבְנַיָא וְאִיקַר שְׁכִינְתָּא דַיְיָ אִתְגְלֵי בַּעֲנָנֵי יְקָרָא בְּמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא


But all the congregation said they would stone them with stones. And the glorious Shekinah of the Lord was revealed in bright clouds at the tabernacle.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:11)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה עַד אֵימָתַי יְהוֹן מְרַגְזִין קֳדָמַי עַמָא הָדֵין וְעַד אֵימָתַי לָא יְהֵימְנוּן בְּמֵימְרִי לְכָל אַתְוָותָא דְעָבָדִית בֵּינֵיהוֹן


And the Lord said to Mosheh, How long will this people provoke Me to anger? How long will they disbelieve in My Word, for all the signs I have wrought among them?


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:12)


אֶמְחֵי יַתְהוֹן בְּמַחְתָא דְמוֹתָא וְאֵישֵׁצֵי יַתְהוֹן וְאַמְנֵי יָתָךְ לְעַם רַב וְתַקִיף מִנְהוֹן


I will strike them with deadly plague and destroy them, and will appoint thee for a people greater and stronger than they.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:13)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה וְיִשְׁמְעוּן בְּנֵיהוֹן דְמִצְרָאֵי דְאִשְׁתַּנָקוּ בְּיַמָא אֲרוּם אֲסֵיקְתָּא בְּחֵילָךְ יַת עַמָא הָדֵין מִבֵּינֵיהוֹן


But Mosheh said, The children of the Mizraee, whom Thou didst drown in the sea, will hear that Thou didst bring up this people from among them by Thy power,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:14)


וְיֵימְרוּן בְּחֶדְוָוא לְיַתְבֵי אַרְעָא הֲדָא דְיִשְׁמְעוּ אֲרוּם אַנְתְּ הוּא יְיָ דִשְׁכִינְתָּךְ שַׁרְיָא בְּגוֹ עַמָא הָדֵין דִבְעֵינֵיהוֹן חָמוּן שְׁכִינַת יְקָרָךְ יְיָ עַל טַוְורָא דְסִינַי וְקַבִּילוּ תַמָן אוֹרַיְיתָךְ וַעֲנָנָא מַטְלוּל עִילַוֵיהוֹן דְלָא יְהַנְזְקוּן מִשְׁרָבָא וּמִטְרָא וּבְעַמוּדָא דַעֲנָנָא אַנְתְּ מְדַבֵּר קֳדָמֵיהוֹן בִּימָמָא מְטוֹל לְמַכָּכָא טַוְורַיָא וּגְלִימְתָּא וּלְמִידְלֵי מַשְׁרַיָא וּבְעַמוּדָא דְאֵישָׁתָא לְאַנְהָרָא בְּלֵילְיָא


and will say with exultation to the people of this land, who have heard that Thou art the Lord, whose Shekinah dwelleth among this people, in whose eyes, O Lord, the Glory of Thy Shekinah appeared on the mountain of Sinai, and who there received Thy law; Thy Cloud hath shadowed, that neither heat nor rain might hurt them; and whom in the pillar of the Cloud Thou hast led on by day, that the mountains and hills might be brought low, and the valleys lifted up, and hast guided in the pillar of Fire by night:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:15)


וּמִן בָּתַר כָּל נִסַיָא הָאִילֵן אַנְתְּ קָטֵיל יַת עַמָא הָדֵין כְּגַבְרָא חָד וְיֵימְרוּן עַמְמַיָא דְיִשְׁמְעוּ יַת שֵׁמַע גְבוּרְתָּךְ לְמֵימָר


and after all of these miracles wilt Thou kill this people as one man? Then the nations who have heard the fame and of Thy power will speak saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:16)


מִן בִּגְלַל דְלָא הֲוָת יוּכְלָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ לְאַעֲלָאָה יַת עַמָא הָדֵין לְאַרְעָא דְקַיֵים לְהוֹן וּקְטָלִינוּן בְּמַדְבְּרָא


Because there was no (more) strength with the Lord to bring this people into the land which swearing He a promised to them, He hath killed them in the wilderness!


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:17)


וּכְדוּן יַסְגֵי כְדוּן חֵילָא קֳדָמָךְ יְיָ וְתִתְמְלֵי רַחֲמִין עֲלֵיהוֹן וְיָתִי תְשַׁוֵי לְעַם רַב הֵיכְמָא דְמַלֵילְתָּא לְמֵימָר


And now, I beseech Thee, magnify Thy power, O Lord, and let mercies be fulfilled upon us, and appoint me for (this) great people, as Thou hast spoken, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:18)


יְיָ אֲרִיךְ רוּחַ וְקָרִיב רַחֲמִין שְׁרֵי לְתוּבִין וּמְכַפֵּר עַל סוּרְחָנוּן מַזְכֵּי לִדְתַיְיבִין לְאוֹרַיְיתָא וְלִדְלָא תַיְיבִין לָא מְזַכֵּי מַסְעַר חוֹבֵי אַבְהָן רַשִׁיעִין עַל בְּנִין מְרוֹדִין עַל דַר תְּלִיתָאֵי וְעַל דַר רְבִיעָאֵי


The Lord is long­ suffering, and nigh in mercy, forgiving sins and covering transgressions, justifying such as return to His law though them who turn not He will not absolve, but will visit the sins of wicked fathers upon rebellious children unto the third and fourth generation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:19)


שְׁבוֹק כְּדוֹן לְסוּרְחָנוּת עַמָא הָדֵין כִּסְגִיאוּת טֵבְוָותָךְ וְהֵיכְמָא דְשָׁרִיתָא לְעַמָא הָדֵין מִן זְמַן דִנְפָקוּ מִמִצְרַיִם וְעַד כְּדוֹן


Pardon now the sin of this people according to Thy great goodness, even as Thou hast forgiven them from the time that they came out from Mizraim until now.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:20)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ שַׁבְקִית כְּפִתְגָמָךְ


And the Lord said, I have forgiven, according to thy word.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:21)


וּבְרַם בִּשְׁבוּעָה קַיֵים אֲנָא וּמַלְיָא יְקָרָא דַיְיָ יַת כָּל אַרְעָא


Nevertheless, by oath have I sworn that the whole earth shall be filled with the glory of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:22)


אֲרוּם כָּל גוּבְרַיָא דִי חָמוּן יַת יְקָרִי וְיַת אַתְוָותַי דַעֲבָדִית בְמִצְרַיִם וּבְמַדְבְּרָא וְנַסִיאוּ קֳדָמַי דְנַן עֲשַר זִמְנִין וְלָא קַבִּילוּ לְמֵימְרִי


Because all the men who have beheld My glory, and My signs, which I have wrought in Mizraim and in the Desert, have tempted Me now ten times, and have not obeyed (received) My Word:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:23)


בִּשְׁבוּעָא אֲמִירָא דָא דְלָא יֶחֱמוּן יַת אַרְעָא דְקַיֵימִית לְאַבְהַתְהוֹן וְלָא דָרָא דְאַרְגִיזוּ קֳדָמַי לָא יֶחְמוּנָהּ


by oath have I said this, That they shall not see the land which I covenanted to their fathers; and the generation which have been provokeful before me shall not behold it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:24)


וְעַבְדִי כָלֵב חוּלַף דַהֲוַות רוּחַ אוֹחֲרֵי עִמֵיהּ וְאַשְׁלִים בָּתַר דַחַלְתִּי וְאַעֲלִינֵיהּ לְאַרְעָא דְעָאל תַּמָן וּבְנוֹי יִרְתוּנָהּ


But My servant, Kaleb, because there is in him another spirit, and he hath entirely followed (in) My fear, him will I bring into the land to which he went, and his children shall possess it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:25)


וַעֲמַלְקָאֵי וּכְנַעֲנָאֵי יָתֵיב בְּמֵישְׁרָא מְחַר אִתְפְּנוּ וְטוּלוּ לְכוֹן לְמַדְבְּרָא אוֹרַח יַמָא דְסוּף


But the Amalekites and Kenaanites dwell in the valley: tomorrow turn you and go into the wilderness by the way of the Red Sea.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:26)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה וְעִם אַהֲרן לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh and Aharon, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:27)


עַד אֵימַת לִכְנִישְׁתָּא בִישָׁתָא דְמִתְחַבְּרִין עָלַי יַת תּוּרְעֲמוּת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל דְהִינוּן מִתְרַעֲמִין עָלַי שְׁמִיעַ קֳדָמָי


How long (shall I bear with) this evil congregation who gather together against Me? The murmurs of the sons of Israel which they murmur against Me are heard before Me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:28)


אֵימַר לְהוֹן בִּשְׁבוּעָא קְיַים אֲנָא אִין לָא הֵיכְמָא דְמַלֵילְתּוּן קֳדָמַי הֵיכְדֵין אַעֲבֵיד לְכוֹן


Say to them, By oath I decree that according to (what) you have spoken, so will I do to you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:29)


בְּמַדְבְּרָא הָדֵין יִתְרְמוּן גוּשְׁמֵיכוֹן כָּל סְכוּמְכוֹן לְכָל חוּשְׁבַּנְכוֹן מִבַּר עַשְרִין שְׁנִין וּלְעֵילָא דְאִתְרַעַמְתּוּן עָלַי


In this wilderness your carcasses shall fall, the whole number of all who were counted from twenty years old and upward, who have murmured against Me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:30)


בִּשְׁבוּעָא חֲמִירָא דְאַתּוּן לָא תֵיעֲלוּן לְאַרְעָא דְקַיֵימִית בְּמֵימְרִי לְאַשְׁרָאָה יַתְכוֹן בָּהּ אֲרוּם אֱלָהֵן כָּלֵב בַּר יְפוּנֶה וִיהוֹשֻׁעַ בַּר נוּן


By a fast oath (have I sworn) that you shall not enter into the land which I covenanted in My Word to give you to inhabit, except Kaleb bar Jephunneh and Jehoshua bar Nun.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:31)


וְטַפְלְכוֹן דַאֲמַרְתּוּן דִלְבִיזָא יְהוֹן וְאָעֵיל יַתְהוֹן וְיִנְדְעוּן יַת אַרְעָא דְקַצְתּוּן בָּהּ


But your children, who you said would be a prey, them will I bring in, and they shall know the land which you rejected;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:32)


וּפִגְרֵיכוֹן דִלְכוֹן יִתְרְמוּן בְּמַדְבְּרָא הָדֵין


but your carcasses shall fall in this wilderness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:33)


וּבְנֵיכוֹן יְהוֹן טַעֲיָין בְּמַדְבְּרָא אַרְבְּעִין שְׁנִין וִיקַבְּלוּן יַת חוֹבֵיכוֹן עַד זְמַן דִיסוּפוּן פִּגְרֵיכוֹן בְּמַדְבְּרָא


Yet your children will have to wander in this wilderness forty years, and bear your sins until the time that your carcasses are consumed in the wilderness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:34)


בְּמִנְיַן יוֹמִין דְאַלֵילְתּוּן יַת אַרְעָא אַרְבְּעִין יוֹמִין יוֹמָא לְשַׁתָּא יוֹמָא לְשַׁתָּא תְּקַבְּלוּן יַת חוֹבֵיכוֹן אַרְבְּעִין שְׁנִין וְתִנְדְעוּן יַת דְאִתְרַעַמְתּוּן עָלָי


According to the number of the days in which you were exploring the land, forty days, a day for a year, a day for a year, you shall receive for your sins, forty years, and shall know (the consequence) of your murmuring against Me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:35)


אֲנָא יְיָ גְזָרִית בְּמֵימְרִי אִין לָא גְזָרִית בְּמֵימְרִי לְכָל כְּנִישְׁתָּא בִישָׁתָא הֲדָא דְאִזְדַמְנוּ לְמַרְדָא עָלַי בְּמַדְבְּרָא הָדֵין יְסוּפוּן וְתַמָן יְמוּתוּן


I the Lord have decreed in My Word, - if I have not made a decree in My Word against all this evil congregation who have gathered a rebel against Me in this wilderness, that they shall be consumed and die there.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:36)


וְגוּבְרַיָא דִי שְׁדַר משֶׁה לְאַלְלָא יַת אַרְעָא וְתָבוּ וְאַרְעִימוּ עֲלוֹהִי יַת כָּל כְּנִישְׁתָּא לְאַפָּקָא טִיב בִּישׁ עַל אַרְעָא


But the men whom Mosheh had sent to explore the land, and who returning had made the whole congregation murmur against him, by bringing forth an evil report of the land,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:37)


וּמִיתוּ גוּבְרַיָא דְאַפִּיקוּ טִיב בִּישׁ עַל אַרְעָא בְשִׁבְעָא יוֹמִין בְּאֵילוּל וַהֲווֹן מוֹרָנֵי נַפְקִין מִן פְּרַתְהוֹן וְאָזְלִין עַד בֵּית לִישַׁנְהוֹן וְאָכְלִין לִישַׁנְהוֹן עִם מוֹרִיגֵיהוֹן וְאִתְקַבָּרוּ בְּמוֹתָנָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ


(even those) men who had brought forth the evil of the report of the land died, on the seventh day of the month of Elul, with worms coming from their navels, and with worms devouring their tongues; and were buried in death from before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:38)


וִיהוֹשֻׁעַ בַּר נוּן וְכָלֵב בַּר יְפֻנֶה אִתְקַיְימוּ מִן גוּבְרַיָא הָאִינוּן דְהַלִיכוּ לְאַלָלָא יַת אַרְעָא


Only Jehoshua bar Nun and Kaleb bar Jephunneh survived of those men who had gone to explore the land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:39)


וּמַלֵיל משֶׁה יַת פִּתְגָמַיָא הָאִילֵן עִם כָּל בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְאִתְאַבְּלוּ עַמָא לַחֲדָא


And Mosheh spake these words with all the sons of Israel, and the people mourned greatly.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:40)


וְאַקְדִימוּ בְּצַפְרָא וּסְלִיקוּ לְרֵישׁ טַוְורָא לְמֵימַר הָא אֲנַחְנָא סַלְקִין לְאַתְרָא דְאָמַר יְיָ אֲרוּם חַבְנָא


And they arose in the morning and went up to the top of the mountain., saying: Behold, we will go up to the place of which the Lord hath spoken; for we have sinned.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:41)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה מַה דֵין אַתּוּן עַבְרִין עַל גְזֵירַת מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ וְאִיהִי לָא תִצְלַח לְכוֹן


But Mosheh said, Why will you act against the decree of the Word of the Lord? But it will not prosper with you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:42)


לָא תִסְקוּן אֲרוּם לֵית שְׁכִינְתָּא דַיְיָ שַׁרְיָא בֵּינֵיכוֹן וַאֲרוֹנָא וּמַשְׁכְּנָא וַעֲנַנֵי יְקָרָא לֵיתֵיהוֹן מְטַיְילִין וְלָא תִתְבְּרוּן קֳדָם בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבֵיכוֹן


Go not up, for the Lord's Shekinah dwelleth not among you; and the ark, the tabernacle, and the Cloud of Glory proceed not; and be not crushed before your enemies.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:43)


אֲרוּם עֲמַלְקָאֵי וּכְנַעֲנָאֵי תַּמָן זְמִינִין קֳדָמֵיכוֹן וְתִתְרְמוּן קְטִילִין בְּחַרְבָּא אֲרוּם מִן בִּגְלַל דְחַבְתּוּן מִן בָּתַר פּוּלְחָנָא דַיְיָ בְּגִין כֵּן לָא יְהֵי מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ בְּסַעַדְכוֹן


For the Amalekites and Kenaanites are there prepared for you, and you will fall slaughtered by the sword. For, because you have turned away from the service of the Lord, the Word of the Lord will not be your Helper.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:44)


וְאִזְדְרָזוּ בַּחֲשֵׁיכָא קֳדָם קְרִיצְתָּא לְמֵיסוֹק לְרֵישׁ טוּרָא בְּרַם אֲרוֹנָא דְבֵיהּ קְיָימָא דַיְיָ וּמשֶׁה לָא זָזוּ מִגוֹ מַשְׁרִיתָא


But they armed themselves in the dark before the morning to go up to the height of the mountain: but the ark, in which was the covenant of the Lord, and Mosheh, stirred not from the midst of the camp.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 14:45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/14:45)


וּנְחַת עֲמַלְקָאָה וּכְנַעֲנָאָה דְיָתֵיב בְּטַוְורָא הַהוּא וּקְטִילוּ יַתְהוֹן וְשֵׁיצֵיאוּנוּן וּטְרָדוּנוּן עַד שֵׁיצָיוּ


And the Amalekites and Kenaanites who dwelt in that mountain came down and slaughtered and destroyed them, and drave them hard to destruction.



## Chapter 15

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:1)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:2)


מַלֵיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן אֲרוּם תֵּיעָלוּן לְאַרַע מוֹתְבָנֵיכוֹן דַאֲנָא יָהֵיב לְכוֹן


Speak with the sons of Israel, and say to them: When you have entered into the land of your habitation which I will give you,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:3)


וְתַעַבְּדוּן עַל מַדְבְּחָא קוּרְבָּנָא קֳדָם יְיָ עֲלָתָא אוֹ נִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָא לְפַרְשָׁא נִדְרָא אוֹ בִּנְדִיבוּתָא אוֹ בִּזְמַן מוֹעֲדֵיכוֹן לְמֶעֱבַּד רַעֲוָותָא דְמָרֵי עַלְמָא לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְּרַעֲוָא קֳדָם יְיָ מִן תּוֹרֵי אוֹ מִן עָנָא


and you may make an oblation upon the altar before the Lord, burnt offering or consecrated sacrifice for release of a vow, or by free-will offering; or at the time of your feasts you offer what is acceptable to the Lord of the world, to be received with approval before the Lord from the herd or from the flock:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:4)


וִיקָרֵיב גַבְרָא דִמְקָרֵב קוּרְבָּנֵיהּ קֳדָם יְיָ מִנְחָתָא סְמִידָא עֶשְרוֹנָא פְּתִיכָא בְּרַבְעוּת הִינָא מְשַׁח זֵיתָא


let the man who offers his oblation before the Lord bring a mincha of a tenth of flour mingled with the fourth of a hina of olive oil;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:5)


וַחֲמַר עִינְבָא לְנִיסוּכָא רַבְעוּת הִינָא דְעָבַד עַל עֲלָתָא אוֹ לְנִכְסַת קוּדְשָׁא לְאִימַר חָד


and wine of grapes for a libation, the fourth of a hina, to be made upon the burnt offering or hallowed sacrifice-for one lamb.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:6)


אוֹ לְדִכְרָא תַעֲבֵּיד מִנְחָתָא תְּרֵין עֶשְרוֹנִין סְמִידָא פְּתִיכָא בִּמְשַׁח זֵיתָא תַּלְתוּת הִינָא


Or for a ram, let him perform a mincha of two tenths of flour mingled with the third of a hin of olive oil,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:7)


וַחֲמַר עִינְבָא תַּלְתוּת הִינָא תַּקְרִיב בְּסִפְלֵי לְנִיסוּכָא מְטוֹל לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְּרַעֲוָא קֳדָם יְיָ


and wine of grapes let him offer in a vase for the libation, the third of a hin, to be received with acceptance before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:8)


וַאֲרוּם תַּעֲבֵּיד בַּר תּוֹרֵי עֲלָתָא אוֹ נִכְסְתָא לְפַרְשָׁא נִדְרָא אוֹ נִכְסַת קוּדְשַׁיָא קֳדָם יְיָ


But when he maketh a bullock a burnt offering, or a sacrifice for release from a vow, or a hallowed sacrifice before the Lord,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:9)


וִיקָרֵב עַל בַּר תּוֹרֵי מִנְחָתָא סְמִידָא תְּלָתָא עֶשְרוֹנִין פָּתִיךְ בִּמְשַׁח זֵיתָא פַּלְגוּת הִינָא


let him bring for the bullock a mincha of three tenths of flour mixed with half of a hin of olive oil,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:10)


וַחֲמַר עִינְבָא לְנִיסוּכָא פַּלְגוּת הִינָא קוּרְבַּן דְמִתְקַבֵּל בְּרַעֲוָא קֳדָם יְיָ


and wine of grapes half a hin, for a libation to be received with acceptance before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:11)


הֵיכְדֵין יִתְעַבֵּד לְתוֹרָא חָד אוֹ לְדִיכְרָא חָד אוֹ לְאִמְרֵי בַּר אִמְרֵי אוֹ בְנֵי גַדְיָא


So let him do with each bullock, with each ram, and each lamb, whether it be from the lambs or the kids:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:12)


כְּחוּשְׁבַּן תּוֹרֵי וְאִמְרֵי וְגַדְיֵי דְתַעַבְּדוּן קָרְבָּנָא הֵיכְדֵין תַּעַבְּדוּן לְכָל חַד וְחַד לְפוּם סְכוּמְהוֹן


according to the number of the bullocks or lambs or goats with which the oblation is made so shall you do, each according to their number.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:13)


כָּל יַצִיבַיָא בְּיִשְרָאֵל וְלָא בְבַר עַמְמִין יַעֲבֵיד הֵיכְדֵין יַת אִלֵין נְסוּכְיָיא לְקָרָבָא קוּרְבָּנָא דְמִתְקַבֵּיל בְּרַעֲוָא קֳדָם יְיָ


All who are native born in Israel, and not of the sons of the Gentiles, shall so make these libations in offering an oblation to be received with acceptance before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:14)


וַאֲרוּם יִתְגַיֵיר עִמְכוֹן גִיוֹרָא אוֹ מַן דְהוּא כְּדוֹן בֵּינֵיכוֹן לְדָרֵיכוֹן וְיֶעֱבַד קוּרְבַּן דְמִתְקַבֵּל בְּרַעֲוָא קֳדָם יְיָ הֵיכְמָא דְתַעַבְדוּן כֵּן יַעֲבֵד


And when a sojourner who sojourneth with you, or whoever is among you now, or in your generations, will bring an oblation to be received with favour before the Lord, as you do so shall he.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:15)


קְהָלָא כּוֹלָא קְיָימָא חֲדָא לְכוֹן וּלְגִיוֹרָא דִי יִתְגַיְירוּן קְיַים עֲלָם לְדָרֵיכוֹן כְּוַתְכוֹן כְּגִיוֹרָא יְהֵי קֳדָם יְיָ


For the whole congregation there is one statute, for you and the sojourner who sojourneth; it is an everlasting statute for your generations; as with you, so shall it be with the sojourner before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:16)


אוֹרַיְיתָא חֲדָא וְדִינָא חַד יְהֵי לְכוֹן וּלְגִיוֹרָא דִי יִתְגַיְירוּן עִמְכוֹן


One law and one judgment shall be for you and for the sojourner who sojourneth with you.

And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:

מַלֵיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן בְּמֵיעַלְכוֹן לְאַרְעָא דַאֲנָא מָעֵיל יַתְכוֹן לְתַמָן


Speak with the sons of Israel, and say to them: When you have entered the land into which I will bring you,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:19)


וִיהֵי בְּמֵיכְלְכוֹן מִלַחְמָא דְעַלַלְתָּא דְאַרְעָא וְלָא מִן אוֹרִיזָא וְדוֹחִינָא וְקִיטְנֵי תִּפְרְשׁוּן אַפְרָשׁוּתָא קֳדָם יְיָ


and you eat the bread of the produce of it, (not rice, nor millet, nor pulse,)


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:20)


שֵׁירוּי אַצְוַותְכוֹן חַלְתָא חַד מִן עֶשְרִין וְאַרְבָּעָה תִּפְרְשׁוּן אַפְרָשׁוּתָא לְכַהֲנָא כְּמָא דִמְפָרְשִׁין מִן אִדְרָא הֵיכְדֵין תִּפְרְשׁוּן יָתָהּ


you shall set apart a separation before the Lord. Of the first of your dough one cake of twenty-four you shall set apart as a separation for the priest; as with the separation from the threshing floor, so shall you set it apart.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:21)

Of the first of your dough you shall give a separation before the Lord in your generations.

whatsoever the Lord hath commanded you by Mosheh from the day He commanded it, and thenceforth unto your generations -

וִיהֵי אִין מִן מֶחְמֵי כְנִשְׁתָּא אִיתְעֲבִידַת סוּרְחָנוּתָא בְּשָׁלוּ וְיַעַבְדוּן כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא תּוֹר בַּר תּוֹרֵי חַד לְעָלָתָא לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְּרַעֲוָא קֳדָם יְיָ וּמִנְחָתֵיהּ וְנִיסוּכֵיהּ כַּד חָמֵי וּצְפִיר בַּר עִזִין דְלָא עֵירוּבִין חַד לְחַטָאתָא


if without the knowledge of the congregation sin hath been committed through ignorance, let all the congregation make one young bullock a burnt offering to be received with acceptance before the Lord, with his mincha and libation. as are proper; and one kid of the goats without mixture for a sin offering;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:25)


וִיכַפֵּר כַּהֲנָא עַל כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְיִשְׁתְּרֵי לְהוֹן אֲרוּם שְׁלוּתָא הִיא וְהִינוּן הַיְיתִיאוּ יַת קוּרְבָּנֵיהוֹן קָרְבָּנָא קֳדָם יְיָ וְקָרְבַּן סוּרְחָנוּתְהוֹן קְרִיבוּ קֳדָם יְיָ עַל שְׁלוּתְהוֹן


and let the priest make atonement for all the congregation of the sons of Israel, and it shall be forgiven them; for it was an error, and they have brought their oblation, an offering before the Lord, even an offering for their sin have they presented before the Lord for their error;

and all the congregation of Israel shall be forgiven before the Lord, and the sojourners who sojourn among them; for an error hath occurred to the people.

And if any one man sin through ignorance, let him bring one goat of the year without mixture for a sin offering,

וִיכַפֵּר כַּהֲנָא עַל בַּר נְשָׁא דְאִשְׁתְּלֵי כַּד סָרַח בְּשָׁלוּ קֳדָם יְיָ לְכַפָּרָא עֲלוֹי וְיִשְׁתְּרֵי לֵיהּ


and let the priest make atonement for the man who hath erred in sinning through ignorance before the Lord to atone for him, that it may be forgiven him;

יַצִיבָא בִּבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וּלְגִיוֹרֵי דְמִתְגַיְירִין בֵּינֵיכוֹן (א"ה צ"ל בֵּינֵיהוֹן) אוֹרַיְיתָא חֲדָא יְהֵי לְכוֹן וּלְמַאן דְיֶעֱבַד בְּשָׁלוּ


as well for the native-born of the children of Israel, and for the strangers who sojourn among you, there shall be one law for him who transgresseth through ignorance:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:30)


וּבַר נַשׁ דְיֶעֱבַד בִּזְדָנָא מִן יַצִיבַיָא אוֹ מִן גִיוֹרַיָא וְלָא תָאִיב מִן סוּרְחָנוּתֵיהּ קֳדָם יְיָ הֲוָה מַרְגִיז וְיִשְׁתֵּיצֵי בַּר נְשָׁא הַהוּא מִגוֹ עַמֵיהּ


but a man who transgresseth. with presumption, whether of the native-born or strangers, and who turneth. not away from his sin before the Lord, - he causeth anger, and that man shall perish from among his people;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:31)


אֲרוּם עַל פִּתְגָמָא קַדְמָאָה דְפַקֵיד יְיָ בְּסִינַי בָּסַר וְיַת תַּפְקִידַת מְהוּלְתָּא בָּטִיל אִישְׁתֵּיצָאָה בְּעַלְמָא הָדֵין יִשְׁתֵּיצֵי בַר נְשָׁא הַהוּא בְּעַלְמָא דְאָתֵי דְעָתִיד לְמִתַּן חוּשְׁבַּן חוֹבָתֵיהּ לְיוֹם דִינָא רַבָּא


for, the primal word which the Lord commanded on Sinai he hath despised, and hath made the commandment of circumcision vain; with destruction in this world shall that man be destroyed; in the world that cometh shall he give account of his sin at the great day of judgment.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:32)


וַהֲווֹ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל שַׁרְיַין בְּמַדְבְּרָא גְזֵירַת שַׁבְּתָא אִשְׁתְּמוֹדַע לְהוֹן בְּרַם קְנָסָא דְשַׁבְּתָא לָא אִשְׁתְּמוֹדַע לְהוֹן קָם גַבְרָא מִדְבֵית יוֹסֵף אָמַר בְּמֵימְרֵיהּ אִיזִיל וְאֶתְּלוֹשׁ קִיסִין בְּיוֹמָא דְשַׁבְּתָא וְיֶחֱמוּן יָתִי סַהֲדַיָא וִיתַנוּן לְמשֶׁה וּמשֶׁה יִתְבַּע אוּלְפַן מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וְיִדוֹן יָתִי וּבְכֵן אִשְׁתְּמוֹדַע קְנָסָא לְכָל בֵּית יִשְרָאֵל וְאַשְׁכָּחוּ סַהֲדָא יַת גַבְרָא כַּד תָּלִישׁ וְעָקַר קִיסִין בְּיוֹמָא דְשַׁבְּתָא


And while the sons of Israel were dwelling in the wilderness, the decree of the Sabbath was known to them, but the punishment (for the profanation) of the Sabbath was not known. And there arose a man of the house of Joseph, and said with himself: I will go and pull up wood on the Sabbath day; and witnesses saw it, and told Mosheh; and Mosheh sought instruction from the presence of the Lord, that he might teach me judgment, and make known the discipline of all the house of Israel. And the witnesses of the man who pulled up and collected wood came,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:33)


וְקָרִיבוּ יָתֵיהּ בָּתַר דְאַתְרוּ בֵּיהּ וְתָּלַשׁ סַהֲדַיָא דְהַשְׁכָּחוּ יָתֵיהּ תָּלִישׁ קִיסִין לְוַת משֶׁה וּלְוַת אַהֲרן וּלְוַת כָּל כְּנִישְׁתָּא


and, after they had monished him, and he had wounded the witnesses who had found him pulling up wood, brought him to Mosheh and Aharon, and all the congregation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:34)


דֵין הוּא חַד מִן אַרְבָּעָה דִינִין דִי עָלוּ קֳדָם משֶׁה נְבִיָא וְדָן יַתְהוֹן עַל פּוּם מֵימְרָא דְקוּדְשָׁא מִנְהוֹן דִינֵי מָמוֹנָא וּמִנְהוֹן דִינֵי נַפְשָׁתָא בְּדִינֵי מָמוֹנָא הֲוָה משֶׁה זָרִיז וּבְדִינֵי נַפְשָׁתָא הֲוָה משֶׁה מָתוּן וּבְאִלְיֵין וּבְאִלְיֵין אָמַר משֶׁה לָא שַׁמְעִית מִן בִּגְלַל לְמַלְפָא רֵישֵׁי סַנְהֶדְרֵי דַעֲתִידִין לְמֵיקוּם דִיהֱווֹן זְרִיזִין בְּדִינֵי מָמוֹנָא וּמְתוּנִין בְּדִינֵי נַפְשָׁתָא וְלָא יְבַהְתוּן לִמְשַׁיְילָא דִינָא דְמִתְקַשֵׁי לְהוֹן אֲרוּם משֶׁה דַהֲוָה רַבְּהוֹן דְיִשְרָאֵל צָרִיךְ דְיֵימַר לָא שְׁמָעִית בְּגִין כֵּן אַצְנָעוּהִי בְּבֵית אֲסִירֵי אֲרוּם עַד כְּדוּן לָא אִתְפְּרַשׁ הָדֵין דִינָא יִתְעֲבֵיד בֵּיהּ


This is one of four judgments which were brought before Mosheh the prophet, which he adjudged according to the Word of the Holy. Of these judgments some related to money, and some to life. In the judgments regarding money Mosheh was prompt, but in those affecting life he was deliberate, and in each he said, I have not heard, - to teach the princes of the future Sanhedrin to be prompt in decisions on mammon, and deliberate in those that involved life, nor to be ashamed to inquire for counsel in what may be difficult, forasmuch as Mosheh the Rabbi of Israel himself had need to say, I have not heard. Therefore put they him in confinement, because they had not yet heard the explanation of the judgment they should execute upon him.

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:38)


מַלֵיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן וְיַעַבְדִין לְהוֹן צִיצְיָיתָא לָא מִן נִימַיָא וְלָא מִן סִיסַיָא וְלָא מִן גַרְדַיָא אֱלָהֵן לְשׁוּמְהוֹן יַעַבְדוּנוּן וְיִפְסְקוּן רֵישֵׁי חוּטֵיהוֹן וְיִתְּלוּן בַּחֲמֵשֶׁת קִיטְרִין אַרְבָּעָה בְּגוֹ תְלָתָא עַל אַרְבַּעַת אַנְפֵּי גוּלֵיהוֹן דְמִתְעַטְפִין בְּהוֹן לְדָרֵיהוֹן וְיִתְּנוּן עַל אַנְפָּא גוּלְיַתְהוֹן שְׁזִיר דְתִכְלָא


Speak with the sons of Israel, and bid them make for themselves fringes, not of threads, nor of yarns, nor of fibres, but after a manner of their own (lesumhon) shall they make them, and shall cut off the heads of their filaments, and suspend by five ligatures, four in the midst of three, upon the four corners of their garment in which they enwrap themselves, unto their generations; and they shall put upon the edge of their robes an embroidery of hyacinth (shezir de-thikela).


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:39)


וִיהֵי לְמִצְוָתָא דְצִיצְיַת וְתֶחֱמוּן יָתֵיהּ בִּזְמַן דְאַתּוּן מִתְעַטְפִין בְּהוֹן בִּימָמָא וְתִדְכְּרוּן יַת כָּל פִּקוּדַי וְתַעַבְדוּן יַתְהוֹן וְלָא תִסְטוּן לְמִטְעֵי בָּתַר הִרְהוּר לִבְּכוֹן וּבָתַר חֵיזוּ עֵינֵיכוֹן דְאַתּוּן טָעַן בַּתְרֵיהוֹן


And this shall be to you a precept for fringes, that you may look upon them at the time when you dress yourselves daily, and remember all My commandments to do them, and not go aside to wander after the imaginations of your heart and the sight of your eyes, after which you have gone astray.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:40)


מִן בִּגְלַל דְתִדְכְּרוּן וְתַעַבְדוּן יַת כָּל פִּקוּדַי וְתֶהֱווֹן קַדִישִׁין הֵי כְמַלְאָכַיָא דִמְשַׁמְשִׁין קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן


To the end that you may remember and perform all My precepts, and be holy, like the angels who minister before the Lord your God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 15:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/15:41)


אֲנָא הוּא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן דִי פְרָקִית וְאַפֵּקִית יַתְכוֹן פְּרִיקִין מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם מְטוֹל לְמֶהֱוֵי לְכוֹן לֶאֱלָהָא אֲנָא הוּא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן


I am the Lord your God who have delivered and brought you free out of the land of Mizraim, to be to you Eloha. I am the Lord your God.



## Chapter 16

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:1)


ויקח קרח וּנְסֵיב גוּלְיָיתֵיהּ דְכוּלָא תִּיכְלָא קרַח בַּר יִצְהָר בַּר קְהָת בַּר לֵוִי וְדָתָן וַאֲבִירָם בְּנֵי אֱלִיאָב וְאוֹן בַּר פֶּלֶת בְּנֵי רְאוּבֵן


But Korach bar Tizhar bar Kehath, bar Levi, with Dathan and Abiram the sons of Eliab, and On bar Peleth, of the Beni-Reuben, took his robe which was all of hyacinth,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:2)


וְקָמוּ בְּחוּצְפָּא וְאוֹרוּ הִילְכְתָא בְּאַנְפּוֹי דְמשֶׁה עַל עֵיסַק תִּיכְלָא משֶׁה אָמַר אֲנָא שְׁמָעִית מִן פּוּם קוּדְשָׁא יְהֵא שְׁמֵיהּ מְבָרַךְ דְצִיצִיַית יְהוֹן מִן חִיוַור וְחוּטָא חַד דְתִיכְלָא יְהֵי בֵּיהּ קרַח וְחַבְרוֹי עָבָדוּ גוּלְיַין וְצִיצְיַיתְהוֹן כּוּלְהוֹן דְתִיכְלָא מַה דְלָא פַקֵיד יְיָ וּמְסַעֲדִין לְהוֹן גוּבְרַיָא מִבְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מָאתָן וְחַמְשִׁין אֲמַרְכְּלֵי כְּנִישְׁתָּא מְעַרְעֵי זְמַן לְמֵיטַל וּלְמֵישְׁרֵי מְפַרְשִׁין בִּשְׁמָהָן


and rose up boldly, and in the face of Mosheh appointed a (different) observance in the matter of the hyacinth. Mosheh had said, I have heard from the mouth of the Holy One, whose Name be Blessed, that the fringes are to be of white, with one filament of hyacinth; O but Korach and his companions made garments with their fringes altogether of hyacinth, which the Lord had not commanded; and two hundred and fifty men of the sons of Israel, who had been made leaders of the congregation at the time when the journeys and encampments were appointed, by expression of their names, supported him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:3)


וְאִתְכַּנָשׁוּ עַל משֶׁה וְעַל אַהֲרן וַאֲמָרוּ לְהוֹן סַגִי לְכוֹן רַבָּנוּתָא אֲרוּם כָּל כְּנִישְׁתָּא כּוּלְהוֹן קַדִישִׁין וּבֵינֵיהוֹן שַׁרְיָא שְׁכִינְתָּא דַיְיָ וּמַה דֵין אַתּוּן מִתְרַבְרְבִין עַל קְהָלָא דַיְיָ


And they gathered together against Mosheh and Aharon, and said to them: Let the authority you have (hitherto had) suffice you, for all the congregation are holy, and the Lord's Shekinah dwelleth among them; and why should you be magnified over the church of the Lord?


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:4)


וּשְׁמַע משֶׁה הֵיךְ קַנְיַין כָּל חַד מִנְהוֹן יַת אִנְתְּתֵיהּ לְאַשְׁקָיוּתְהֶן מַיָא בְּדוֹקַיָא מְטוֹל משֶׁה וּנְפַל עַל אַנְפּוֹי מִן כִּיסוּפָא


And Mosheh heard, as if every one of them was jealous of his wife, and would have them drink of the trial-water on account of Mosheh; and he fell on his face for shame.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:5)


וּמַלֵיל עִם קרַח וְעִם כְּנִישַׁת סַעֲדוֹי לְמֵימָר צַפְרָא וִיהוֹדַע יְיָ יַת דְכָשַׁר לֵיהּ וְיַת דְקַדִישׁ וְיִקְרַב לְפוּלְחָנֵיהּ וְיַת דְיִתְרְעֵי בֵיהּ יִקְרַב לְשַׁמוּשֵׁיהּ


And he spake with Korach and all the company who supported him, saying: In the morning the Lord will make known him whom He hath approved, and hath consecrated to approach unto His service, and who it hath pleased Him should come nigh in ministering, unto Him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:6)


דָא עִיבִידוּ סָבוּ לְכוֹן מַחְתְּיַין קרַח וְכָל כְּנִישַׁת סַעֲדוֹי


Do this: Let Korach and all the company of his helpers take censers,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:7)


וְהָבוּ בְהוֹן אֵישָׁתָא וְשַׁווּ עֲלֵיהוֹן קְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמִין קֳדָם יְיָ מְחַר וִיהֵי גַבְרָא דְיִתְרְעֵי בֵיהּ יְיָ הוּא דְקָדִישׁ סַגִי לְכוֹן בְּנֵי לֵוִי


put fire in them, and lay incense upon them before the Lord, tomorrow; and the man whom the Lord shall make known, he it is who is consecrated. Let it suffice to you, sons of Levi.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:8)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְקרַח וְלִגְנִיסָתֵיהּ קַבִּילוּ כְעַן בְּנֵי לֵוִי


And Mosheh said to Korach and his kindred: Hear now, ye sons of Levi:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:9)


הַזְעֵירָא הִיא לְכוֹן אֲרוּם אַפְרֵישׁ אֱלָהָא דְיִשְרָאֵל יַתְכוֹן מִן כְּנִישְׁתָּא דְיִשְרָאֵל לְקָרָבָא יַתְכוֹן לְשִׁימוּשֵׁיהּ לְמִפְלַח יַת פּוּלְחַן מַשְׁכְּנָא דַיְיָ וּלְמֵיקוּם קֳדָם כְּנִישְׁתָּא לְשַׁמְשׁוּתְהוֹן


Is it too little For you that the God of Israel hath set you apart from the congregation of Israel to draw near to do His service to fulfil the ministry of the Lord's tabernacle, and to stand before the congregation to minister to them?


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:10)


וְקָרֵיב יָתָךְ וְיַת כָּל אָחָךְ בְּנֵי לֵוִי עִמָךְ וּכְדוּן אַתּוּן תַּבְעִין אוּף כְּהוּנְתָּא רַבְּתָא


But so hath he brought nigh thee and all the sons of Levi with thee and now do ye demand the high-priesthood also?


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:11)


בְּגִין כֵּן אַנְתְּ וְכָל כְּנִישַׁת סַעֲדָךְ דְאִזְדַמַנְתּוּן עַל מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ וְאַהֲרן מַה הוּא אֲרוּם מִתְרַעֲמִין אַתּוּן עֲלוֹי


Therefore art thou and all the company of thy helpers gathered together against the Word of the Lord: and Aharon, what is he, that you murmur against him?


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:12)


וְשָׁדַר משֶׁה פּוּלִין לְזַמָנָא לְבֵי דִינָא רַבָּא לְדָתָן וְלַאֲבִירָם בְּנֵי אֱלִיאָב וַאֲמָרוּ לָא נֵיסוֹק


And Mosheh sent men to summon Dathan and Abiram, the sons of Eliab, to the house of the great judgment; but they said, We will not come up.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:13)


הַזְעֵירָא הוּא אֲרוּם אַסֵיקְתָּנָא מִמִצְרַיִם אַרְעָא דְעַבְדָא חֲלַב וּדְבָשׁ לְקַטְלוּתָנָא בְּמַדְבְּרָא אֲרוּם מִתְרַבְרְבַת עֲלָנָא אוּף אִתְרַבְרְבָא


Is it a little thing, that thou hast brought us from Mizraim, a land that produceth milk and honey, to kill us in the wilderness, that ruling thou mayest domineer over us?


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:14)


בְּרַם לָא לְאַרְעָא עַבְדָא חֲלַב וּדְבָשׁ אַעַלְתָּנָא וִיהַבְתְּ לָנָא אַחְסָנַת חַקְלִין וְכַרְמִין הַעֵינֵיהוֹן דְגוּבְרַיָא הָאִינוּן דִבְאַרְעָא הַהִיא תְּסַנְוַור וּתְנַצֵחַ יַתְהוֹן לָא נֵיסוֹק לְתַמָן


Neither hast thou brought us into the land producing milk and honey to give us an inheritance of fields and vineyards. Wilt thou blind the eyes of the men of that land, that thou mayest overcome them? We shall not go up thither.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:15)


וּתְקֵיף לְמשֶׁה לַחֲדָא וַאֲמַר קֳדָם יְיָ בְּבָעוּ מִינָךְ לָא תִסְתַּכֵּל לְדוֹרוֹן דִידְהוֹן דְלָא חַמְרָא דְחַד מִנְהוֹן שְׁחָרִית וְלָא אַבְאָשִׁית לְחַד מִנְהוֹן


And Mosheh was very wroth, and said before the Lord: I beseech thee, look not upon their offering, the portion of their hands; for not an ass have I taken from one of them, nor to any of them done an injury,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:16)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְקרַח אַנְתְּ וְכָל כְּנִישַׁת סַעֲדָךְ הֲווֹ זְמִינִין לְבֵי דִינָא קֳדָם יְיָ אַנְתְּ וְהִינוּן וְאַהֲרן מְחָר


And Mosheh said to Korach, Thou, and all the company of thy helpers, come together to the house of judgment before the Lord tomorrow, thou, they, and Aharon.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:17)


וְסָבוּ גְבַר מַחְתְּיָתֵיהּ וְתִתְּנוּן עֲלֵיהוֹן קְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמִין וְתִקְרְבוּן קֳדָם יְיָ גְבַר מַחְתְּיָתֵיהּ מָאתָן וְחַמְשִׁין מַחְתְּיַן וְאַנְתְּ וְאַהֲרן גְבַר מַחְתְּיָתֵיהּ


And take every one his censer, and put incense upon them; and let each offer his censer before the Lord, two hundred and fifty censers; thou also, and Aharon, each man his censer.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:18)


וּנְסִיבוּ גְבַר מַחְתְּיָתֵיהּ וִיהָבוּ בְהוֹן אֵישָׁתָא וְשַׁוִיאוּ עֲלֵיהוֹן קְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמִין וְקָמוּ בִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא מִצִיטְרָא חָד וּמשֶׁה וְאַהֲרן מִצִיטְרָא חָד


And they took every one his censer, and put fire in them and sweet incense with it, and stood at the door of the tabernacle of ordinance on one side; but Mosheh and Aharon on the other side.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:19)


וּכְנַשׁ עֲלֵיהוֹן קרַח יַת כָּל כְּנִישְׁתָּא לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְאִתְנַטַל בְּעוּתְרֵיהּ דְאַשְׁכַּח תְּרֵין אוֹצְרִין מִן אוֹצְרוֹי דְיוֹסֵף מַלְיָין כְּסַף וּדְהַב וּבְעָא לְמִטְרַד בְּהַהוּא עוּתְרָא יַת משֶׁה וְיַת אַהֲרן מִן עַלְמָא אִילוּלֵי דְאִתְגְלֵי אִיקָרָא דַיְיָ לְכָל כְּנִישְׁתָּא


And Korach gathered to them the whole congregation at the door of the tabernacle. And he had brought forth, from his riches, two treasures which he had found among the treasures of Joseph filled with silver and gold, and sought with them to drive the riches of Mosheh and Aharon out of the world; but the glory of the Lord revealed itself to all the congregation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:20)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה וְעִם אַהֲרן לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh and Aharon, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:21)


אִתְפְּרָשׁוּ מִגוֹ כְּנִישְׁתָּא הֲדָא וְאֵישֵׁיצֵי יַתְהוֹן כְּשָׁעָה זְעֵירָא


Separate yourselves from among this congregation, that I may destroy them quickly.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:22)


וְאִתְרְכִינוּ בִּצְלוֹ עַל אַפֵּיהוֹן וַאֲמָרוּ אֵל אֱלָהָא דְשַׁוֵי רוּחַ נִשְׁמָתָא בְּגוּפֵי בְּנֵי נְשָׁא וּמִנֵיהּ מִתְיַהֲבָא רוּחָא לְכָל בִּישְרָא הַאִין גַבְרָא חַד יֵיחוֹב וְעַל כָּל כְּנִישְׁתָּא יְהֵי רוּגְזָא


But they bowed down upon their faces in prayer, and said: El Eloha, who hast put the spirit of life in the bodies of the children of men, and from whom is given the spirit of all flesh, - if one man hath sinned, wilt Thou be angry with all the congregation


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:23)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:24)


קַבֵּלִית צְלוֹתְכוֹן עַל כְּנִישְׁתָּא כְּדוֹן מַלֵיל לְהוֹן לְמֵימָר אִסְתַּלָקוּ מִן חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר לְמַשְׁכְּנָא דְקרַח דָתָן וַאֲבִירָם


I have accepted thy prayer for the congregation. Now speak thou with them, saying: Remove away from the tents of Korach, Dathan, and Abiram.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:25)


וְקָם משֶׁה וַאֲזַל לְאוֹכָחָא לְדָתָן וַאֲבִירָם וַאֲזָלוּ בַתְרוֹי סָבֵי יִשְרָאֵל


And Mosheh arose, and went to remonstrate with Dathan and Abiram; and the elders of Israel followed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:26)


וּמַלֵיל לִכְנִשְׁתָּא לְמֵימָר זוֹרוּ כְדֵין מֵעִלַוֵי מַשְׁכְּנֵי גוּבְרַיָא חַיָיבַיָא אִלְיֵין דְאִתְחַיְיבוּ קְטוֹל מִן טַלְיוּתְהוֹן בְּמִצְרַיִם פַּרְסִימוּ מְסַטְרִין דִילִי כַּד קְטָלִית יַת מִצְרָאֵי עַל יַמָא אַרְגִיזוּ קֳדָם יְיָ בְּאָלוּשׁ אַפִיסוּ יַת יוֹמָא דְשַׁבְּתָא כְּדוֹן אִזְדַמְנוּ עַל מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ בְּגִין כֵּן חַזֵי לְנִידוּיֵיהוֹן וּלְגַמְרָא יַת כָּל נִיכְסֵיהוֹן וְלָא תִקְרְבוּן בְּכָל מַה דְאִית לְהוֹן דִלְמָא תִלְקוּן בְּכָל חוֹבֵיהוֹן


And he said to the congregation, Remove now away from the tents of these men of sin, who have been worthy of death from (the days of) their youth in Mizraim, for they betrayed my secret when I slew the Mizraite; they provoked the Lord at the sea; at Alush they profaned the Sabbath, and now are they gathered together against the Word of the Lord; and therefore is it fit that their wealth should be scattered abroad and destroyed. Touch not, then, anything that is theirs, nor be smitten on account of their sins.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:27)


וְאִסְתְּלָקוּ מֵעִלַוֵי מַשְׁכְּנָא דְקרַח דָתָן וַאֲבִירָם מִן חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר וְדָתָן וַאֲבִירָם נְפָקוּ בְּמִילֵי חֵירוּפוּן וְקָמוּ וְאַרְגִיזוּ לְמשֶׁה תְּרַע מַשְׁכְּנֵיהוֹן וּנְשֵׁיהוֹן וּבְנֵיהוֹן וְטַפְלֵיהוֹן


And they went apart from the tents of Korach, Dathan, and Abiram round about. But Dathan and Abiram came out, with reviling words, and arose and provoked Mosheh at the door of their tents, with their wives, their sons, and their little ones.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:28)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה בְּדָא תִנְדְעוּן אֲרוּם יְיָ שַׁדְרַנִי לְמֶעֱבַד יַת כָּל עוֹבָדַיָא הָאִלֵין אֲרוּם לָא מִן רְעוּת לִבִּי


And Mosheh said, By this you shall know that the Lord hath sent me to do all these works, and that (I do them) not from the thoughts of my heart.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:29)


אִין כְּמִיתוּתָא דְמַיְיתוּן בָּהּ כָּל בְּנֵי נְּשָׁא יְמוּתוּן אִלְיֵין וּסְכָמוּת כָּל אֱנָשָׁא יִסְתְּכַם עֲלֵיהוֹן לָא יְיָ שְׁדָרַנִי


If these men die after the manner of dying in which all men die, and the (common) account of all men be accounted upon them, the Lord hath not sent me.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:30)


וְאִין לָא אִתְבָּרִיאַת מִיתוּתָא לְהוֹן מִן יוֹמַת עַלְמָא תִּתְבְּרֵי לְהוֹן כְּדוֹן וְאִין לָא אִתְבְּרֵי פּוּם לְאַרְעָא מִן שֵׁירוּיָא אִתְבְּרֵי לָהּ כְּדוֹן וְתִפְתּוֹחַ אַרְעָא יַת פּוּמָהָא וְתִבְלַע יַתְהוֹן וְיַת כָּל דִלְהוֹן וְיֵחְתוּן כַּד חַיִין לְשֵׁיוּל וְתִנְדְעוּן אֲרוּם אַרְגִיזוּ גוּבְרַיָא הָאִלֵין קֳדָם יְיָ


But if a death which hath not been created since the days of the world be now created for them, and if a mouth for the earth, which hath not been made from the beginning, be created now, and the earth open her mouth and swallow them and all they have, and they go down alive into Sheol, you will understand that these men have provoked the Lord to anger.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:31)


וַהֲוָה כְדִי פָסַק לְמַלָלָא יַת כָּל פִּתְגָמַיָא הָאִלֵין וְאִתְבְּזָעַת אַרְעָא דִתְחוֹתֵיהוֹן


And it came to pass, when he had finished speaking these words, the earth beneath them clave asunder;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:32)


וּפְתָחַת אַרְעָא יַת פּוּמָהּ וּבְלָעַת יַתְהוֹן וְיַת אֱנַשׁ בָּתֵּיהוֹן וְיַת כָּל אֵינָשָׁא דִלְקרַח וְיַת כָּל נִכְסַיָא


and the earth opened her mouth and swallowed them up, and the men of their houses, and all the men who adhered to Korach, and all their substance.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:33)


וּנְחָתוּ הִינוּן וְכָל דִלְהוֹן כַּד חַיִין לְשֵׁיוּל וַחֲפַת עֲלֵיהוֹן אַרְעָא וְאוֹבְדוּ מִגוֹ קְהָלָא


And they went down with all that they had alive into Sheol; and the earth closed upon them, and they perished from the midst of the congregation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:34)


וְכָל יִשְרָאֵל דִי בְּחַזְרָנַתְהוֹן עָרְקוּ מִן דְחִיל קָלְהוֹן הֵיךְ צַוְוחִין וְאָמְרִין זַכַּאי הוּא יְיָ וְקוּשְׁטָא הֵיךְ דִינוֹי וְקוּשְׁטָא הִינוּן פִּתְגָמֵי משֶׁה עַבְדֵיהּ וַאֲנַן רַשִׁיעַיָא דְמָרַדְנָא בֵיהּ וּבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל אַפָּכוּ כְדִי שָׁמְעוּ אֲרוּם אָמְרוּ דִלְמָא תִבְלוֹעַ יָתָן אַרְעָא


And all Israel who were round about them fled from the terror of their voice, as they cried and said, Righteous is the Lord, and His judgment is truth, and the words of His servant Mosheh are truth; but we are wicked who have rebelled against him: and the children of Israel fled when they heard; for they said, Lest the earth swallow us up.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 16:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/16:35)


וְאֵישָׁתָא נְפָקַת בְּרוּגְזָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וַאֲכָלַת יַת מָאתָן וְחַמְשִׁין גוּבְרִין מַסְקֵי קְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמַיָא


And a fire came out in wrath from before the Lord, and devoured the two hundred and fifty men who offered the incense.



## Chapter 17

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:1)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:2)


אֲמַר לְאֶלְעָזָר בַּר אַהֲרן כַּהֲנָא וְיַפְרֵשׁ יַת מַחְתְּיָא מִן בֵּינֵי יַקִידַיָא וְיַת אֵישָׁתָא בַּדְרֵי לְהָאֵל אֲרוּם אִתְקֵדְשָׁא


Bid Elazar bar Aharon the priest to take away the censers from among the burnings, and scatter the fire hither and thither; for the censers of these guilty men who have been punished by the destruction of their lives are consecrated;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:3)


יַת מַחְתְּיַית חַיָיבַיָא הָאִלֵין דְאִתְחַיְיבוּ קְטוֹל בְּנַפְשַׁתְהוֹן וְיַעַבְדוּן מִנְהוֹן רְדִידֵי טָסִין חַפְיֵי לְמַדְבְּחָא אֲרוּם קְרֵיבוּנוּן קֳדָם יְיָ וְאִתְקַדָשׁוּ וִיהוֹן לְאַת לִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


and make of them broad plates for the covering of the altar, because they bare them before the Lord, therefore they are consecrate; and they shall be for a sign to the children of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:4)


וּנְסֵיב אֶלְעָזָר כַּהֲנָא יַת מַחְתְּיַית נְחָשָׁא דְקָרִיבוּ יַקִידַיָא וְרַדִידִינוּן חַפְיֵי לְגוּפָא דְמַדְבְּחָא דְמִן שֵׁירוּיָא הֲווֹן תַּשְׁמִישְׁתֵּיהּ דְמַדְבְּחָא


And Elazar the priest took the brasen censers which they who had been burned had carried, and beat them out for a covering for the body of the altar, as they had before used them for the service of the altar:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:5)


דוּכְרָנָא לִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מִן בִּגְלַל דְלָא יִקְרַב גְבַר חִלוֹנַי דְלָא מִן בְּנֵי אַהֲרן לְאַסָקָא קְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמִין קֳדָם יְיָ וְלָא יְהֵי גְבַר מִתְנַטֵל לְמִפְלוֹג עַל עֵיסַק כְּהוּנְתָּא כְּקרַח וְכִכְנִישַׁת סַעֲדוֹי וְסוֹפֵיהּ לְהוֹבָדָא וְלָא כְמִיתוּתָא דְקרַח וְכִכְנִשְׁתֵּיהּ בִּיקִידַת אֵשָׁא וּבְלִיעַת אַרְעָא אֱלָהֵן לְמִלְקֵי בִּסְגִירוּתָא הֵיכְמָא דְמַלֵיל יְיָ לְמשֶׁה שַׁוֵי יְדָךְ בְּעִיטְפָךְ וְלָקַת יְדֵיהּ בְּצוֹרְעָא הֵיכְדֵין יִמְטֵי לֵיהּ


for a memorial to the sons of Israel, that no common man, who is not of the sons of Aharon, may offer incense before the Lord; and that no man should behave himself factiously to obtain the priesthood, as did Korach and the company of his helpers; and whose end would be to perish, not (indeed) with a death like that of Korach and his company, by being burned by fire, and being swallowed up by the earth, but punished with leprosy: as when the Lord said to Mosheh, Put thy hand into thy bosom, and his hand was stricken with leprosy; so would it be with him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:6)


וְאִתְרַעֲמוּ כָּל כְּנִישְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל בְּיוֹמָא הָדֵין עַל משֶׁה וְעַל אַהֲרן לְמֵימָר אַתּוּן גְרַמְתּוּן דִין מוֹתָא עַל עַמָא דַיְיָ


But on the following day the whole congregation murmured against Mosheh and Aharon, saying: You have been the occasion of the judgment of death against the people of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:7)


וַהֲוָה בְּאִתְכַּנָשׁוּת כְּנִישְׁתָּא עַל משֶׁה וְעַל אַהֲרן לְמִקְטַלְהוֹן וְאִתְפְּנִיוּ לְמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְהָא חַפְיֵיהּ עֲנַן אִיקַר שְׁכִינְתָּא וְאִתְגְלֵי תַמָן אִיקָרָא דַיְיָ


And it was, that when the congregation had gathered against Mosheh and Aharon to kill them, they looked towards the Tabernacle of Ordinance, and, behold, the Cloud of the Glory of the Shekinah covered it, and the Glory of the Lord was revealed there.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:8)


וְעָאל משֶׁה וְאַהֲרן מִן קֳדָם קְהָלָא לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא


And Mosheh and Aharon went from the congregation to the door of the tabernacle.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:9)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:10)


אִתְפְּרָשׁוּ מִגוֹ כְּנִשְׁתָּא הֲדָא וְאֵישֵׁיצֵי יַתְהוֹן כְּשָׁעָה זְעֵירָא וְאִתְרְכִינוּ בִּצְלוֹ עַל אַפֵּיהוֹן


Separate from the midst of this congregation, and I will consume them at once. But they bowed themselves on their faces in prayer.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:11)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְאַהֲרן סַב יַת מַחְתְּיָא וְהַב עֲלָהּ אֵישָׁתָא מֵעִילַוֵי מַדְבְּחָא וְשַׁוִי קְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמִין עַל אֵישָׁתָא וְאוֹבִיל בִּפְרִיעַ לְוַת כְּנִישְׁתָּא וְכַפֵּר אַמְטוּלְהוֹן אֲרוּם נְפַק מְחַבְּלָא דְאִתְכָּלוּ בְּחוֹרֵב דִשְׁמֵיהּ קֶצֶף מִן קָדָם יְיָ בִּהוּרְמָנָא שָׁרֵי לְקַטְלָא


And Mosheh said to Aharon, Take the censer, put fire in it from the altar, and sweet incense on the fire; bear it quickly into the congregation, and make atonement for them: for a destruction like that which consumed them in Horeb, whose name is Burning, hath begun by commandment to kill, from the presence of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:12)


וּנְסֵיב אַהֲרן הֵיכְמָא דְמַלֵיל משֶׁה וּרְהַט לְמִיצַע קְהָלָא וְהָא שָׁרֵי קֶצֶף מְחַבְּלָא לְחַבְּלָא בְּעַמָא וִיהַב יַת קְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמִין וְכַפֵּר עַל עַמָא


And Aharon took, as Mosheh had said, and ran into the midst of the congregation, and, behold the destructive burning had begun to destroy the people: but he put on incense, and made atonement for the people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:13)


וְקָם לֵיהּ אַהֲרן בִּצְלוֹי בְּמַצַע וְעָבַד מְחִיצוּתָא בְּמַחְתְּיָיתָא בֵּינֵי מֵתַיָא וּבֵינֵי חַיַיָא וְאִתְכְּלִיאַת מוֹתָנָא


And Aharon stood in the midst, between the dead and the living with the censer, and interceded in prayer; and the plague was restrained.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:14)


וַהֲווֹ סְכוּם דְמִיתוּ בְּמוֹתָנָא אַרְבֵּיסַר אַלְפִין וּשְׁבַע מְאָה בַּר מִדְמִיתוּ עַל פְּלוּגְתָּא דְקרַח


But the number who had died by the plague was fourteeen thousand and seven hundred, beside those who had died in the schism of Korach.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:15)


וְתַב אַהֲרן לְוַת משֶׁה לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וּמוֹתָנָא אִתְכַּלְיַית


And Aharon returned to Mosheh at the door of the tabernacle; and the plague was stayed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:16)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:17)


מַלֵיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְסַב מִנְהוֹן חוּטְרָא חוּטְרָא לְבֵית אַבָּא מִלְוַת כָּל אֲמַרְכְּלֵיהוֹן לְבֵית אַבְהַתְהוֹן תְּרֵיסַר חוּטְרִין גְבַר יַת שְׁמֵיהּ תִּכְתּוֹב עַל חוּטְרֵיהּ


Speak with the sons of Israel, and take of them severally a rod, according to the house of their fathers; twelve rods; and upon each rod thou shalt inscribe its (tribe) name.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:18)


וְיַת שְׁמָא דְאַהֲרן תִּכְתּוֹב עַל חוּטְרָא דְלֵוִי אֲרוּם חוּטְרָא חָד לְרֵישׁ בֵּית אַבְהַתְהוֹן


But on the rod of Levi thou shalt write the name of Aharon: for there is but one rod for each head of their father's house.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:19)


וְתַצְנְעִינוּן בְּמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא קֳדָם סַהֲדוּתָא דְאֵיזַמֵן מֵימְרִי לְכוֹן תַּמָן


And thou shalt lay them up in the tabernacle before the testimony, where My Word is appointed to meet you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:20)


וִיהֵי גַבְרָא דְאִתְרְעֵי בֵּיהּ לְשַׁמָשָׁא קֳדָמַי חוּטְרֵיהּ יִנְעֵי וְאִישְׁדֵיךְ מִנִי יַת תּוּרְעֲמוּת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל דְהִינוּן מִתְרַעֲמִין עֲלֵיכוֹן


And the man whose rod germinateth shall be he whom I approve to minister before Me; and I will make the murmurings of the sons of Israel with which they have murmured against you to cease from Me.
