###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:21)


וּמַלֵיל משֶׁה עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וִיהָבוּ לֵיהּ כָּל אֲמַרְכְּלֵיהוֹן חוֹטֶר לַאֲמַרְכּוֹל חַד חוֹטֶר לַאֲמַרְכּוֹל חַד לְבֵית אַבְהַתְהוֹן תְּרֵיסַר חוּטְרִין וְחוֹטֶר אַהֲרן בִּמְצִיעוּת חוּטְרֵיהוֹן


Mosheh spake, therefore, with the sons of Israel, and the chiefs of them gave him severally their rods, according to the house of their fathers, twelve rods; and Aharon's rod was among theirs.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:22)


וְאַצְנַע משֶׁה יַת חוּטְרַיָא קֳדָם יְיָ בְּמַשְׁכְּנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא


And Mosheh laid up the rods before the Lord in the tabernacle of ordinance.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:23)


וַהֲוָה מִיוֹמָא חוֹרָן וְעָאל משֶׁה לְמַשְׁכְּנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא וְהָא נְעָא חוֹטֶר אַהֲרן לְבֵית לֵוִי וְאַפֵּיק לִבְלוּבִין וְאָנֵיץ נִצִין בֵּיהּ בְּלֵילְיָא גְמַר וַעֲבַד לוּזִין


And it came to pass, the day after, when Mosheh went into the tabernacle of the testimony, that, behold, the rod of Aharon had germinated; it had shot forth branches, blossomed with flowers, and, in the same night, produced and ripened almonds.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:24)


וְהַנְפַּק משֶׁה יַת כָּל חוּטְרַיָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ לְכָל בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְאִשְׁתְּמוֹדָעוּ וּנְסִיבוּ גְבַר חוּטְרֵיהּ


And Mosheh brought out all the rods from before the Lord to all the sons of Israel, who recognised and took severally their rods.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:25)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה אָתֵיב יַת חוּטְרָא דְאַהֲרן קֳדָם סַהֲדוּתָא לְמַטָרָא לְאָת לִבְנַיָא סַרְבָנַיָא וִיסוּפַן תּוּרְעֲמַתְהוֹן מִן קֳדָמַי וְלָא יְמוּתוּן


And the Lord said to Mosheh, Take back the rod of Aharon, before the testimony, to be kept for a sign for the rebellious children, that their murmurings may cease from before Me, lest they die.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:26)


וַעֲבַד משֶׁה הֵיכְמָא דְפַקֵיד יְיָ יָתֵיהּ הֵיכְדֵין עָבַד


And Mosheh did so; as the Lord commanded so did he.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:27)


וַאֲמָרוּ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְמשֶׁה לְמֵימָר הָא מִינַן אִישְׁתֵּיצִיאוּ בְּשַׁלְהוֹבִית אֵישָׁתָא וּמִנַן אִתְבְּלָעוּ בְּאַרְעָא וְאָבָדוּ הָא אֲנַחְנָא חֲשִׁיבִין כְּאִילוּ אָבַדְנָא כּוּלָנָא


And the sons of Israel spake with Mosheh, saying: Behold, some of us have been consumed with the flaming fire; some of us have been swallowed up by the earth, and have perished! Behold, we are accounted as if all of us are to be destroyed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 17:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/17:28)


כָּל דְקָרֵיב מִיקְרַב לְמַשְׁכְּנָא דַיְיָ מָאִית הַבְּרַם סַפְנָא לְמִשְׁתֵּיצְיָא


Any one who approaches the tabernacle must die: are we not doomed to destruction?



## Chapter 18

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:1)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְאַהֲרן אַנְתְּ וּבְנָךְ וּבֵית אַבָּךְ עִמָךְ תְּקַבְּלוּן חוֹבֵי קוּדְשַׁיָא אִין לָא מִזְדַהֲרִין בְּאַסְקוּתְהוֹן וְאַנְתְּ וּבְנָךְ עִמָךְ תְקַבְּלוּן יַת חוֹבֵי כְּהוּנַתְכוֹן אִין לָא מִזְדַהֲרִין בְּאַפְרָשׁוּתְהוֹן


And the Lord said unto Aharon, Thou, and thy sons, and the house of thy fathers with thee, shall bear the iniquity of the consecrated things, when you nave not been heedful in offering them; and thou and thy sons with thee shall bear the iniquity of your priesthood, when you have not been heedful of their separations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:2)


וְאוּף יַת אָחָךְ שִׁבְטָא דְלֵוִי דְמִתְקְרֵי עַל שְׁמָא דְעַמְרָם אַבָּךְ קָרֵיב לְוָתָךְ וְיִתְחַבְּרוּן לְוָתָךְ וִישַׁמְשׁוּנָךְ וְאַנְתְּ וּבְנָךְ עִמָךְ תְּקוּמוּן קֳדָם מַשְׁכְּנָא דְסַהֲדוּתָא


And thy brethren also of the tribe of Levi, who are called by the name of Amram thy father, shalt thou bring near to thee, that they may consociate with and minister to thee. But thou, and thy sons with thee, (only) shall stand before the tabernacle of the testimony.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:3)


וְיִטְרוּן מַטַרְתָּךְ וּמַטְרַת כָּל מַשְׁכְּנָא בְּרַם לְמָאנֵי קוּדְשָׁא וּלְמַדְבְּחָא לָא יִקְרְבוּן וְלָא יְמוּתוּן אוּף הִינוּן אוּף אַתּוּן


And they shall keep thy charge, and have charge of all the tabernacle; yet to the vessels of the sanctuary and to the altar they are not to come near, lest both they and you die.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:4)


וְיִזְדַמְנוּן לְוָתָךְ מִלְבַּר וְיִטְרוּן יַת מַטְרַת מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לְכָל פּוּלְחַן מַשְׁכְּנָא וְחִילוֹנֵי לָא יִקְרַב לְוַתְכוֹן


And they shall have appointment from thee without, and keep charge of the tabernacle of ordinance for all its service; and a stranger shall not come near you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:5)


וְתִטְרוּן יַת מַטְרַת קוּדְשָׁא וְיַת מַטְרַת מַדְבְּחָא וְלָא יְהֵי תוּב רוּגְזָא דַהֲוָה עַל בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


And you shall keep the charge of the sanctuary and of the altar, that there may be no more the wrath that hath been upon the children of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:6)


וַאֲנָא הָא קְרֵיבִית יַת אֲחוּכוֹן לֵיוָאֵי מִגוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְכוֹן מַתָּנָא יְהִיבִין קֳדָם יְיָ לְמִפְלַח יַת פּוּלְחַן מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא


And, behold, I have taken your brethren the Levites from among the sons of Israel; to you they are given, a gift before the Lord, to perform the work of the tabernacle of ordinance.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:7)


וְאַנְתְּ וּבְנָךְ עִמָךְ תִּנְטְרוּן יַת כְּהוּנַתְכוֹן לְכָל פִּתְגַם מַדְבְּחָא וּלְמִגֵיו לְפַרְגוֹדָא וְתִפְלְחוּן לְפוּם עַדְבַיָא כְּפוּלְחָנָא כֵּן מֵיכַל מַתָּנָה אֶתֵּן בִּרְבוּתָא יַת כְּהוּנַתְכוֹן וְחִילוֹנֵי דְיִקְרַב יִתְקְטֵיל


But thou, and thy sons with thee, shall keep the charge of your priesthood in all things that pertain to the altar, and (those) within the veil, and shall minister by lots, according to the service. So, provision of food have I given you, on account of the anointing of your priesthood; and the stranger who cometh near shall die.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:8)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם אַהֲרן וַאֲנָא בְּחֶדְוָא יְהָבִית לָךְ יַת מַטְרַת אַפְרָשׁוּתִי חַלְתָא וּבִכּוּרַיָא וְכָל קוּדְשַׁיָא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לָךְ יְהַבְתִּינוּן לִרְבוּ וְלִבְנָךְ לִקְיַים עֲלָם


And the Lord said to Aharon, And I have been pleased to give you the charge of My separated offerings; the cakes of the firstfruits, and all the consecrated things of the children of Israel, to thee have I given them, on account of the anointing, and to thy sons, by an everlasting statute.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:9)


דֵין יְהֵי לָךְ מִקוֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשַׁיָא מַה דְמִשְׁתַּיֵיר מֵעָלָתָא דְעָנָא מִן אֵישָׁתָא כָּל קוּרְבַּנְהוֹן לְכָל מִנְחַתְהוֹן וּלְכָל חַטְוַותְהוֹן וּלְכָל אֲשַׁמְהוֹן דִיְתִיבִין קֳדָמַי קוֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשִׁין דִילָךְ הוּא וְדִבְנָךְ


They shall be to thee most sacred; whatsoever remaineth of the sheep offered by fire, all their oblations, of all their minchas, of all their sin offerings, and of all their trespass offerings which they present before Me, they are most sacred for thee and for thy sons.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:10)


בְּקוֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשַׁיָא תֵּיכְלוּנֵיהּ כָּל דְכוּרָא יֵיכוֹל יָתֵיהּ בִּרְבוּתָא קוּדְשָׁא יְהֵי לָךְ


Thou mayest eat it in the sanctuary; every male may eat thereof; on account of the holy anointing it shall be thine.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:11)


וְדֵין דְזָכִיתִי לָךְ אַפְרָשׁוּת מַתְּנַתְהוֹן לְכָל אֲרָמוּת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לָךְ יְהַבְתִּינוּן וְלִבְנָךְ וְלִבְנָתָךְ עִמָךְ לִקְיַים עֲלָם כָּל דְיִדְכֵּי בְּבֵיתָךְ יֵיכוּל יָתֵיהּ


And this is what I have set apart to thee of their separated minchas, and of all the uplifted things of the sons of Israel, to thee have I given them, and to thy sons and thy daughters with thee by an everlasting statute. Whoever is clean in thy house may eat of it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:12)


כָּל טוּב מְשַׁח זֵיתָא וְכָל טוּב חֲמַר עִינְבָא וְעִיבּוּר שֵׁירוּיֵיהוֹן דְיִתְּנוּן קֳדָם יְיָ לָךְ יְהַבְתִּינוּן


All the best of the olive oil, of the grape wine, and of the wheat of their firstfruits which they present before the Lord, I have given unto thee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:13)


בִּכּוּרֵי דְכָל פֵּירֵי אִילָנֵי אַרְעֲהוֹן דְיִקְרְבוּן קֳדָם יְיָ דִילָךְ יְהֵי כָּל דִדְכֵי בְּבֵיתָךְ יֵיכְלוּנֵיהּ


The firsts of all the trees of their ground which they present before the Lord shall be thine; every one who is clean in thy house may eat them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:14)


כָּל דְמִגְמַר בְּיִשְרָאֵל דִילָךְ יְהֵי


Every devoted thing in Israel shall be thine.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:15)


כָּל פְּתַח וַלְדָא לְכָל בִּשְרָא בִּבְעִירָא דְיִקְרְבוּן מִינָהּ קֳדָם יְיָ כְּדִינָא דְאֵינָשָׁא כֵּן דִינָא בִּבְעִירָא לְמֶהֱוֵי דִילָךְ בְּרַם מִפְרַק תִּפְרוֹק יַת בּוּכְרָא דְאֵינָשָׁא בְּחַמְשָׁא סִלְעִין וְיַת בּוּכְרָא דִבְעִירָא מְסָאָבָא תִּפְרוֹק בְּאִמְרַיָא


Whatever openeth the womb, of all flesh among animals which they offer before the Lord, as the regulation concerning men, so the regulation concerning cattle, it is to be thine: only thou art to redeem the firstborn of man by the five shekels, and the firstlings of the unclean animal thou shalt redeem with lambs.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:16)


וּפִרְקוּנֵיהּ דְבַר נַשׁ מִבַּר יַרְחָא תִּפְרוֹק בִּסְכוּם עִלוֹיָיךְ כְּסַף חָמֵשׁ סִלְעִין בְּסִלְעֵי בֵּית קוּדְשָׁא עַשְרִין מָעִין הוּא


And the redemption of a man child of a month old thou shalt make, according to thy estimation of him, by five shekels of silver in the shekel of the sanctuary, which is twenty meahs.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:17)


בְּרַם בּוּכְרָא דְתוֹרֵי אוֹ בוּכְרָא דְאִימְרֵי אוֹ בוּכְרָא דְעִזֵי לָא תִפְרוֹק מְטוֹל דְקוּדְשָׁא הִינוּן וְיַת אַדְמַתְהוֹן תִּדְרוֹק עַל מַדְבְּחָא וְיַת תַּרְבֵּיהוֹן תַּסִיק קוּרְבַּן דְמִתְקַבֵּל בְּרַעֲוָא קֳדָם יְיָ


But the firstlings of oxen, of sheep, or of goats thou mayest not redeem, for they are sacred; but thou shalt sprinkle their blood upon the altar, and burn their fat for an oblation to be accepted before, the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:18)


וּבִשְרְהוֹן יְהֵי לָךְ לְמֵיכַל הֵי כְּחַדְיָא דַאֲרָמוּתָא וְהֵי כְשַׁקָא דְיַמִינָא דִילָךְ יְהֵי


And their flesh shall be thine, for food; as the breast of the elevation, and as the right shoulder, it shall be thine.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:19)


כָּל אַפְרָשׁוּת קוּדְשַׁיָא דִי יְקַדְשׁוּן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל קֳדָם יְיָ יְהָבִית לָךְ וְלִבְנָךְ וְלִבְנָתָךְ עִמָךְ לִקְיַים עֲלָם וְלָא יִתְבַּטֵיל הֵי כְּמִלְחָא דִמְבַסִים בְּשַר קוּרְבָּנַיָא דִקְיַים עֲלָם הוּא קֳדָם יְיָ הֵיכְדֵין הוּא לָךְ וְלִבְנָךְ


Every thing set apart of the sacred things which the sons of Israel consecrate to the Lord have I given to thee, to thy sons and thy daughters with thee, by a perpetual statute not to be abolished; as the salt which seasoneth the flesh of the oblation, because it is an everlasting statute before the Lord, so shall it be for thee and for thy children.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:20)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְאַהֲרן בְּאַרְעֲהוֹן לָא תְקַבֵּל אַחְסָנָא כְּמִשְׁאַר שִׁבְטַיָא וְחוּלְקָא לָא יֶהֱוֵי לָךְ בֵּינֵיהוֹן אֲנָא חֶלְקָךְ וְאַחְסַנְתָּךְ בְּגוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


And the Lord said to Aharon, Thou wilt not receive a possession in their land as the rest of the tribes, nor wilt thou have a portion among them: I am thy Portion and thy Inheritance in the midst of the children of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:21)


וְלִבְנוֹי דְלֵוִי הָא יְהָבִית יַת כָּל מַעַשְרָא בְּיִשְרָאֵל בְּאַחְסָנָא חוֹלַף פּוּלְחַנְהוֹן דְהִינוּן פַּלְחִין יַת פּוּלְחַן מַשְׁכְּנָא זִמְנָא


And, behold, I have given to the sons of Levi all the tenths in Israel for a possession, on account of their service with which they serve in the work of the tabernacle of ordinance.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:22)


וְלָא יְקַרְבוּן תּוּב בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לְקַבָּלָא חוֹבָא לִמְמָת


And the sons of Israel shall no more come near the tabernacle to incur the sin unto death;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:23)


וְיִפְלְחוּן לֵיוָאֵי הִינוּן יַת פּוּלְחַן מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְהִינוּן יְקַבְּלוּן יַת חוֹבֵיהוֹן אִין לָא מִזְדַהֲרִין בְּפוּלְחַנְהוֹן קְיַים עֲלָם לְדָרֵיכוֹן וּבְגוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לָא יַחְסְנוּן אַחְסָנָא


but the Levites shall minister in the work of the tabernacle, and shall bear their sin if they be not diligent in their work. It is an everlasting statute for your generations; but among the sons of Israel they shall have no possession.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:24)


אֲרוּם יַת מַעַשְרָא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל דְיִפְרְשׁוּן קֳדָם יְיָ אַפְרָשׁוּתָא יְהָבִית לְלֵיוָאֵי לְאַחְסָנָא בְגִין כֵּן לְהוֹן אַמְרִית דִבְגוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לָא יַחְסְנוּן אַחְסָנָא


Therefore the tenths of the children of Israel, which they set apart for a separation before the Lord, have I given to the Levites for a possession, because I have said to them that among the sons of Israel they shall possess no inheritance.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:25)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:26)


וּלְלֵיוָאֵי תְמַלֵיל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן אֲרוּם תִּסְבוּן מִן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל יַת מַעַשְרָא דִיהָבִית לְהוֹן בְּאַחְסַנְתְּהוֹן וְתִפְרְשׁוּן מִינֵיהּ אַפְרָשׁוּתָא קֳדָם יְיָ מַעַשְרָא מִגוֹ מַעַשְרָא


Speak to the Levites, and bid them take from the sons of Israel the tenth which I have given them for their possession; and (then) shall you separate from it a separation before the Lord, a tenth from the tenth;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:27)


וְאִתְחַשָׁב לְכוֹן אַפְרָשׁוּתְכוֹן הֵי כְּעִבּוּרָא מִן אִידְרָא וְהֵי כְּחַמְרָא דְמַלְיָתָא מִן מַעֲצַרְתָּא


and your separation shall be reckoned to you as the corn from the threshing floor, and as the wine from the fulness of the winepress:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:28)


הֵיכְדֵין תִּפְרְשׁוּן לְחוֹד אַתּוּן אַפְרָשׁוּתָא קֳדָם יְיָ מִכָּל מַעַשְרֵיכוֹן דְתִסְבוּן מִן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְתִתְּנוּן מִנֵיהּ אַפְרָשׁוּתָא קֳדָם יְיָ לְאַהֲרן כַּהֲנָא


so shall you set apart your separation before the Lord from all your tenths, which you may receive from the sons of Israel, and give thereof a separation before the Lord unto Aharon the priest.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:29)


מִכָּל מַתְּנָתֵיכוֹן תִּפְרְשׁוּן יַת אַפְרָשׁוּתָא קֳדָם יְיָ מִן כָּל שְׁפַר טוּבֵיהּ מִינֵיהּ וּבֵיהּ


Of all your gifts you shall set apart a separation before the Lord, of all the finest and the best therein.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:30)


וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן לְכַהֲנַיָא בְּאַפְרָשׁוּתְכוֹן יַת שְׁפַר טוּבֵיהּ מִינֵיהּ וּבֵיהּ וְיִתְחַשֵׁב לְלֵיוָאֵי הֵי כְּאַפְרָשׁוּת עִבּוּרָא מִן גוֹ אִידְרָא וְהֵי כְּאַפְרָשׁוּת חַמְרָא מִגוֹ מַעֲצַרְתָּא


And say thou to the priests, When you have set apart the finest and the best of it and in it, then shall it be reckoned to the Levites as the setting apart of corn from the threshing floor, and of wine from the winepress.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:31)


וְתֵיכְלוּן יָתֵיהּ אַתּוּן כַּהֲנַיָא בְּכָל אֲתַר אַתּוּן וְאֵינַשׁ בָּתֵּיכוֹן אֲרוּם אַגְרָא הוּא לְכוֹן חִילוּפֵי פּוּלְחַנְכוֹן בְּמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא


And you may eat it, you, the priests, in any place, you and the men of your house; for it is your remuneration for your service in the tabernacle of ordinance.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 18:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/18:32)


וְלָא תְקַבְּלוּן עֲלוֹי חוֹבָא בִּזְמַן אַפְרָשׁוּתְכוֹן יַת שְׁפַר טוּבֵיהּ מִינֵיהּ וּבֵיהּ לְאוֹכְלֵי מִינֵיהּ לִדְסָאִיב וְיַת קוּדְשַּׁיָא דִבְנֵי יִשְ¦רָאֵל לָא תִפְסוּן וְלָא תְמוּתוּן


And you shall not contract guilt by it, at what time you set apart the finest and best of it, by any one eating of it who is unclean; neither shall you profane the consecrated things of the children of Israel, lest you die.



## Chapter 19

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:1)


וידבר וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה וְעִם אַהֲרן לְמֵימָר


AND the Lord spake with Mosheh and Aharon, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:2)


דָא גְזֵירַת אַחְוָיַת אוֹרַיְיתָא דְפַקֵיד יְיָ לְמֵימָר מַלֵיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְיִסְבוּן לָךְ מֵאַפְרָשׁוּת לִישְׁכְּתָא תוֹרָתָא סוּמְקָתָא בְּרַת תַּרְתֵּין שְׁנִין דְלֵית בָּהּ מוּמָא וְשׁוּמָא מִשֵעָר חַוְורָן דְלָא סַלִיק עֲלָהּ דְכַר וְלָא אַטְרָחָא בְּאוּבְצַן עִיבִידְתָּא וְאִפְסְרָא וּקְטַרְבָא וְלָא אִידְעָצָא בְּזִקְתָא וְסוּל וְסִירְתָּא וְכָל דְדָמֵי לְנִירָא


This is the decree, the publication of the law which the Lord hath commanded, saying; Speak to the sons of Israel, that they bring to thee from the separation of the fold a red heifer, two years old, in which there is neither spot nor white hair, on which no male hath come, nor the burden of any work been imposed, neither hurt by the thong, nor grieved by the goad or prick, nor collar (band) or any like yoke.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:3)


וְתִתְּנוּן יָתָהּ לְאֶלְעָזָר סְגַן כַּהֲנַיָא וִיהַנְפַּק יָתָהּ לְחוֹדָא לְמִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא וִיסַדֵר חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר לָהּ סִידוּרֵי קִיסִין דְתֵינַיָא וְכַהֲנָא אוֹחֲרַן יִכּוֹס יָתָהּ קֳדָמוֹי בִּתְרֵין סִימָנַיָא כְּמִשְׁאַר בְּעִירַן וְיִבְדִיקְנָהּ בִּתְמַנֵי סְרִי טְרִיפַן


And thou shalt give her unto Elazar, the chief of the priests, who shall lead her alone without the camp, and set round about her a railing (border) of the branches of fig trees; and another priest shall slay her with the two signs before him, after the manner of other animals, and examine her by the eighteen kinds of divisions.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:4)


וְיִסַב אֶלְעָזָר בְּכִיהוּנֵיהּ מִן אַדְמֵיהּ בְּאַדְבַּע יַד יְמִינֵיהּ וְלָא יְקַבְּלִינֵיהּ בְּמָנָא וְיַדֵי לְסִידוּרָא דְקִיסֵי תֵינַיָא מִן לָגִין מִן צִטְרָא דִמְמַצַע כָּל קֳבֵיל אַפֵּי מַשְׁכַּן זִימְנָא מֵאַדְמָא בְּטִיבוּלָא חֲדָא שְׁבַע זִמְנִין


And Elazar, in his priestly dress, shall take of her blood with the finger of his right hand, without (first) containing it in a vessel, and shall sprinkle the border of fig branches, and (afterwards) from the midst of a vessel on one side towards the tabernacle of ordinance, with one dipping, seven times (shall he sprinkle).


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 19:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:5)


וְיִפְקוּן מִגוֹ סְדוּרָא וְיוֹקֵד כּהֵן אוֹחֲרַן יַת תּוֹרָתָא כַּד חָמֵי אֶלְעָזָר יַת מוֹשְׁכָהּ וְיַת בִּשְרָהּ וְיַת אַדְמָהּ עַל רֵיעַיָיהּ יוֹקֵד


And they shall bring her out from the midst of the railing and another priest, while Elazar looketh on, shall burn the heifer, her skin, flesh, and blood, with her dung shall he burn.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 19:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:6)


וְיִסַב כּהֵן אוֹחֲרַן בִּקְעֲתָא דְקֵיסָא דְגוּלְמוּשׁ וְאֵיזוֹבָא וּצְבַע דְאִשְׁתָּנֵי בִּזְהוֹרִי וְיִטְלוֹק לְגוֹ יְקִידְתָּא דְתוֹרָתָא וְיַסְגֵי דְלֵיקְתָּא לְאַפּוּשֵׁי קִיטְמָא


And another priest shall take a piece of cedar wood and hyssop, and (wool) whose colour hath been changed to scarlet, and throw them into the midst of the burning of the heifer; and he shall enlarge the burning, that the ashes may be increased.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 19:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:7)


וִיצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹי כַּהֲנָא דִינְכֵס יַת תּוֹרָתָא וְיַסְחֵי בִישְרֵיהּ בְּאַרְבְּעִין סָוִוין דְמוֹי וּמִן בָּתַר כְּדֵין יֵיעוּל לְמַשְׁרִיתָא וִיהֵי מְסָאָב כַּהֲנָא הַהוּא קֳדָם טִיבוּלֵיהּ עַד רַמְשָׁא


And the priest who slew the heifer shall wash his dress in forty satas of water, and afterwards he may go into the camp; but the priest before his ablution shall be unclean until the evening.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 19:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:8)


וְכַהֲנָא דְמִתְעַסֵק בִּיקִידְתָּא יְצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹי בְּאַרְבְּעִין סָוִוין דְמוֹי וְיַסְחֵי בִישְרֵיהּ בְּאַרְבְּעִין סָוִוין דְמוֹי וִיהֵי מְסָאָב קֳדָם טִבוּלֵיהּ עַד רַמְשָׁא


And the priest who was employed in the burning shall wash his dress in forty satas of water, and his flesh in forty satas, and before his ablution shall be unclean until the evening.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 19:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:9)


וִיכְנוֹשׁ גְבַר כָּהִין דְכֵי יַת קִטְמָא דְתוֹרָתָא בְּקָלָל דְפָחַר מַקַף מְגוּפַת שְׁיַיע וִיפַלֵג יַת קִטְמָא לִתְלַת חוּלְקִין חֲדָא יָהִיב בְּחֵיל וַחֲדָא בְּטַוַור מִשְׁחָא וְחָדָא מִפְלַג לְכָל מַטְרַת לֵיוָאֵי וּתְהֵי לִכְנִישְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְמוֹי אַדְיוּתָא בְּרַם שִׁיבוּק חוֹבַת עֵיגְלָא הִיא


And a man, a priest who is clean, shall gather up the ashes of the heifer in an earthenware receptacle, its opening covered round about with clay; and shall divide the ashes into three portions, of which one shall be placed within the wall (of Jerusalem), another in the Mount of Olives, and the third portion be in the custody of the Levites; and it shall be for the congregation of Israel, for the Water of Sprinkling: it is the heifer (immolated) for the remission of sins.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 19:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:10)


וִיצַבַּע כַּהֲנָא דִכְנֵישׁ יַת קִטְמָא דְתוֹרָתָא יַת לְבוּשׁוֹי וִיהֵי מְסָאָב קֳדָם טִיבוּלֵיהּ עַד רַמְשָׁא וּתְהֵי לִדְכוּתָא לִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וּלְגִיוֹרַיָא דְיִתְגַיְירוּן בֵּינֵיהוֹן לִקְיַים עֲלָם


And the priest who gathered up the ashes of the heifer shall wash his clothes, and before his ablution be unclean till the evening. And this shall be for the cleansing of the children of Israel, a statute for ever.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 19:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:11)


דְיִקְרַב בִּשְׁכִיבָא לְכָל בַּר נְשָׁא וַאֲפִילוּ לְוַולְדָא בַּר יַרְחִין לְגוּשְׁמֵיהּ וּבִדְמֵיהּ יְהֵי מְסָאָב שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמִין


Whoever toucheth the body of a dead man, or of a child of some months old, either his body or his blood, shall be unclean seven days.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 19:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:12)


הוּא יַדֵי עֲלוֹי מֵי קִטְמָא הַהוּא בְּיוֹמָא תְלִיתָאָה וּבְיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה יִדְכֵּי וְאִין לָא יַדֵי עֲלוֹי בְּיוֹמָא תְלִיתָאָה יְעַכֵּב עֲלוֹי וּבְיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה לָא יִדְכֵּי


He shall sprinkle himself with this water of the ashes on the third day, and on the seventh day he shall be clean. But if he sprinkle not himself on the third day, his uncleanness will remain upon him, and he will not be clean on the seventh day.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 19:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:13)


כָּל דִי יִקְרַב בִּשְׁכִיבָא וּבְוַולְדָא בַּר תִּשְׁעָא יַרְחִין בְּגוּשְׁמֵיהּ וּבִדְמֵיהּ דְיֵימִית וְלָא יַדֵי עֲלוֹי יַת מַשְׁכְּנָא דַיְיָ סָאִיב וְיִשְׁתֵּיצֵי בַּר נְשָׁא הַהוּא מִיִשְרָאֵל אֲרוּם מוֹי אַדְיוּתָא לָא אִזְדְרִיקוּ עֲלוֹי מְסָאָב הוּא תּוּב סוּבְתֵּיהּ בֵּיהּ עַד דְיַדֵי וִיהַדֵר וְיַדֵי וְיִטְבּוֹל בְּרַמְשָׁא שְׁבִיעָאָה


Whoever hath touched the body of a dead man, or of a child nine months old, either the body or the blood, and will not sprinkle himself, he hath defiled the tabernacle of the Lord, and that man shall be cut off from Israel; forasmuch as the water of sprinkling is not sprinkled upon him, he is unclean, his uncleanness is yet on him, until he shall sprinkle himself; yet may he sprinkle and make ablution on the seventh evening.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 19:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:14)


דָא אַחְוָויַית אוֹרַיְתָא גְבַר אֲרֵי יְמוֹת תְּחוֹת גַנָנָא דְפָרִיס כָּל מַאן דְעָלִיל לְמַשְׁכְּנָא אוֹרַח תַּרְעָא וְלָא מִן צִדְדֵיהּ כִּדְפָתַח תַּרְעֵיהּ מִכָּל דִבְמַשְׁכְּנָא וַאֲפִילוּ קַרְקָעִיתֵיהּ וְאַבְנוֹי וְקִיסוֹי וּמָנוֹי יְהֵי מְסָאָב שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמִין


This is the indication of the law concerning a man when he hath died under the outspread tent every one who entereth into the tent by the way of the door, but not from its side, when its door is open, (or when one hath opened its door,) and whatever is in the tent, its floor, stone, wood, and vessels, shall be unclean seven days.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 19:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:15)


וְכָל מַאן דְפָחַר פָּתִיחַ דְלֵית מְגוּפְתָּא מְחַבְּרָא בֵּיהּ מַקַף עַל פּוּמֵיהּ דְיַפְרֵישׁ בֵּינֵיהּ וּבֵין סוּבְתָּא מְסָאָב הוּא בִּגְנֵינָא בְּאַוִירָא דְסוֹבְתָּא דְמִפּוּמֵיהּ וּמִגַוֵיהּ וְלָא מִגַבֵּיהּ


And every earthen vessel which hath no covering fastened upon its mouth, which would have kept it separate from the uncleanness, is defiled by the uncleanness of the air which toucheth its mouth, and its interior, and not the outside of it (only).


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 19:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:16)


וְכָל מַאן דְמִקְרַב עַל אַנְפֵּי בָרָא וְלָא בְּמִיתָא דְבִכְרֵיסָא דְאִמֵיהּ בִּקְטוֹל סַיְיפָא אוֹ בְּסַיְיפָא דְקָטִיל בֵּיהּ אוֹ בִּשְׁכִיבָא שְׁלֵימָא וַאֲפִילוּ בְּגַרְמֵיהּ כְּשַעַרְתָּא אוֹ בְגַרְמָא דְאֵינָשָׁא חַיָא דְפָרַשׁ מִנֵיהּ אוֹ בִּקְבוּרְתָּא וְגוֹלְלָא וְדוֹפְקָא יְהֵי מְסָאָב שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמִין


And whoever shall touch not one who hath died in his mother's womb, but who hath been slain with the sword on the face of the field, or the sword with which he was slain, or the dead man himself, or a bone of his, or the hair, or the bone of a living man which hath been separated from him, or a grave, or a shroud, or the bier, shall be unclean seven days.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 19:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:17)


וְיִסְבוּן לְדִמְסָאָב מִן עֲפַר יְקִידַת חַטָאתָא וְיִתֵּן עֲלוֹי מֵי מַבּוּעַ לְגוֹ מַאן דְפָחַר


And for him who is unclean, they shall take of the ashes of the burnt sin offering, and put spring water upon them in an earthen vessel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 19:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:18)


וְיִסַב אֵזוֹבָא תְּלָתָא קִילְחִין בְּאִיסְרָא חֲדָא וְיִטְמוֹשׁ בְּמַיָא הָאִינוּן בְּעִידַן קִבּוּל סוּבְתָּא גְבַר כָּהֵן דְכֵי וְיַדֵי עַל מַשְׁכְּנָא וְעַל כָּל מָנַיָא וְעַל בְּנֵי נַשׁ דַהֲווֹ תַמָן וְעַל דְמִקְרַב בְּגַרְמָא דְחַיָא דְפָרֵישׁ מִנֵיהּ וְנָפַל אוֹ בִּקְטוֹל חַרְבָּא אוֹ בִּשְׁכִיבָא בְּמוֹתָנָא אוֹ בְּבֵית קְבוּרְתָּא וְגוֹלְלָא וְדוֹפְקָא


And let a man, a priest, who is clean, take three branches of hyssop bound. together, and dip (them) in the water at the time of receiving the uncleanness, and sprinkle the tent and all its vessels, and the men who are in it, or upon him who hath touched the bone of a living man that hath been severed from him, and hath fallen, or him who hath. been slain with the sword, or hath died by the plague, or a grave, or a wrapper, or a bier.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 19:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:19)


וְיַדֵי כַּהֲנָא דַכְיָא עַל גַבְרָא מְסָאֲבָא בְּיוֹמָא תְלִיתָאָה וּבְיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה וְיַדְכִּינֵיהּ בְיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה וִיצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹי וְיַסְחֵי בְמַיָא וְיִדְכֵּי בְרַמְשָׁא


And the priest who is clean shall sprinkle upon the unclean man on the, third day, and on the seventh day, and shall make him clean on the seventh day; and he shall sprinkle his clothes, and wash himself with water, and at eventide be clean.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 19:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:20)


וּגְבַר דְיִסְתָּאַב וְלָא יַדֵי עֲלוֹי וְיִשְׁתֵּיצֵי בַר נְשָׁא הַהוּא מִגוֹ קְהָלָא אֲרוּם יַת מַקְדְשָׁא דַיְיָ סָאִיב מֵי אַדְיוּתָא לָא אִזְדְרִיקוּ עֲלוֹי מְסָאָב הוּא


But the unclean man who will not be sprinkled, that man shall be cut off from among the congregation, because he hath defiled the sanctuary of the Lord; the water of sprinkling hath not been sprinkled upon him, he is unclean.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 19:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:21)


וּתְהֵי לְכוֹן לִקְיַים עֲלָם וּלְחוֹד כַּהֲנָא דְמַדֵי מֵי אַדְיוּתָא יְצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹי וּדְיִקְרַב בְּמֵי אַדְיוּתָא יְהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא


And it shall be unto you an everlasting statute. The priest, also, who sprinkleth the water of sprinkling shall sprinkle his clothes, and he who toucheth the water of sprinkling shall be unclean until evening.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 19:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/19:22)


וְכָל מִדַעַם דְיִקְרַב בֵּיהּ מְסָאֲבָא וְלָא בְהֵיסֵיטָא יְהֵי מְסָאָב וּבַר נַשׁ דְכֵי דְיִקְרַב בֵּיהּ יְהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא


And whatever the unclean person hath touched, though he carry it not, shall be unclean; and the clean man who toucheth him shall be unclean till evening.



## Chapter 20

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:1)


וְאָתוּ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא לְמַדְבְּרָא דְצִין בְּעַשְרָא יוֹמִין לְיַרְחָא דְנִיסָן וּמִיתַת תַּמָן מִרְיָם וְאִתְקַבָּרַת תַּמָן


And the whole congregation of the children of Israel came to the desert of Zin on the tenth day of the month Nisan. And Miriam died there, and was buried there.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:2)


וּלְפוּם דְבִזְכוּתָא דְמִרְיָם אִתְיְהִיבַת בֵּירָא כַּד שְׁכִיבַת אִתְגְנִיזַת בֵּירָא וְלָא הֲוָה מוֹי לִכְנִישְׁתָּא וְאִתְכְּנִישׁוּ עַל משֶׁה וְעַל אַהֲרן


And as on account of the innocency of Miriam a well had been given, so when she died the well was hidden, and the congregation had no water.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:3)


וּנְצָא עַמָא עַל משֶׁה וַאֲמַרוּ לְמֵימָר הַלְוַאי דְמִיתְנָא כַּד מִיתוּ אֲחָנָא קֳדָם יְיָ


And they gathered against Mosheh and Aharon, and the people contended with Mosheh, and said, Would that we had died when our brethren died before the Lord!


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:4)


וּלְמָא אָתֵיתוּן יַת קְהָלָא דַיְיָ לְמַדְבְּרָא הָדֵין לִמְמַת תַּמָן אֲנַחְנָא וּבְעִירָנָא


And why hast thou brought the congregation of the Lord into this desert, that we and our cattle may die here?


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:5)


וּלְמָא אַסֵיקְתּוּנָא מִמִצְרַיִם לְאַיְתָאָה יָתָן לְאַתְרָא בִּישָׁא הָדֵין לָא אֲתַר כְּשַׁר לְבֵית דְרַע וְאוּף לָא לְמִנְצַב תֵּינִין וְגוּפְנִין וְרוֹמָנִין וּמוֹי לֵית לְמִשְׁתֵּי


And why didst thou make us come up out of Mizraim., to bring us to this evil place, a place which is not fit for sowing, or for planting fig trees, or vines, or pomegranates, and where there is no water to drink?


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:6)


וְעָאל משֶׁה וְאַהֲרן מִן קֳדָם תּוּרְעֲמוּת קְהָלָא לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְאִתְרְכִינוּ עַל אַפֵּיהוֹן וְאִתְגְלֵי אִיקַר שְׁכִינְתָּא דַיְיָ לְהוֹן


And Mosheh and Aharon went from the face of the murmuring congregation to the door of the tabernacle of ordinance, and bowed upon their faces, and the Glory of the Lord's Shekinah was revealed to them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:7)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:8)


סַב יַת חוֹטֶר נִיסַיָא וּכְנַשׁ יַת כְּנִישְׁתָּא אַנְתְּ וְאַהֲרן אָחוּךְ וְתוּמוּן תְּרֵיכוֹן יַת כֵּיפָא בִּשְׁמָא רַבָּא וּמְפַרְשָׁא כַּד הִינוּן חַמְיָין וְיִתֵּן מוֹהִי וְאִין יְסָרֵב לְאַפּוֹקֵי מָחֵי אַנְתְּ לְחוֹדָךְ בֵּיהּ בְּחוּטְרָא דְבִידָךְ וּתְהַנְפֵּק לְהוֹן מַיָא מִן כֵּיפָא וְתַשְׁקֵי יַת כְּנִישְׁתָּא וְיַת בְּעִירֵיהוֹן


Take the rod of the miracles, and gather the congregation, thou, and Aharon thy brother, and both of you adjure the rock, by the Great and manifested Name, while they look on, and it shall give forth its waters: but if it refuse to bring forth, smite thou it once with the rod that is in thy hand, and thou wilt bring out water for them from the rock, that the congregation and their cattle may drink.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:9)


וּנְסֵיב משֶׁה יַת חוֹטֶר נִיסַיָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ הֵיכְמָא דְפַקְדֵיהּ


And Mosheh took the rod of the miracles from before the Lord, as he had commanded him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:10)


וּכְנִישׁוּ משֶׁה וְאַהֲרן יַת קְהָלָא לִקְדָם כֵּיפָא וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן משֶׁה שְׁמָעוּ כְדוֹן סוּרְבָנַיָא הֲמִן כֵּיפָא הָדֵין אֶפְשַׁר לָן לְהַנְפָּקָא לְכוֹן מַיָא


And Mosheh and Aharon gathered the congregation together before the rock. And Mosheh said to them, Hear now, rebels: is it possible for us to bring forth water for you from this rock?


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:11)


וּזְקֵף משֶׁה יַת יְדֵיהּ וּמְחָא יַת כֵּיפָא בְּחוּטְרֵיהּ תַּרְתֵּין זִמְנִין בְּזִמְנָא קַמָאָה אֲטִיפַת אַדְמָא וּבְזִמְנָא תִּנְיָינָא נְפָקוּ מַיִין סַגִיאִין וְאִשְׁתִיאַת כְּנִשְׁתָּא וּבְעִירֵיהוֹן


And Mosheh lifted up his hand, and with his rod struck the rock two times: at the first time it dropped blood; but at the second time there came forth a multitude of waters. And the congregation and their cattle drank.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:12)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה וּלְאַהֲרן בִּשְׁבוּעֲתָא חוּלַף דְלָא הֵימַנְתּוּן בְּמֵימְרִי לְקַדָשׁוּתִי לְמֶחֱמֵיהוֹן בִּבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל בְּגִין כֵּן לָא תְהַנְעֲלוּן יַת קְהָלָא הָדֵין לְאַרְעָא דִי אֶתֵּן לְהוֹן


But the Lord spake to Mosheh and Aharon with the oath, Because ye have not believed in My Word, to sanctify Me in the sight of the children of Israel, therefore you shall not bring this congregation into the land that I will give them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:13)


הִינוּן מֵי מַצוּתָא דְנָצוּ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל קֳדָם יְיָ עַל עֵיסַק בֵּירָא דְאִתְגְנִיזַת וְאִתְקְדַשׁ בְּהוֹן בְּמשֶׁה וְאַהֲרן כַּד אִתְיְהִיבַת לְהוֹן


These are the Waters of Contention, where the sons of Israel contended before the Lord on account of the well that had been hidden; and He was sanctified in them, in Mosheh and Aharon, when (the waters) were given to them.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:14)


וְשָׁדַר משֶׁה אִזְגַדִין מֵרְקָם לְוַת מַלְכָּא דֶאֱדוֹם לְמֵימָר כִּדְנַן אָמַר אָחוּךְ יִשְרָאֵל אַנְתְּ יַדְעַת יַת כָּל אַנִיקֵי דְאַשְׁכַּחְתָּנָא


Then Mosheh sent messengers from Rekem unto the king of Edom, saying, Thus saith thy brother Israel. Thou hast known all the trouble that hath found us;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:15)


וּנְחָתוּ אַבְהָתָן לְמִצְרַיִם וִיתֵיבְנָא בְמִצְרַיִם יוֹמִין סַגִיאִין וְאַבְאִישׁוּ לָנָא מִצְרָאֵי וּלְאַבְהָתָנָא


that our fathers went down into Mizraim and dwelt in Mizraim many days, and the Mizraee afflicted us and our fathers.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:16)


וְצַלִינָא קֳדָם יְיָ וְקַבֵּיל צְלוֹתָנָא וְשָׁדַר חַד מִמַלְאֲכֵי שֵׁירוּתָא וְהַנְפָקָנָא מִמִצְרַיִם וְהָא אֲנַחְנָא בִּרְקַם קַרְתָּא דְמִתְבַּנְיָא בִּסְטַר תְּחוּמָךְ


And we prayed before the Lord, who heard our prayers, and sent one of the ministering angels to lead us out of Mizraim: and, behold, we are in Rekem, a city built on the side of thy border.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:17)


נְעִיבַר כְּדוֹן בְּאַרְעָךְ לָא נְשַׁרְגְנָא בְּתוּלַן וְלָא נְאַנְנַס אֲרִיסַן וְלָא נִבְעוֹל נְשֵׁי גוּבְרִין בְּאוֹרַח מַלְכָּא דְבִשְׁמַיָא נֵיזֵיל וְלָא נִסְטֵי לְיַמִינָא וְלִשְמָאלָא לְהַנְזָקָא בִּשְׁבִילֵי רְשׁוּתָא עַד דִנְעִיבַּר תְּחוּמָךְ


Let us now pass through thy land: we will not seduce virgins, nor carry off the betrothed, nor commit adultery: on the king's highway, under the heavens, we will go forward, and turn not to the right or to the left, to do any injury in the public way while we pass through thy border.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:18)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ אֱדוֹמָאָה לָא תְעִיבַּר בִּתְחוּמִי דִלְמָא בִשְׁלִיפֵי חֶרֶב אֵפּוֹק לִקְדָמוּתָךְ


But Edomea answered him, You shall not go through my coast, lest I come to meet thee with the unsheathed sword.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:19)


וַאֲמָרוּ לֵיהּ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל בְּאִסְטַרְטַיָא דְמַלְכָּא נֵיזֵיל אִין מַיִיךְ נִשְׁתֵּי אֲנָא וּבְעִירֵי וְאֵיתֵּן דְמֵי מֵימְהוֹן לְחוֹד לֵית פִּתְגַם דְבִישׁ בִּלְחוֹדִי אֲעִיבַר


And Israel said to him, We would go by the king's highway; if we drink thy waters, I and my cattle, I will give thee the price of their value. I will only pass through, without doing wrong.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:20)


וַאֲמַר לָא תְעִיבַר וּנְפַק אֱדוֹמָאָה לְקַדְמוּתֵיהּ בְחֵיל רַב וּבִידָא תַקִיפְתָּא


But he said, You shall not pass through. And Edomea came out to meet him with a large army and with a strong hand.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:21)


וְסָרֵיב אֱדוֹמָאָה לְמִשְׁבּוֹק יַת יִשְרָאֵל לִמְעִבַּר בִּתְחוּמֵיהּ וּסְטָא יִשְרָאֵל מִלְוָותֵיהּ דַהֲווֹ מַפְקְדִין מִן קֳדָם מֵימְרָא דִשְׁמַיָא דְלָא לִמְסַדְרָא עִמְהוֹן סִדְרֵי קְרָבָא מְטוֹל דְעַד כְּדוֹן לָא מָטָא זִמְנָא לְמִתְיַהֲבָא נִקְמָתָא דֶאֱדוֹם בִּידֵיהוֹן


So Edomea would not suffer Israel to pass through his coast; and Israel turned away from him, because it was commanded from before the Word of the Heavens that they should not set battle in array against them, forasmuch as the time was not yet come when the punishment of Edom should be given into their hands.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:22)


וּנְטָלוּ מֵרְקָם וְאָתוּ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא לְטַוְורוֹס אוּמָנוֹס


And the whole congregation of the children of Israel journeyed from Rekem, and came unto Mount Umanom.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:23)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה בְּטַוְורוֹס אוּמָנוֹס עַל תְּחוּם אַרְעָא דֶאֱדוֹם לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake unto Mosheh in the Mount Umanom, on the coast of the land of Edom, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:24)


יִתְכְּנֵישׁ אַהֲרן לְעַמֵיהּ אֲרוּם לָא יֵיעוֹל לְאַרְעָא דִיהָבִית לִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מְטוֹל דְסָרֵבְתּוּן עַל מֵימְרִי בְּמֵי מַצוּתָא


Aharon shall be gathered unto his people; for he shall not enter into the land which I have given unto the children of Israel, because you were rebels against My Word at the Waters of Contention.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:25)


דְבַר יַת אַהֲרן וְיַת אֶלְעָזָר בְּרֵיהּ וְאַסֵיק יַתְהוֹן לְטַוְורוֹס אוּמָנוֹס


Take Aharon and Elazar his son, and make them come up to Mount Umanom.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:26)


וְאַשְׁלַח יַת אַהֲרן יַת לְבוּשׁוֹי אִיקַר כְּהוּנְתָּא וְתַלְבִּישׁוּנוּן יַת אֶלְעָזָר בְּרֵיהּ וְאַהֲרן יִתְכְּנֵישׁ וִימוּת תַּמָן


And thou shalt strip Aharon of his vestments, the adornment (glory) of the priesthood, and put them on Elazar his son; but Aharon shall be gathered, and die there.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:27)


וְעָבַד משֶׁה הֵיכְמָא דְפַקֵיד יְיָ יָתֵיהּ וּסְלִיקוּ לְטַוְורוֹס אוּמָנוֹס כַּד חָמָן כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא


And Mosheh did as the Lord commanded him. And they ascended Mount Umanom, in the view of all the congregation.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:28)


וְאַשְׁלַח משֶׁה יַת אַהֲרן יַת לְבוּשׁוֹי אִיקַר כְּהוּנְתָּא וְאַלְבֵּישׁ יַת אֶלְעָזָר בְּרֵיהּ וּמִית אַהֲרן תַּמָן בְּרֵישׁ טַוְוָרא וּנְחַת משֶׁה וְאֶלְעָזָר מִן טַוְורָא


And Mosheh stripped Aharon of his vestments, the priestly decoration, and put them on Elazar his son; and Aharon died there on the summit of the mountain and Mosheh and Elazar came down from the mount.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 20:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/20:29)


וְכֵיוַן דְנַח נַפְשֵׁיהּ דְאַהֲרן אִסְתַּלָקוּ עֲנָנָא יְקָרָא בְּחַד לְיַרְחָא דְאָב וְחָמוּן כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא דְאָתָא משֶׁה נָחִית מִן טַוְורָא מָנוֹי בְּזִיעִין וַהֲוָה בָּכֵי וַאֲמַר וַוי לִי עֲלָךְ אַהֲרן אָחִי עַמוּד צְלוֹתְהוֹן דְיִשְרָאֵל אוּף הִינוּן בָּכוֹן יַת אַהֲרן תְּלָתִין יוֹמִין גוּבְרַיָא וּנְשַׁיָא דְיִשְרָאֵל


And when the soul of Aharon was at rest, the Cloud of Glory was lifted up on the first day of the month Ab; and all the congregation beheld Mosheh come down from the mountain with rent garments; and he wept and said, Woe unto me, for thee, my brother Aharon, the pillar of Israel's prayers! And they too wept for Aharon thirty days, the men and the women of Israel.



## Chapter 21

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:1)


וּשְׁמַע עֲמָלֵק דַהֲוָה שָׁרֵי בְּאַרַע דָרוֹמָא וְאָתָא וְאִשְׁתַּנֵי וּמָלַךְ בַּעֲרַד אֲרוּם נַח נַפְשֵׁיהּ דְאַהֲרן וְאִסְתַּלֵק עֲמוּדָא דַעֲנָנָא דַהֲוָה מַדְבַּר בִּזְכוּתֵיהּ קֳדָם עַמָא בֵּית דְיִשְרָאֵל וַאֲרוּם אָתֵי יִשְרָאֵל אוֹרַח אַלָלַיָא אֲתַר דְמָרְדוּ בְּמָארֵי עַלְמָא דְכַד תָּבוּ אַלָלַיָא הֲווֹ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל שַׁרְיָין בִּרְקַם וַהֲדָרוּ לְבַּתְרֵיהוֹן מִן רְקַם עַד מוֹסֵרוֹת שִׁית מַשׁוֹרְיַין אַרְבְּעִין שְׁנִין נְטָלוּ מִן מוֹסֵרוֹת וְתָבוּ לִרְקַם אוֹרַח מְאַלְלַיָא וְאָתוּ לְטַוְורוֹס אוּמָנוֹס וּמִית אַהֲרן תַּמָן הָא בְכֵן אָתָא וַאֲגַח קְרָבָא בְּיִשְרָאֵל וְשָׁבָא מִנְהוֹן שִׁבְיָא רַבָּא


And Amalek, who had dwelt in the south, and changed, and came and reigned in Arad, heard that the soul of Aharon was at rest, that the pillar of the Cloud which for his sake had led the people of the house of Israel had been taken up, and that Israel was coming by the way of the explorers to the place where they had rebelled against the Lord of the world. For, when the explorers had returned, the children of Israel abode in Rekem, but afterward returned from Rekem to Motseroth, in six encampments during forty years, when they journeyed from Motseroth, and returned to Rekem by the way of the explorers, and came unto Mount Umanom, where Aharon died; (and,) behold, he came and arrayed battle against Israel, and captured some of them with a great captivity.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:2)


וְקַיֵים יִשְרָאֵל קְיַים קֳדָם יְיָ וַאֲמַר אִין מִמְסַר תִּמְסַר יַת עַמָא הָדֵין בִּידִי וְאִגְמַר יַת קִירְוֵיהוֹן


And Israel vowed a vow before the Lord and said, If Thou wilt indeed deliver this people into my hand, I will destroy their cities.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:3)


וְקַבֵּיל יְיָ צְלוֹתֵיהּ דְיִשְרָאֵל וּמְסַר יַת כְּנַעֲנָאָה וּגְמַר יַתְהוֹן וְיַת קִירְוֵויהוֹן וּקְרָא שְׁמָא דְאַתְרָא חָרְמָה


And the Lord heard Israel's prayer, and delivered up the Kenaanites, and he destroyed them and their cities. And he called the name of the place Hormah.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:4)


וּנְטָלוּ מִטַוְורוֹס אוּמָנוֹס מַהֲלַךְ יַמָא דְסוּף לְאַחֲזָרָא יַת אַרְעָא דֶאֱדוֹם וּקְנָטַת נַפְשָׁא דְעַמָא בְּאָרְחָא


And they journeyed from Mount Umanom, by the way of the Sea of Suph, that they might compass the land of Edom; and the soul of the people was wearied in the way.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:5)


וְהִרְהֲרוּ עַמָא בִּלְבָבֵיהוֹן וְאִשְׁתָּעִיאוּ עַל מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ וּבְמשֶׁה נָצוּ לְמָא אַסֵיקְתָּנָא מִמִצְרַיִם לִמְמַת בְּמַדְבְּרָא אֲרוּם לֵית לַחֲמָא וְלֵית מַיָא וְנַפְשָׁתָנָא קַנְטַת בְּמַנָא הָדֵין דִמְזוֹנֵיהּ קָלִיל


And the people thought (wickedly) in their heart, and talked against the Word of the Lord, and contended with Mosheh, saying: Why didst thou bring us up from Mizraim to die in the wilderness; for there is neither bread nor water, and our soul is weary of manna, this light food?


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:6)


בְּרַת קָלָא נַפְלַת מִן שְׁמֵי מְרוֹמָא וְכֵן אָמְרַת אִיתוּן חָמוּן כָּל בְּנֵי נְשָׁא כָּל טַבְיָין דַעֲבָדִית לְעַמָא אַסֵיקִית יַתְהוֹן פְּרִיקִין מִמִצְרַיִם אָחֵיתִית לְהוֹן מַנָא מִן שְׁמַיָא וּכְדוּן חָזְרוּ וְאִתְרַעֲמוּ עֲלוֹי וְהָא חִיוְיָא דְגָזְרִית עֲלוֹי מִן יוֹמֵי שֵׁירוּי עַלְמָא עָפָר יֶהֱוֵי מְזוֹנֵיהּ וְלָא אִתְרְעַם עִלַוַי וְעַמִי אִתְרַעֲמוּ עַל מְזוֹנֵיהוֹן וּכְדוּן יֵיתוּן חִיוָון דְלָא אִתְרַעֲמוּ עַל מְזוֹנֵיהוֹן וִינַכְתוּן יַת עַמָא דִי אִתְרַעֲמוּ עַל מְזוֹנֵיהוֹן בְּכֵן גָרֵי מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ בְּעַמָא יַת חִיוָון חוּרְמָנִין וּנְכִיתוּ יַת עַמָא וּמִיתוּ אוֹכְלוֹסִין סַגִיאִין מִיִשְרָאֵל


And the bath-kol fell from the high heaven, and thus spake: Come, all men, and see all the benefits which I have done to the people whom I brought up free out of Mizraim. I made manna come down for them from heaven, yet now turn they and murmur against Me. Yet, behold, the serpent, whom, in the days of the beginning of the world, I doomed to have dust for his food, hath not murmured against me: but My people are murmuring about their food. Now shall the serpents who have not complained of their food come and bite the people who complain. Therefore did the Word of the Lord send the basilisk serpents, and they bit the people, and a great multitude of the people of Israel died.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:7)


וְאָתוּ עַמָא לְוַת משֶׁה וַאֲמָרוּ חַבְנָא אֲרוּם הַרְהִירְנָא וְאִשְׁתָּעִינָא בִיקַר שְׁכִינְתָּא דַיְיָ וְעִמָךְ נָצֵינָא צַלִי קֳדָם יְיָ וְיַעֲדִי מִינָנָא יַת מְחַת חִוְיָא וְצַלִי משֶׁה עַל עַמָא


And the people came to Mosheh, and said: We have sinned, in thinking and speaking against the glory of the Lord's Shekinah, and in contending with thee. Pray before the Lord to remove the plague of serpents from us. And Mosheh prayed for the people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:8)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה עִיבַד לָךְ חִיוְיָא דִנְחָשָׁא וְשַׁוִי יָתֵיהּ עַל אֲתַר תְּלֵי וִיהֵי כָּל דִנְכִית יָתֵיהּ חִיוְיָא וִיהֵי מִסְתַּכֵּל בֵּיהּ וְחָאִי אִין מְכַוִין לִבֵּיהּ לְשׁוּם מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ


And the Lord said to Mosheh, Make thee a serpent of brass, and set it upon a place aloft; and it shall be that when a serpent hath bitten any one, if he behold it, then shall he live, if his heart be directed to the Name of the Word of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:9)


וַעֲבַד משֶׁה חִיוְיָא דִנְחָשָׁא וְשַׁוִי יָתֵיהּ עַל אֲתַר תְּלֵי וַהֲוֵי כַּד נְכִית חִיוְיָא יַת גַבְרָא וַהֲוָה מִסְתַּכֵּל בְּחִיוְיָא דִנְחָשָׁא וּמְכַוֵין לִבֵּיהּ לְשׁוּם מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ וְחָאִי


And Mosheh made a serpent of brass, and set it upon a place aloft; and it was, when a serpent had bitten a man, and the serpent of brass was gazed at, and his heart was intent upon the Name of the Word of the Lord, he lived.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:10)


וּנְטָלוּ מִתַּמָן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וּשְׁרוֹ בְּאוֹבוֹת


And the children of Israel journeyed from thence, and pitched in Oboth;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:11)


וּנְטָלוּ מֵאוֹבוֹת וּשְׁרוֹ בְּמֵשְׁרֵי מְגִזְתָא בְּמַדְבְּרָא אָתַר דִמְכֻוָן עַל אַנְפֵּי מוֹאָב מִמַדְנַח שִׁמְשָׁא


and they journeyed from Oboth, and encamped in the plain of Megistha, in a desert place which looketh toward Moab from the rising of the sun.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:12)


מִתַּמָן נְטָלוּ וּשְׁרוֹ בְּנַחְלָא דְמִירְבֵּי חִילְפֵי וְגַלִי וְסִיגְלֵי


Thence they journeyed and encamped in a valley abounding in reeds, osiers, and mandrakes.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:13)


וּמִתַּמָן נְטָלוּ וּשְׁרוֹ מִן לְהָאֵל לְאַרְנוֹן בְּמַעְבְּרָא דִבְמַדְבְּרָא דְנָפִיק מִתְּחוּם אֱמוֹרָאָה אֲרוּם אַרְנוֹן תְּחוּם מוֹאָב מְמֻצָע בֵּין מוֹאָב וּבֵין אֱמוֹרָאָה וְיַתְבִין בֵּיהּ כּוּמְרָנַיָא פַּלְחֵי טַעֲוַותְהוֹן


And they journeyed from thence, and encamped beyond the Arnon, in a passage of the desert that stretcheth from the coast of the Amoraah; for Arnon is the border of Moab, situate between Moab and the Amoraah; and therein dwelt a priesthood of the worshippers of idols.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:14)


עַל כֵּן יִּתְאַמַר בְּסֵפֶר אוֹרַיְיתָא דְתַמָן כְּתִיבִין קְרָבַיָא דַיְיָ אֶת וָהֵב דַהֲווֹן בְּעִלְעוּלָא דִסְגִירוּתָא וּטְרִידוּן בְּסוֹף מַשְׁרִיתָא הִינוּן בִּשְרוּ יַת יִשְרָאֵל דֶאֱדוֹם וּמוֹאָב אִיטְמָרוּ בֵּינֵי טַוְרַיָא לְמִכְמַן וּלְשֵׁיצָאָה עַמָא בֵּית יִשְרָאֵל וּמָארֵי עַלְמָא רָמַז לְטַוְרַיָא וְקָרִיבוּ דֵין לְדֵין וּמִיתוּ וַאֲרַמְהוֹן הֲוָה נְגִיד בְּנַחֲלָא סְמִיךְ לְאַרְנוֹן


Therefore it is said in the book of the Law, where are recorded the wars of the Lord: Eth and Heb, who had been smitten with the blast of the leprosy, and had been banished beyond the confine of the camp, made known. to Israel that Edom and Moab were concealed among the mountains in ambush, to destroy the people of the house of Israel. But the Lord of the world made a sign to the mountains, which pressed one to another so that they died: and their blood flowed through a valley on the brink of the Arnon (or, a valley adjoining Arnon).


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:15)


וְשִׁפְכוּת נַחֲלַיָא דְאַדְמֵיהוֹן הֲוָה נְגִיד עַד מוֹתְבוֹת לְחָיַית בְּרַם אִיהִי אִשְׁתֵּזִיבַת מִן דֵין גְמִירָא עַל דְלָא הֲוַת בְּעֵיטַתְהוֹן וְהָא הִיא לִתְחוּם מוֹאָב


And the effusion of the streams of their blood flowed to the habitations of Lechaiath, which were, however, delivered from this destruction, because they had not been in their counsels; and, behold, it was unto the confine of Moab.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:16)


וּמִתַּמָן אִתְיְהִיבַת לְהוֹן בֵּירָא הִיא בֵּירָא דְאָמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה כְּנוֹשׁ יַת עַמָא וְאֶתֵּן לְהוֹן מוֹי


And from thence was given to them (the Israelites) the living well, the well concerning which the Lord said to Mosheh, Assemble the people and give them water.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:17)


הָא בְּכֵן שַׁבַּח יִשְרָאֵל יַת שְׁבַח שִׁירָתָא הֲדָא בִּזְמַן דְאִתְכַּסְיַית וְהַדְרַת בֵּירָא דְאִתְיְהִיבַת לְהוֹן בִּזְכוּתָא דְמִרְיָם סוּקִי בֵירָא סוּקִי בֵירָא הֲווֹן מְזַמְרִין לָהּ וְהִיא סַלְקָא


Then, behold, Israel sang the thanksgiving of this song, at the time that the well which had been hidden was restored to them through the merit of Miriam: Spring up, O well, spring up, O well ! sang they to it, and it sprang up:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:18)


בֵּירָא דְחָפָרוּ יָתָהּ אַבְהַת עַלְמָא אַבְרָהָם יִצְחָק וְיַעֲקב רַבְרְבָנַיָא דְמִלְקַדְמִין חָפְרוּ יָתָהּ רֵישֵׁי עַמָא משֶׁה וְאַהֲרן סַפְרֵיהוֹן דְיִשְרָאֵל מַשְׁכוּ יָתָהּ בְּחוּטְרֵיהוֹן וּמִמַדְבְּרָא אִתְיְהִיבַת לְהוֹן לְמַתָּנָא


the well which the fathers of the world, Abraham Izhak, and Jakob digged: the princes who were of old digged it, the chiefs of the people: Mosheh and Aharon, the scribes of Israel, found it with their rods; and from the desert it was given to them for a gift.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:19)


וּמִן דְאִתְיְהִיבַת לְהוֹן לְמַתָּנָא חָזְרַת לְמֵיסוֹק עִמְהוֹן לְטַוְרָא רָמַיָא וּמִטַוְרָא רָמַיָא נַחֲתָא עִמְהוֹן לִגְלִימָתָא מְחַזְרָא לְכָל מַשִׁרְיָיתָא דְיִשְרָאֵל וּמַשְׁקְיָא יַתְהוֹן כָּל חַד וְחַד בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכְּנֵיהּ


And from thence it was given to them in Mattana; turning, it went up with them to the high mountains, and from the high mountains it went down with them to the hills surrounding all the camp of Israel, and giving them drink, every one at the door of his tent.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:20)


וּמִטַוְרַיָא רָמַיָא נַחְתָא עִמְהוֹן לִגְלִימָתָא עֲמִיקְתָא וְאִתְגְנִיזַת עִמְהוֹן בִּתְחוּמֵיהוֹן דְמוֹאֲבָאֵי רֵישׁ רָמָתָא דִמְדִיקָא כָּל קֳבֵיל בֵּית יְשִׁימוֹן מְטוֹל פִּתְגָמֵי אוֹרַיְיתָא


And from the high mountains it descended with them to the lower hills, but was hidden from them on the borders of Moab, at the summit of the hill looking toward Bethjeshimon, because there they neglected the words of the Law.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:21)


וְשָׁדַר יִשְרָאֵל עִזְגַדִין לְוַת סִיחוֹן מַלְכָּא דֶאֱמוֹרָאָה לְמֵימָר


Then sent Israel messengers to Sihon, king of the Amorites, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:22)


אֲעִיבַּר בְּאַרְעָךְ לָא נְאַנוֹס אֲרִיסַן וְלָא נְשַׁרְגָן בְּתוּלָן וְלָא נִבְעוֹל נְשֵׁי גוּבְרִין אוֹרַח מַלְכָּא דְבִשְׁמַיָא נֵזֵיל עַד דְנֵיעִיבַר תְּחוּמָךְ


I would pass through thy country. We will not carry off the betrothed, nor seduce virgins, nor have to do with the wives of men; by the highway of the King who is in the heavens we will go, until we have passed through thy border.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:23)


וְלָא שְׁבַק סִיחוֹן יַת יִשְרָאֵל לְמֶעֱבַר בִּתְחוּמֵיהּ וּכְנַשׁ סִיחוֹן יַת כָּל עַמֵיהּ וּנְפַק לִקְדָמוּת יִשְרָאֵל לְמַדְבְּרָא וְאָתָא לְיָהַץ וְאַגַח קְרָבָא בְּיִשְרָאֵל


But Sihon would not permit Israel to pass through his limit, but constrained all his people, and came out to Jahaz, and made war against Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:24)


וּמְחוֹהִי יִשְרָאֵל בִּשְׁמָתָא דַיְיָ דְקַטְלָא כְּפִתְגַם דְחָרֶב וְיָרִית יַת אַרְעֵיהּ מֵאַרְנוֹנָא עַד יוּבְקָא עַד תְּחוּם בְּנֵי עַמוֹן אֲרוּם תַּקִיף הֲוָת רַבַּת תְּחוּם בְּנֵי עַמוֹן וְעַד כְּדוֹן אִית לְהוֹן אָרְכָּא


And Israel smote him with the anathema of the Lord, that he would destroy (him) with the edge of the sword; and he took possession of his country, from Arnon unto the Jabbok, unto the border of the children of Ammon; because Rabbath, which is the limit of the children of Ammon, was strong; and so far was their boundary.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:25

וּנְסֵיב יִשְרָאֵל יַת כָּל קִרְוַיָא הָאִלֵין וִיתֵיב יִשְרָאֵל בְּכָל קִרְוֵי אֱמוֹרָאֵי בְּחֶשְׁבּוֹן וּבְכָל כַּפְרָנָהָא


And Israel took all those cities, and dwelt in all the cities of the Amorites, in Heshbon, and in all her villages.

אֲרוּם חֶשְׁבּוֹן קַרְתָּא דְסִיחוֹן מַלְכָּא דֶאֱמוֹרָאָה הִיא וְהוּא אַגַח קְרָבָא בְּמַלְכָּא דְמוֹאָב קַדְמָאָה וּנְסֵיב יַת כָּל אַרְעֵיהּ מִן יְדֵיהּ עַד אַרְנוֹן


For Heshbon was the city of Sihon, king of the Amorites; for he had beforetime made war with the King of Moab, and had taken all his country from his hand unto the Arnon.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:27)


עַל כֵּן יֵימְרוּן בְּחִידְתָא מְתוּלַיָיא אָמְרִין צַדִיקַיָא דְשָׁלְטִין בְּיִצְרֵיהוֹן אִיתוּ וְנַחְשׁוֹב זֵיינָא דְעוּבְדָא טָבָא כֻּלוֹ קֳבֵיל אַגְרָא וַאֲגַר עוֹבָדָא בִּישָׁא כֻּלוֹ קֳבֵיל זֵיינָא יִתְבְּנֵי וְיִשְׁתַּכְלַל דְמִיתְעַר וּמְשִ¦יחַ בְּאוֹרַיְיתָא


Therefore, say the young men, (or the chosen ones,) using proverbs: The righteous who rule their passions say, Come let us reckon (Heshbon) the strength of a good work by the recompense, and the recompense of an evil work by the strength for whoso is watchful and diligent in the law is builded up and perfected;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:28)


אֲרוּם מִילִין תַּקִיפִין הֵי כְאֵישָׁא נַפְקִין מִפּוּמֵי צַדִיקַיָא מָרֵי חוּשְׁבְּנָא דְנָא וְזַכְוַון חֲסִינַן הֵי כְשַׁלְהוֹבִיתָא מֵאִלֵין דְקַרְיָין וּמְשִ¦יחִין בְּאוֹרַיְיתָא אָכְלַת אֵישְׁתְּהוֹן לְסַנָאָה וְלִבְעֵיל דְבָבָא דְמִתְחַשְׁבִין קוּמֵיהוֹן הֵי כְפִלְחֵי בַּמְתֵי טַעֲוָותָא דְנַחֲלֵי אַרְנוֹנָא


for mighty words like fire go forth from the lips of the righteous, the masters of such thought, (calculation, heshbona,) and powerful merit like flames from those who are read and devoted in the law: their fire devoureth the foe and the adversary, who are reckoned before them as the worshippers of the idol altars in the valley of Arnona.

יְיָא לְכוֹן סַנְאֵי צַדִיקַיָא אֲבַדְתּוּן עַמָא דִכְמִישׁ מִדְסֵינַת פִּתְגָמֵי אוֹרַיְיתָא לֵית לְהוֹן תַּקַנְתָּא עַד דְיֵיצְרְכוֹן לְמִגְלֵי לַאֲתַר דְיִלְפוּן אוֹרַיְיתָא וּבְנֵיהוֹן וּבְנַתְהוֹן מִתְרַחֲקָן בְּשִׁבְיַית חַרְבָּא כָּל קֳבֵל מִתְמָלְכִין בְּמוֹלְכָנָא דְאוֹרַיְיתָא אֱמוֹרָאִין וּמְשִיחִין בְּאוֹרַיְיתָא


Woe to you, ye haters of the just! ye have perished, ye people of Kemosh, haters of the words of the law, in whom there is no righteousness, unless he waste you to bring you captive unto the place where they teach. the law, and their sons and daughters be removed by captivity of the sword to be near them who consult in its counsels the instructors and those anointed with the law.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:30)


אֲמִירִין רַשִׁיעַיָא לֵית רְמָא וּמְנַטְלָא חָמֵי כָּל דָא עָבַד חוּשְׁבַּנְכוֹן עַד דִיסוּף כִּדְבוֹנָא נַפְשֵׁיכוֹן וּמָרֵי עַלְמָא יִצְדֵי יַתְהוֹן עַד דִיפַח נַפְשֵׁיהוֹן וְיִצְדוּן כְּמָא דְאַצְדֵי קִרְוֵי אֱמוֹרָאֵי וּפַלְטִירֵי רַבָּנֵיהוֹן מִן תַּרְעָא רַבָּא דְבֵית מַלְכוּתָא עַד שׁוּקָא דִנְפָחַיָא דִסְמִיךְ עַד מֵידְבָא


The wicked have said, In all this there is nothing lofty to the sight; but your numbers shall perish until the falsehood of your souls be ended, and the Lord of the world destroy them till their lives have expired, and they have come to nothing, as the cities of the Amorites have perished, and the palaces of their princes from the great gate of the house of the kingdom to the street of the smiths which is nigh to Medeba.

וִיתִיבוּ יִשְרָאֵל בָּתַר דְקָטִילוּ יַת סִיחוֹן בְּאַרְעָא דֶאֱמוֹרָאֵי


And Israel, after they had destroyed Sihon, dwelt in the land of the Amorites.

וְשָׁדַר משֶׁה יַת כָּלֵב וְיַת פִּנְחָס לְאַלָלָא יַת מַכְבַּר וּכְבָשׁוּ כּוּפְרָנָהָא וְשֵׁיצוּן יַת אֱמוֹרָאֵי דְתַמָן


And Mosheh sent Kaleb and Phineas to examine Makbar, and they subdued the villages, and destroyed the Amorites who were there.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:33

וְאִתְפְּנִיאוּ וּסְלִיקוּ אוֹרַח מַתְנָן וּנְפַק עוֹג מַלְכָּא דְמַתְנָן לִקְדָמוּתָנָא הוּא וְכָל עַמֵיהּ לְאַגָחָא קְרָבָא לְאֶדְרֶעִי


Then they turned, and went up by the way of Mathnan; and Og, the king of Mathnan, came out to meet us, he and all his people, to give battle at Edrei.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:34)


וַהֲוָה כֵּיוָן דְחָמָא משֶׁה יַת עוֹג זָע וְאַרְתֵּית מִן קֳדָמוֹי עָנֵי וַאֲמַר דֵין הוּא עוֹג רַשִׁיעָא דַהֲוָה מַחְסִיד יַת אַבְרָהָם וְשָרָה אַבְהָתָן לְמֵימָר אַתּוּן מְדַמְיַן לְאִלָנִין שְׁתוּלִין עַל פִּרְקַטוּנִין דְמַיָין בְרַם פֵּירִין לֵית הִינוּן עַבְדִין בְּגִין כֵּן אַמְתִּין לֵיהּ קוּדְשָׁא בְּרִיךְ הוּא לִמְחַוְיָא לְדָרַיָא וְיֶחְמֵי מִן בְּנֵיהוֹן אוּכְלוּסִין סַגִיאִין לְאִתְמַסְרָא בִּידֵיהוֹן בְּכֵן אָמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה לָא תִדְחַל מִנֵיהּ אֲרוּם בִּידָךְ מְסָרִית יָתֵיהּ וְיַת כָּל עַמֵיהּ וְיַת כָּל אַרְעֵיהּ וְתַעֲבֵיד לֵיהּ הֵיכְמָא דְעָבַדְתָּ לְסִיחוֹן מַלְכָּא דֶאֱמוֹרָאֵי דַהֲוָה יָתֵיב בְּחֶשְׁבּוֹן


And it was, when Mosheh saw Og, he trembled before him, stricken with fear: but he (soon) answered and said, This is Og the Wicked, who taunted Abraham our father and Sarah, saying: You are like trees planted by the water channels, but bring forth no fruit: therefore hath the Holy One, blessed be He, spared him to live through generations, that he might see the great multitude of their children, and be delivered into our hands. Then spake the Lord unto Mosheh: Fear him not, for I have delivered him into thy hand, and all his people and country; and thou shalt do to him as thou hast done to Sihon, king of the Amorites, who dwelt in Heshbon.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 21:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/21:35)


וַהֲוָה כֵּיוַן דְחָמָא עוֹג רַשִׁיעָא יַת מַשִׁירְיָיתָא דְיִשְרָאֵל דַהֲוָה שִׁיתָּא פַּרְסֵי אָמַר לֵית אֲנָא מְסַדֵר סִדְרֵי קְרָבָא כֻּלוֹ קֳבֵיל עַמָא הָדֵין דִלְמָא יַעַבְדוּן לִי הֵיכְמָא דְעָבָדוּ לְסִיחוֹן וַאֲזַל וְעָקַר טַוְורָא בַּר שִׁיתָּא פַּרְסֵי וְאַחֲתֵיהּ עַל רֵישֵׁיהּ לְמִטְלַק עֲלֵיהוֹן מִן יַד זַמִין מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ זוֹחֲלָא וּפָכַר טַוְורָא וְנַקְרֵיהּ וְטָמַע רֵישֵׁיהּ בְּגַוֵיהּ בָּעָא לְמִשְׁלְפֵיהּ מִן רֵישֵׁיהּ וְלָא יָכִיל מִן בִּגְלַל דְמַשְׁכֵי כַּכֵיהּ וְשִׁינֵיהּ פּוּמֵיהּ הַלְכָא וְהַלְכָא אָזַל משֶׁה וְנָסַב נַרְגָא בְּרַת עִישַרְתֵּי אַמִין וְטָפַז עִישַרְתֵּי אַמִין וּמַחְיֵיהּ בְּקַרְסוּלֵיהּ וְנָפַל וּמִית מִן לְהָאֵל לְמַשְׁרִיתָא דְיִשְרָאֵל דְהָכִין כְּתִיב וּמְחוֹ יָתֵיהּ וְיַת בְּנוֹי וְיַת כָּל עַמֵיהּ עַד דִי לָא שְׁיָירוּ לֵיהּ מְשֵׁיזִיב וִירִיתוּ יַת אַרְעֵיהּ


Now it was, after Og the Wicked had seen the camp of Israel spreading over six miles he said with himself, I will make war against this people, that they may not do to me as they have done to Sihon: so went he and tare up a mountain six miles in size, and brought it upon his head to hurl it upon them. But the Word of the Lord forthwith prepared a reptile which ate into the mountain and perforated it, and his head was swallowed up within it; and he sought to withdraw it, but could not, because his back teeth and his front ones were drawn hither and thither. And Mosheh went and took an axe of ten cubits, and sprang ten cubits, and struck him on the ankle of his foot, and he fell, and died beyond the camp of Israel. Thus it is written. And they smote him and his sons and daughters, and all his people, till none of them remained to escape; and they took possession of his land.



## Chapter 22

And the children of Israel journeyed, and encamped in the plains of Moab, near the passage of the Jordan (toward) Jericho.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/22:2)

AND Balak bar Zippor saw what Israel had done to the Amoraee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/22:3)


וּדְחִילוּ מוֹאֲבָאֵי מִן קֳדָם עַמָא לַחֲדָא אֲרוּם סַגִי הוּא וְאִתְיַעֲקוּ מוֹאֲבָאֵי בְּחַיֵיהוֹן מִן קֳדָם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל


And the Moabaee feared before the people greatly because they were many, and they were distressed in their life before the sons of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/22:4)


וַאֲמַר מוֹאֲבָאֵי לְסָבֵי מִדְיָנָאֵי אֲרוּם עַמָא חַד וּמָלְכוּ חַד הֲווֹן עַד הַהוּא יוֹמָא כְּדוּן יְשֵׁיצוּן קְהָלָא יַת כָּל חָזְרָנוּתָא הֵיכְמָא דְמִבְעֵי תוֹרָא יַת עִיסְבָּא דְחַקְלָא וּבָלָק בַּר צִפּוֹר מִדְיָנָאָה מַלְכָּא לְמוֹאָב בְּעִידָנָא הַהִיא וְלָא בְּעִידָנָא חוֹרָנָא דְהָכִין הֲוָה תְנָאָה בֵּינַתְהוֹן לְמֶהֱוֵי מַלְכִין לְפִרְקִין מֵאִילַךְ וְאִילַךְ


And they said to the elders of the Midianee, for the people had been one and the kingdom one unto that day: Now will this congregation consume all that is about them, as the ox eateth up the grass of the field. And Balak bar Zippor, a Midianite, was the king of Moab at that time; without (a Midianite) being such at another time; for so was the tradition among them, to have kings from this people and from that, by turns.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 22:5

And he sent unto Laban the Aramite, who was Bileam, (so called because he it was) who sought (Biluva) to swallow up (Amma) the people of the house of Israel: the son of Beor, who was insane from the vastness of his knowledge; and would not spare Israel, the descendants of his sons and daughters: and the house of his habitation in Padan was at Pethor, a name signifying an interpreter of dreams. It was built in Aram upon the Phrat, in a land where the children of the people worshipped and adored him. (To him did Balak send) to call him, saying: Behold, a people hath come out of Mizraim, and, lo, they cover the face of the earth, and are encamped over against me.
