These you shall offer before the Lord in the time of your festivals, beside your vows which you vow at the festival, and which you shall bring on the day of the feast, with your free-will oblation for your burnt sacrifice, your mincha, libations, and consecrated victims.



## Chapter 30

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:1)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל כְּכָל מַה דְפַקֵיד יְיָ יַת משֶׁה


And Mosheh spake to the sons of Israel, according to all that the Lord had commanded Mosheh.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:2)


וידבר וּמַלֵיל משֶׁה עִם אֲמַרְכְּלֵי שִׁבְטַיָא לִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְמֵימָר דֵין פִּתְגָמָא דְמַלֵיל יְיָ לְמֵימָר


AND Mosheh spake with the chiefs of the Tribes of the Beni Israel, saying: This is the Word which the Lord hath spoken, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:3)


גְבַר בַּר תְּלֵיסַר שְׁנִין אֲרוּם יִדַר נִדְרָא קֳדָם יְיָ אוֹ יְקַיֵים קְיַים לְמֵיסַר אִיסַר מִמַדַעַם דְהֶתֵּירָא עַל נַפְשֵׁיהּ לָא יָפִיס פִּתְגָמֵיהּ בְּרַם בֵּית דִינָא שָׁרַן לֵיהּ וְאִין לָא שָׁרוּ לֵיהּ כְּכָל מַה דְיִפּוֹק מִפּוּמֵיהּ יַעֲבֵיד


A man, a son of thirteen when he shall have vowed a vow before the Lord, or have sworn an oath, saying, I will withhold from such a thing which is permitted to me, shall not be allowed to relax his word (at his own will): nevertheless, the house of judgment (beth dina) can absolve him; but if they absolve him not, whatsoever hath gone out of his mouth he shall perform.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 30:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:4)


וְאִתְּתָא דְלָא עָבְרַת תְּרֵיסַר שְׁנִין אֲרוּם תִּידַר נְדַר קֳדָם יְיָ וְתֵיסַר אִיסַר בְּבֵית אִיבָהָא עַד תְּרֵיסַר שְׁנִין


And a female who hath not passed twelve years when she hath vowed a vow before the Lord, and hath bound herself in her father's house until her thirteenth year;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 30:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:5)


וְיִשְׁמַע אִיבָהָא יַת נִדְרָהָא וְכָל אִיסְרָהָא דְאָסְרַת עַל נַפְשָׁהּ וְיִתְכַּוֵון וְיִשְׁתּוֹק לָהּ אִיבָהָא וְיִתְקַיְימוּן כָּל נִדְרָהָא וְכָל אִיסָרָא דְאַסָרַת עַל נַפְשָׁהּ יִתְקַיֵים


and her father hear her vow, and whatever bond she hath bound upon her soul, and her father be acquiescent, and speak not to her; then every vow and every bond which she hath bound upon her soul shall be confirmed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 30:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:6)


וְאִין יִבְטַל אִיבָהָא יָתָהּ בְּיוֹמָא דְשָׁמַע אוֹ לָא אִתְכַּוֵון לְקַיְימָא וּבַטֵיל בָּתַר דְיִשְׁמַע כָּל נִדְרָהָא וְאֵיסָרָא דְאַסָרַת עַל נַפְשָׁהּ לָא יִתְקַיְימוּן וּמִן קֳדָם יְיָ יִשְׁתְּרֵי וְיִשְׁתְּבֵק לָהּ אֲרוּם בָּטֵיל אָבוּהָא יָתָהּ מִן רְשׁוּתָא דְנִדְרָא


But if her father prohibit her on the day that he heareth, or, not being prepared to confirm, annulleth after he hath heard, (then) no vow or bond that she hath bound upon her soul shall be confirmed; but is remitted and forgiven her before the Lord, because her father hath made her free from the authority of the vow, (or, nullified to her the power of the vow.)


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 30:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:7)


וְאִין אִתְנַסְבָא אִתְנְסִיבַת לִגְבַר וְנִדְרָהָא עֲלָהּ אוֹ פֵּירוּשֵׁי סִיפְתָהָא דְאָסְרַת עַל נַפְשָׁהּ בְּבֵית אִיבָהָא וְלָא בְטִיל אִיבָהָא עַד לָא אִתְנְסִיבַת וּמִדְאִתְנְסִיבַת לִגְבַר יִתְקַיְימוּן


And if when she hath been taken by a husband a vow be upon her, or her lips have expressed that which is binding upon her soul while in her father's house, and her father had not absolved her while unmarried, then, when she hath been married, it shall be confirmed.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 30:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:8)


וְאִין בָּתַר דְאִתְנְסִיבַת נְדָרַת וְיִשְׁמַע בַּעֲלָהּ וּבְיוֹמָא דְשָׁמַע יִתְכַּוֵון לְקַיְימוּתְהוֹן וְיִשְׁתּוֹק לָהּ וְיִתְקַיְימוּן נִדְרָהָא וְאִיסְרָהָא דְאַסָרַת עַל נַפְשָׁהּ יִתְקַיְימוּן


But if after she is married she make a vow, and her husband hear it, and on the day that he heareth it he is minded to confirm it, and is silent to her, then the vow and the bond which she hath bound upon her soul shall be ratified.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 30:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:9)


וְאִין בְּיוֹמָא דְשָׁמַע בַּעֲלָהּ בָּטִיל לָהּ וְיִשְׁרֵי יַת נִדְרָהּ דַעֲלָהּ וְיַת פֵּירוּשׁ סִפְתָהָא דַאֲסָרַת עַל נַפְשָׁהּ וּמִן קֳדָם יְיָ יִשְׁתְּרֵי וְיִשְׁתְּבֵק לָהּ


But if her husband prohibit her on the day that he heareth, then the vow which is upon her, and the utterance of her lips which bound her soul, are remitted and forgiven her.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 30:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:10)


וְנִדְרָא דְאַרְמְלָא וּמִתַרְכָא כָּל דְאַסָרַת עַל נַפְשָׁהּ יְקוּם עֲלָהּ


Yet the vow of a widow, or a divorced, whatever hath bound her soul, shall be confirmed upon her.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 30:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:11)


וְאִין עַד דְהִיא בְּבֵית בַּעֲלָהּ וְלָא בָּגְרַת נָדְרַת אוֹ אָסְרַת אִסַר עַל נַפְשָׁהּ בְּקִיוּמָא


But if, while she was in her husband's house, or while she had not attained to marriage years, she had vowed, or bound her soul with the bond of an oath


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 30:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:12)


וְיִשְׁמַע בַּעֲלָהּ וְיִשְׁתּוֹק לָהּ וְלָא בָטִיל יָתָהּ וּמִית עַד דְלָא בָּגְרַת וְיִתְקַיְימוּן כָּל נִדְרָהָא וְכָל אִיסְרֵי דַאֲסָרַת עַל נַפְשָׁהּ יִתְקַיְימוּן וְלֵית אִיבָהָא זָכֵי בָהּ תּוּב לְבַטְלוּתְהוֹן


which her husband had heard of, and had neither spoken nor prohibited her, or had died before she was married, then all her vows shall be confirmed, and all the obligations with which she had bound her soul be ratified, and her father shall have no power to absolve her.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 30:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:13)


וְאִין מִישְׁרָא יִשְׁרֵי יַתְהוֹן בַּעֲלָהּ בְּיוֹמָא דְשָׁמַע כָּל אַפְקוּת שִפְתָהָא לְנִדְרָהָא וּלְאִיסְרֵי נַפְשָׁהָא לָא יִתְקַיְימוּן וְאִין בַּעֲלָהּ בְּטֵילִינוּן וְלָא יַדְעַת וַעֲבָרַת מִן קֳדָם יְיָ יִשְׁתְּבֵק לָהּ


But if her husband released her on the day that he heard, then, whatever her lips had pronounced to be a vow, or a bond upon her soul, shall not be confirmed; and if her husband had annulled them, and she, not knowing, had performed, it shall be forgiven her before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 30:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:14)


כָּל נִדְרָא וְכָל קִיוּם אִסְרָא לְסַגָפָא נְפַשׁ בַּעֲלָהּ יְקַיְימִינוּן וּבַעֲלָהּ יְבַטֵילִינוּן


Every vow, every oath-bond to chasten the soul, her husband may ratify or annul.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 30:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:15)


וְאִין מִשְׁתַּק יִשְׁתּוֹק וּמִתְכַּוֵון לָהּ בַּעֲלָהּ מִיוֹמָא דְשָׁמַע לְיוֹמָא חוֹרַן וְיִתְקַיְימוּן כָּל נִדְרָהָא אוֹ יַת כָּל אִיסְרָהָא דַעֲלָהּ וּבְמִשְׁתּוּקֵיהּ קְיַים יַתְהוֹן אֲרוּם שָׁתִיק לָהּ בְּיוֹמָא וְאִתְכַּוֵון וְלָא שְׁרִינוּן בְּיוֹמָא דְשָׁמַע


But if her husband was silent and consented when he heard from one day to the next, then all her vows and all the bonds upon her are ratified; by his silence he hath confirmed them; for be was silent to her on the day, and consented, and absolved her not on the day that he heard.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 30:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:16)


וְאִין מֵישְׁרָא יִשְׁרֵי לָהּ יוֹמָא חַד בָּתַר דְשָׁמַע לָא סַגְיָא לָהּ בְּשֵׁירוּתָא וְאִין מַפְסָא פִתְגָמָא בַּעֲלָהּ אוֹ אִיבָהּ מְקַבֵּל יַת חוֹבָהּ


But if, absolving, he would absolve her one day after he had heard, there is no force in the absolution; and if he then nullify the word, her husband or her father shall bear her sin.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 30:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/30:17)


אִלֵין אַחֲוָויַת קְיָימַיָא דְפַקֵיד יְיָ יַת משֶׁה בֵּין גְבַר לְאִתְּתֵיהּ בֵּין אִיבָא לִבְרַתֵּיהּ בְּיוֹמֵי טַלְיוּתָא עַד דְהִיא בְּבֵית אִיבָהָא וְלָא בְּיוֹמֵי טַלְיוּתָא וְהִיא בְּבֵית בַּעֲלָהּ


These are the publications of the statutes which the Lord commanded Mosheh (on these matters) between a man and his wife, and a father and his daughter in the day of her youth in her father's house; but not in the time of her youth, and she be in the house of her husband.



## Chapter 31

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/31:1)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/31:2)


אִתְפְּרַע פּוּרְעָנוּת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מִן מִדְיָנָאֵי וּמִן בָּתַר כְּדֵין תִּתְכְּנֵשׁ לְעַמָךְ


Take retribution for the children of Israel from the Midianites; and afterward thou shalt be gathered to thy people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/31:3)


וּמַלֵיל משֶׁה עִם עַמָא לְמֵימָר אִזְדַרְזוּ לְוַותְכוֹן גוּבְרִין לְחֵילָא וִיהוֹן מְסַדְרִין סִדְרֵי קְרָבָא עַל מִדְיָן לְמִתַּן פּוּרְעָנוּת עַמָא דַיְיָ בְּמִדְיָן


And Moses spake with the people, saying: Arm of you men, for the host to make war against Midian, to give the people of the Lord avengement upon Midian;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 31:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/31:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/31:4)


אַלְפָא לְשִׁבְטָא אַלְפָא לְשִׁבְטָא לְכָל שִׁבְטַיָא דְיִשְרָאֵל תְּשַׁדְרוּן לְחֵילָא


a thousand of each tribe of all the tribes of Israel send ye to the war.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 31:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/31:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/31:5)


וְאִתְבְּחָרוּ גוּבְרִין צַדִיקִין וּמְסָרוּן נַפְשְׁהוֹן מֵאַלְפַיָא דְיִשְרָאֵל אַלְפָא לְשִׁבְטָא תְּרֵיסַר אַלְפִין מְזַרְזֵי חֵילָא


And of the thousands of Israel fit men were chosen who gave up themselves, a thousand of a tribe, twelve thousand, armed for the war.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 31:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/31:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/31:6)


וּשְׁדַר יַתְהוֹן משֶׁה אַלְפָא לְשִׁבְטָא לְחֵילָא יַתְהוֹן וְיַת פִּנְחָס בַּר אֶלְעָזָר כַּהֲנָא לְחֵילָא וְאוּרַיָא וְתֻמַיָא דְקוּדְשַׁיָא לִמְשַׁיְילָא בְּהוֹן וַחֲצוֹצְרוֹת יַבָּבָא בִּידֵיהּ לְמִכְנַשׁ וּלְמִשְׁרֵי וּלְמֵיטַל מַשְׁרִיתָא דְיִשְרָאֵל


And Mosheh sent them, a thousand of each tribe to the war, them and Phinehas bar Elazar the priest unto the war, with the Uraia and Thummaia consecrated to inquire for them, and the Jubilee trumpets in his hand for assembling, encamping, and ordering forward the host of Israel.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 31:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/31:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/31:7)


וְאִתְחַיָילוּן עַל מִדְיָן אַקְפוּהָ מִתְּלַת טְרִיגוּנָהָא הֵיכְמָא דְפַקֵיד יְיָ יַת משֶׁה וּקְטָלוּ כָּל דְכוּרָא


And they warred against Midian, circumventing them from three corners, as the Lord had instructed Mosheh, and they killed every male;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 31:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/31:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/31:8)


וְיַת מַלְכֵי מִדְיָנָאֵי קְטָלוּ עַל קְטוּלֵי מַשִׁרְיַיתְהוֹן יַת אֱוִי וְיַת רֶקֶם וְיַת צוּר הוּא בָּלָק וְיַת חוּר וְיַת רֶבַע חֲמֵשֶׁת מַלְכֵי מִדְיָן וְיַת בִּלְעָם בַּר בְּעוֹר קְטָלוּ בְּסַיְיפָא וַהֲוָה כֵּיוַן דְחָמָא בִּלְעָם חַיָיבָא יַת פִּינְחָס כַּהֲנָא רָדִיף מִן בַּתְרוֹי עָבַד מִלְתָא דְקוּסְמִין וּפָרַח בְּאַוִיר שְׁמַיָא מִן יַד אִידְכַּר פִּינְחָס שְׁמָא רַבָּא וְקַדִישָׁא וּפָרַח בַּתְרוֹי וְאַחְדֵיהּ בְּרֵישֵׁיהּ וְאַחְתֵיהּ שְׁלַף סַיְיפָא וּבְעָא לְמִקְטְלֵיהּ פָּתַח פּוּמֵיהּ בְּמִילֵי תַחֲנוּנִין וַאֲמַר לְפִינְחָס אִין תְּקַיֵים יַת נַפְשִׁי מִשְׁתַּבַּעְנָא לָךְ דְכָל יוֹמִין דַאֲנָא קְיַים לֵית אֲנָא מְלַטֵיט יַת עַמָךְ עָנֵי וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ הֲלָא אַנְתְּ הוּא לָבָן אֲרַמָאָה דְבָעִית לְמֵישֵׁיצַיָא יַת יַעֲקב אָבוּנָן וּנְחָתַת לְמִצְרַיִם בְּגִין לְמוֹבְדָא זַרְעָא וּמִן בָּתַר דִנְפָקוּ מִמִצְרַיִם גָרֵיתָא בְּהוֹן עֲמָלֵק רַשִׁיעָא וּכְדוֹן אִיתְגְרָתָא אִיתְגְרָא לְמֵילוֹט יַתְהוֹן וְכֵיוַן דְחָמִיתָא דְלָא אַהַנְיַן עוֹבָדָךְ וְלָא קַבֵּיל מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ מִינָךְ אִמְלִיכַת מַלְכָּא בִּישָׁא יַת בָּלָק לְמוּקְמָא יַת בְּנָתֵיהּ בְּפַרְשַׁת אוֹרְחָתָא לְמִטְעַיָא יַתְהוֹן וּנְפָלוּ בְּגִין כֵּן מִנְהוֹן עֶשְרִין וְאַרְבְּעָא אַלְפִין בְּגִין כֵּן לֵית אֶפְשַׁר תּוּב לִמְקַיְימָא יַת נַפְשָׁךְ וּמִן יַד שָׁלַף סַיְיפֵיהּ מִן תְּיָקָא וְקַטְלֵיהּ


and they slew the kings of the Midianites with the slain of their armies, Evi, Rekem, Zur, who is Balak, and Hur and Reba, five kings of Midian; and Bileam bar Beor they killed with the sword. And it was when Bileam the guilty saw Phinehas the priest pursuing him, he made use of his magical arts, (lit., made words of enchantment,) and flew in the air of the heavens; but Phinehas forthwith pronounced the Great and Holy Name, and flew after him, and seized him by his head, and bringing him down drew the sword, and sought to kill him; but he opened his mouth with words of deprecation, and said to Phinehas: If thou wilt spare my life, I swear to thee that all the days I live I will not curse thy people. He answered him, and said: Art thou not Laban the Amarite who didst seek to destroy Jakob our father, who wentest down into Mizraim, to destroy his children, and, after they had come out of Mizraim, didst send the wicked Amalek against them; and hast thou not now been sent to curse them? But after thou hadst seen that thy works did not prosper, and that the Word of the Lord would not hear thee, thou didst give the evil counsel to Balak to set his daughters in the way to make them go wrong, when there fell of them twentyfour thousand. Therefore, it cannot be that thy life may be spared; and at once he drew the sword and slew him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 31:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/31:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/31:9)


וּשְׁבוּ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל יַת נְשֵׁיהוֹן דְמִדְיָנָאֵי וְיַת טַפְלֵיהוֹן וְיַת כָּל בְּעִירֵיהוֹן וְיַת כָּל גֵיתֵיהוֹן וְיַת כָּל נִיכְסֵיהוֹן בָּזוּ


And the sons of Israel led captive the wives of the Midianites, their children, their cattle, and all their flocks, and destroyed all their goods;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 31:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/31:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/31:10)

but they took all the spoil and the prey both of men and beasts,

but every female child you shall stand before the Crown of Holiness, (the priest's tiara,) and look upon her: she who is not a virgin will be pallid in the face, but she who is a virgin child will blush in the face, like fire; them you shall spare.

וְקָרִיבְנָא דוֹרוֹנָא לִשְׁמָא דַיְיָ כֵּיוַן דְמָסַר יְיָ יַת מִדְיָנָאֵי בִידֵינָן וּכְבַשְׁנָן יַת אַרְעֲהוֹן וְיַת מְדִינַתְהוֹן וַהֲוֵינָן עַיְילִין לִטְרַקְלִינֵיהוֹן וְחַמְיַין בְּנָתֵיהוֹן יָאֲתָא חַטְיָיתָא מְפַרְנְקָתָא וְכָל גְבַר דַהֲוָה מַשְׁכַּח עֲלֵיהוֹן מָאנִין דִדְהַב הֲוָה שָׁרֵי כְּלִילַיָא מִן רֵישֵׁיהוֹן קַדִישַׁיָא מִן אוּדְנֵיהוֹן קַטְלַיָא מִן צַוְורֵיהוֹן שִׁירַיָא מִן אֶדְרָעֵיהוֹן עִזְקָתָא מִן אֶצְבְּעָתֵיהוֹן מְחוּכְיָא מִבֵּית חַדְיֵיהוֹן וּבְכָל דָא חַס לָן לְמִיתְלֵי עֵינָן בָּן וְלָא אִסְתַּכְּלוּן בַּחֲדָא מִנְהוֹן דְלָא לִמְחַיְיבָא בַּחֲדָא מִנְהוֹן וְלָא נְמוּת בְּמִתוּתָא דְמַיְיתוּן בָּהּ רַשִׁיעַיָא לְעַלְמָא דְאָתֵי וְדָא יַידְכַּר לָן לְיוֹם דִינָא רַבָּא לִמְכַפְּרָא עַל נַפְשָׁתָן קֳדָם יְיָ


And we have brought a gift unto the Name of the Lord, forasmuch as the Lord hath delivered the Midianites into our hands, and we have been able to subdue their land and their cities. And we entered into their chambers, and there saw their daughters, fair, tender, and delicate; and every man who found on them jewels of gold, loosened the coronets from their heads, the earrings from their ears, the necklaces from their necks, the bracelets from their arms, the rings from their fingers, and the brooches from their bosoms;-but in all this we abstained from lifting our eyes upon themselves, or gazing on one of them, lest we should sin with any one of them, and die the death which the wicked die in the world to come. And may this be had in memorial for us in the day of the great judgment, to make propitiation for our souls before the Lord.

## Chapter 32

וַאֲזָלוּ בְּנֵי מָכִיר בַּר מְנַשֶׁה לְגִלְעָד וְכַבְּשׁוּהָ וּתְרִיכוּ יַת אֱמוֹרָאֵי דְבָהּ


And the sons of Makir bar Menasheh went to Gilead and subdued it, and drave out the Amoraee who were therein.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 32:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/32:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/32:40)


וִיהַב משֶׁה יַת גִלְעָד לְמָכִיר בַּר מְנַשֶׁה וִיתֵב בָּהּ


And Mosheh gave Gilead to Makir bar Menasheh, and he dwelt in it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 32:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/32:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/32:41)


וְיָאִיר בַּר מְנַשֶׁה אָזַל וּכְבַשׁ יַת כַּפְרָנֵיהוֹן וּקְרָא יַתְהוֹן כּוּפְרָנֵי יָאִיר


And Jair bar Menasheh went and subdued their villages, and called them the villages of Jair.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 32:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/32:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/32:42)


וְנוֹבַח אָזַל וּכְבַשׁ יַת קְנָת וְיַת כּוּפְרָנָהָא וּקְרָא לָהּ נוֹבַח עַל שְׁמֵיהּ


And Nobach went and subdued Kenath and its villages, and called it Nobach, after his own name.



## Chapter 33

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:1)


אלה אִלֵין מַטְלָנֵי בְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל דִי נְפָקוּ מֵאַרַע דְמִצְרַיִם לְחֵילְוַתְהוֹן כַּד אִתְעֲבִידוּ לְהוֹן נִיסִין עַל יַד משֶׁה וְאַהֲרן


These are the journeys of the Beni Israel who came out from Mizraim by their hosts, after the miracles had been wrought for them by the hand of Mosheh and Aharon.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:2)


וּכְתַב משֶׁה יַת מַפְקָנֵיהוֹן לְמַטְלָנֵיהוֹן עַל מֵימַר דַיְיָ וְאִלְיֵין מַטְלָנֵיהוֹן לְמַפְקָנֵיהוֹן


And Mosheh recorded their outgoings by their journeys by the Word of the Lord; and these are their journeys by their goings forth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:3)


וּנְטָלוּ מִן פִּילוּסִין בְּיַרְחָא דְנִיסָן בַּחֲמֵשַׁת עַסְרָא יוֹמָא לְיַרְחָא קַדְמָאָה מִבָּתַר דְאָכְלוּ נִכְסַת פִּסְחָא נְפָקוּ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל בְּרֵישׁ גְלֵי לְמֵחְמֵיהוֹן דְכָל מִצְרָאֵי


They departed from Pelusin in the month of Nisan, on the fifteenth day of the month; after they had eaten the sacrifice of the Pascha did the children of Israel go forth, with uncovered head, in sight of all the Mizraee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:4)


וּמִצְרָאֵי מְקַבְּרִין יַת דְקָטַל יְיָ בְּהוֹן כָּל בּוּכְרָא וּבְטַעֲוַותְהוֹן עָבַד מֵימַר דַיְיָ דִינִין טַעֲוַות מַתְּכָא הֲווֹ מִתְרַכְּכִין טַעֲוַות אַבְנָא מִתְגַדְעִין טַעֲוַות פַּחְרָא מִתְעַבְּדִין בְּקִיקִין טַעֲוִוין דְעָא מִתְעַבְּדִין קְטוֹם וְדִבְעִירֵי מַיְיתוּן


And the Mizraee buried those whom the Lord had killed among them, even all the first-born; and upon their idols did the Word of the Lord do judgments; their molten idols were dissolved, their idols of stone were mutilated, their idols of earthenware broken in pieces, their wooden idols turned to ashes, and their cattle gods were slain with death.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:5)


וּנְטָלוּ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מִן פִּילוּסִין וּשְׁרוֹ בְּסֻכּוֹת אַתְרָא דְאִתְחַפִּיאוּ שִׁבְעַת עֲנָנֵי יְקָרָא


And the sons of Israel went forth from Pelusin, and encamped in Sukkoth, a place where they were protected by seven glorious clouds.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:6)


וּנְטָלוּ מִסֻכּוֹת וּשְׁרוֹ בְּאֵיתָם דְבִסְטַר מַדְבְּרָא


And they removed from Sukkoth, and encamped in Etham, on the side of the wilderness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:7)


וּנְטָלוּ מֵאֵיתָם וְתָבוּ עַל פּוּמֵי חִירָתָא מְרַבְעָתָא דִקְדָם טַעֲוַות צְפוֹן וּשְׁרוֹ קֳדָם מִגְדוֹל


They removed from Etham, and returned unto Pumey Hiratha, which lie in front of the idol of Zephon, and encamped before Migdol.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:8)


וּנְטָלוּ מִפּוֹרוֹקֵי חֵירָתָא וַעֲבָרוּ בְּגוֹ יַמָא וּנְפָקוּ מִן יַמָא וַאֲזָלוּ עַל כֵּיף יַמָא כְּנִישִׁין אוּנְכִין וּמַרְגַלְיַין וַאֲזָלוּ מִבָּתַר כְּדֵין מַהֲלַךְ תְּלָתָא יוֹמִין בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְאֵיתָם וּשְׁרוֹ בְּמָרָה


And from the caravansaries of Hiratha they removed, and passed through the midst of the sea, and went upon the shore of the sea, collecting onyx stones and pearls. Afterwards they proceeded three days' journey in the wilderness of Etham, and encamped in Marah.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:9)


וּנְטָלוּ מִמָרָה וַאֲתוֹ לְאֵילִם וּבְאֵילִם תַּרְתֵּיסְרֵי עִינְוָון דְמַיָין לִתְרֵיסַר שִׁבְטִין וְשׁוּבְעִין דִיקְלִין כֻּלוֹ קֳבֵיל שׁוּבְעִין חַכִּימַיָא וּשְׁרוֹ תַמָן עַל מַיָא


And they removed from Marah, and came to Elim; in Elim were twelve fountains of water for the twelve tribes, and seventy palm trees, answering to the seventy sages; and they encamped there by the waters.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:10)


וּנְטָלוּ מֵאֵילִם וּשְׁרוֹ עַל גֵיף יַמָא דְסוּף


And they removed from Elim, and camped on the banks of the Sea of Suph;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:11)


וּנְטָלוּ מִגֵיף יַמָא דְסוּף וּשְׁרוֹ בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְסִין


and they removed from the banks of the sea, and encamped in the wilderness of Sin;

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:12)


וּנְטָלוּ מִמַדְבְּרָא דְסִין וּשְׁרוֹ בְּדָפְקָה


thence to Dopheka,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:13)


וּנְטָלוּ מִדָפְקָה וּשְׁרוֹ בִכְרַךְ תַּקִיף


Kerak Takiph (the strong tower),


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:14)


וּנְטָלוּ מֵאֲתַר תַּקִיף וּשְׁרוֹ בִּרְפִידִים וּמְטוֹל דְרָפִין יְדֵיהוֹן מִפִּתְגָמֵי אוֹרַיְיתָא לָא הֲוָה תַמָן מוֹי לְמִשְׁתֵּי לְעַמָא


Rephidim, where, because their hands were (raphin) neglectful of the words of the law, there was no water for the people to drink;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:15)


וּנְטָלוּ מֵרְפִידִים וּשְׁרוֹ בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְסִינָי


thence to the wilderness of Sinai;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:16)


וּנְטָלוּ מִמַדְבְּרָא דְסִינָי וּשְׁרוֹ בְּקִבְרֵי דִמְשַׁאֲלֵי בִּישְרָא


thence to the Graves of those who desired flesh;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:17)


וּנְטָלוּ מִקִבְרֵי דִמְשַׁיְילֵי בִּישְרָא וּשְׁרוֹ בַּחֲצֵרוֹת אַתְרָא דְאִסְתַּגְרַת מִרְיָם נְבִיאֲתָא


thence to Hazeroth, where Miriam the prophetess was struck, with leprosy;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:18)


וּנְטָלוּ מֵחֲצֵרוֹת וּשְׁרוֹ בְּרִיתְמָא אֲתַר דִמְרַבֵּי אִילָנֵי רְתָמֵי


thence to Rithema, the place of many juniper trees;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:19)


וּנְטָלוּ מֵאֲתַר דִמְרַבֵּי אִילָנֵי רְתָמֵי וּשְׁרוֹ בְּרוּמָנָא דְמַתְקֵיף פֵּירוֹי


thence to Rumana, whose fruit is hard;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:20)


וּנְטָלוּ מֵרוֹמָנָא דְמַתְקֵיף פֵּירוֹי וּשְׁרוֹ בְּלִבְנָא אֲתַר דִתְחוּמִין לָהּ מַלְבֵּינְתָּא בִּנְיַין


thence to Libnah, whose borders are built of bricks (Iibnetha);


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:21)


וּנְטָלוּ מִלִבְנָא וּשְׁרוֹ בְּבֵית רִיסָא


thence to Beth Rissa;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:22)


וּנְטָלוּ מֵרִיסָא וּשְׁרוֹ בְּמַקְהֵלָת אֲתַר דְאִתְכַּנָשׁוּ קרַח וְסִיַעֲתֵּיהּ עַל משֶׁה וְאַהֲרן


thence to Kehelath, where Korach and his companions banded together against Mosheh and Aharon;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:23)


וּנְטָלוּ מִקְהֵלָת וּשְׁרוֹ בְּטַוְורָא דְשַׁפִּירַן פֵּירוֹי


thence to the mountain whose fruit is good;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:24)


וּנְטָלוּ מִטַוְורָא דְשַׁפִּירַן פֵּירוֹי וּשְׁרוֹ בַּחֲרָדָה אֲתַר דְתָוְוהוּ עַל בִּישָׁתָא דְמוֹתָנָא


thence to Harada, where they were confounded by the evil plague;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:25)


וּנְטָלוּ מֵחֲרָדָה וּשְׁרוֹ בְּמַקְהֵלוֹת אֲתַר כְּנוּפְיָא


thence to Makheloth, the place of congregation;

thence to the lower Makheloth;

thence to Tharach,

and Muka, whose waters were sweet;

וּנְטָלוּ מֵאֲתַר דְבַסִימִין מוֹהִי וּשְׁרוֹ בְּחַשְׁמוֹנָה


thence to Hasmona;

וּנְטָלוּ מֵחַשְׁמוֹנָה וּשְׁרוֹ בַּאֲתַר מַרְדוּתָא


thence to Meredotha, the place of rebellion (or chastisement);

thence to Bere-Haktha,

Gudgad, at the Rocks,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:33)

Jotebath, a good and quiet place;

thence to the Fords;

thence to Tarnegolla, the tower of the cock;

thence to the wilderness of Zin; at the Iron Mount, which is Rekem;

thence to Mount Umano, on the borders of the Land of Edom.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:38)

And Aharon the priest went up to Mount Umano by the Word of the Lord, and died there, in the fortieth year from the going out of the children of Israel from Mizraim, in the fifth month, on the first of the month.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:39)

And Aharon was one hundred and twenty-three years old when he died on Mount Umano.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:40)


וּשְׁמַע עֲמָלֵק חַיָיבָא וְאִתְחַבֵּר בִּכְנַעֲנָאֵי וּמְלַךְ בַּעֲרָד וּבֵית מוֹתְבֵיהּ בְּאַרַע דָרוֹמָא כַּד אָתוּ בְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְאַגִיחַ בְּהוֹן וּגְמָרוּ יַתְהוֹן וְיַת קִירְוֵויהוֹן


And Amalek the wicked, who was combined with the Kenaanites, and reigned in Arad,-the house of his abode was in the land of the south,-heard that the sons of Israel were coming to wage war against them, and utterly to destroy their cities.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:41)


וּנְטָלוּ מִטַוְורוֹס אוּמָנוֹס וּשְׁרוֹ בְּצַלְמוֹנָה אֲתַר חוֹבָאֵי וְכוּר בְּאַרַע אֱדוֹמָאֵי וְתַמָן עָקַת נַפְשָׁא דְעַמָא בְּאוֹרְחָא


And they removed from Mount Umano, and encamped in Zalmona, a place of thorns, and narrow (or squalid), in the land of the Edomaee; and there the soul of the people was distressed on account of the way;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:42)


וּנְטָלוּ מִצַלְמוֹנָה וּשְׁרוֹ בְּפוּנוֹן אֲתַר דְגָרֵי יְיָ בְּהוֹן יַת חֵיוַן קָלָן וְסִילְקַת קְבֵילְתְּהוֹן עַד צֵית שְׁמַיָא


thence to Punon, where the Lord sent burning serpents among them, and their cry went up to heaven.

וּנְטָלוּ מֵאֲתַר דְגָרֵי יְיָ בְהוֹן יַת חֵיוַן קָלָן וּשְׁרוֹ בְּאוֹבוֹת


And they removed to Oboth;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:44)

thence to the passage of the Fords, on the border of the Moabaee;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:45)


וּנְטָלוּ מִמְגִיזְתָּא וּשְׁרוֹ בְּדִיבוֹן בֵּית מַזְלָא


thence to Dibon, the place of fortune;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:46

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:46](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:46)


וּנְטָלוּ מִדִיבוֹן בֵּית מַזְלָא וּשְׁרוֹ בְּעַלְמוֹן דִבְלָתָיְמָה אוּף תַּמָן אִתְכַּסְיַית מִנְהוֹן בֵּירָא עַל דִשְׁבָקוּ פִתְגָמֵי אוֹרַיְיתָא דְבַסִימִין כְּדִבְלָתָא


thence to Almon Diblathaimah, where the well was hidden from them, because they had forsaken the words of the law, which are as delicious as figs (diblatha);


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:47

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:47](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:47)


וּנְטָלוּ מֵעַלְמוֹן דִבְלָתָיְמָה וּשְׁרוֹ בְּטַוְורָא עִבְרָאֵי קֳדָם בֵּית קְבוּרְתֵּיהּ דְמשֶׁה


thence to the Mount Ibraee, in front of the place of the burial of Mosheh;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:48

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:48](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:48)


וּנְטָלוּ מִטַוְורֵי עִבְרָאֵי וּשְׁרוֹ בְּמֵישְׁרַיָא דְמוֹאָב עַל יוֹרְדְנָא דִירֵיחוֹ


thence they removed and encamped in the fields of Moab, by Jordan, near Jericho;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:49

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:49](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:49)


וּשְׁרוֹ עַל יוֹרְדְנָא מִבֵּית יְשִׁימוֹת עַד מֵישַׁר שִׁיטִין בְּמֵישְׁרַיָא דְמוֹאָב


and they encamped by the Jordan, from Bethjeshimon unto the plain of Sillan in the fields of Moab.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:50

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:50](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:50)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה בְּמֵישְׁרַיָא דְמוֹאָב עַל יוֹרְדְנָא דִירֵיחוֹ לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, in the fields of Moab, at the Jordan, by Jericho, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:51

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:51](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:51)


מַלֵיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן אֲרוּם אַתּוּן עָבְרִין יַת יוֹרְדְנָא לְאַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן


Speak with the sons of Israel, and say to them: When you have passed over the Jordan into the land of Kenaan,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:52

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:52](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:52)


וּתְתַרְכוּן יַת כָּל יַתְבֵי אַרְעָא מִן קֳדָמֵיכוֹן וְתִסְפוּן יַת כָּל בֵּית סִגְדֵיהוֹן וְיַת כָּל צַלְמֵי מַתְּכַוַותְהוֹן תְּסַיְיפוּן וְיַת כָּל בַּמְסֵיהוֹן תְּשֵׁיצוּן


you shall drive out all the inhabitants of the country from before you, and lay waste all the houses of their worship, destroy all their molten images, and overthrow all their high places.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:53

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:53](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:53)


וּתְתַרְכוּן יַת יַתְבֵי אַרְעָא וְתֵיתְבוּן בָּהּ אֲרוּם לְכוֹן יְהָבִית יַת אַרְעָא לְמֵירוּת יָתָהּ


And you shall drive out the inhabitants of the land, and dwell therein, for I have given you the land to possess it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:54

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:54](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:54)


וְתַחְסְנוּן יַת אַרְעָא בְּעַדְבִין לִגְנִיסַתְכוֹן לְשֵׁיבַט דְעַמֵיהּ סַגִין תַּסְגֵי וּלְשֵׁיבַט דְעַמֵיהּ זְעִירִין תַּזְעֵיר לִדְיִפּוֹק לֵיהּ תַּמָן עַדְבָא דִילֵיהּ יְהֵי לְשִׁבְטָא אֲבָהַתְכוֹן תִּתְחַסְנוּן


And you shall inherit the land by lots, according to your families; to the tribe whose people are many you shall enlarge, and to the tribe whose people are few, you shall diminish. According to the place where one's lot falleth, there shall his place be; you shall inherit by the tribes of your fathers.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:55

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:55](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:55)


וְאִין לָא תְּתַרְכוּן יַת כָּל יַתְבֵי אַרְעָא מִן קֳדָמֵיכוֹן וִיהֵי מַה דִתְשַׁיְירוּן מִנְהוֹן לְסָכְיַין בְּעֵינָא בִּישָׁא בְּכוֹן וּמַקִיפִין לְכוֹן כִּתְרֵיסִין בְּסִיטְרֵיכוֹן וְיַתְעִיקִין יַתְכוֹן עַל אַרְעָא דְאַתּוּן יַתְבִין בָּהּ


But if you will not drive out the inhabitants of the land from before you, it will be that the residue whom you have spared looking at you with an evil eye will surround you as shields (terisin) on your sides, and afflict you in the land wherein you dwell;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 33:56

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:56](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/33:56)


וְהֵיכְמָא דַחֲשֵׁילִית לְמֶעֱבַד לְהוֹן אַעֲבֵיד לְכוֹן


and it shall be that as I had thought to do to them I will do to you.



## Chapter 34

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:1)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:2)


פַּקֵיד יַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן אֲרוּם אַתּוּן עָלִין לְאַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן דָא אַרְעָא דְתִתְפְּלֵיג לְכוֹן בְּאַחְסָנָא אַרְעָא דִכְנַעַן בִּתְחוּמָהָא


Command the sons of Israel, and say to them: When you have entered into the land of Kenaan, this shall be the land that shall be divided to you for an inheritance, the land of Kenaan by its limits.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 34:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:3)


וְיֶהֱוֵי לְכוֹן תְּחוּם דָרוֹמָא מִן מַדְבְּרָא דְצִינֵי טַוָר פַּרְזְלָא עַל תְּחוּמֵי אֱדוֹם וְיֶהֱוֵי תְּחוּם דָרוֹמָא מִן סְיָפֵי יַמָא דְמִלְחָא מַדִינְחָא


Your south border (shall be) from the Wilderness of Palms, by the iron mountain, at the confines of Edom, even the south border at the extremities of the Sea of Salt, eastward.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 34:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:4)


וְיַקִיף לְכוֹן תְּחוּמָא מִן דָרוֹמָא לְמִסֵיקְתָּא דְעַקְרַבִּים וִיעִיבַר לְצִינֵי טַוָר פַּרְזְלָא וִיהוֹן מַפְקָנוֹי מִן דָרוֹמָא לִרְקַם גֵיאָה וְיִפּוֹק לְטִירִית אַדְרַיָא וִיעִיבַר לְקֵיסַם


And your border shall turn from the south to the ascent of Akrabbith, and pass on to the palms of the mountain of iron, and the going forth thereof shall be southward of Rekem Giah, and shall go onward to the tower of Adar, and pass over to Kesam.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 34:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:5)


וְיַקִיף תְּחוּמָא מִקֵיסָם לְנִילוֹס דְמִצְרָאֵי וִיהוֹן מַפְקָנוֹי לְמַעֲרָבָא


And the border shall wind round from Kesam unto Nilos, of the Mizraee, and its outgoings shall be to the west.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 34:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:6)


וּתְחוּם מַעֲרָבָא וְיֶהֱוֵי לְכוֹן יַמָא רַבָּא אוֹקְיָינוּס וּתְחוּמֵיהּ הִינוּן מֵי בְּרֵאשִׁית עִם מַיָא קַדְמָאִין דְהַווֹן בְּגַוֵיהּ אַבִּירוֹי וּפַרְכִּידוֹי כִּרְכוֹי וּמַדִנְתֵּיהּ נִיסוֹי וּמְחוּזוֹי סְפִינְתֵּיהּ וְאַלְגַוְתֵּיהּ דֵין יֶהֱוֵי לְכוֹן תְּחוּם מַעַרְבַיָא


And for the western border you shall have the Great Ocean Sea; its limits are the waters of the beginning with the waters of old which are in its depth; its capes and havens, its creeks and its cities, its islands and ports, its ships and its recesses: this shall be your border westward.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 34:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:7)


וְדֵין יֶהֱוֵי לְכוֹן תְּחוּם צִפּוּנָא מִן יַמָא רַבָּא תְּכַוְונוּן לְכוֹן לְטַוְורוֹס אוּמָנוֹס


And this shall be your northern border; — from the Great Sea you shall appoint to you unto Mount Umanis.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 34:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:8)


מִטַוְורוֹס אוּמָנוֹס תְּכַוְונוּן לְכוֹן מַעֲלָךְ לִטְבַרְיָא וִיהוֹן מַפְקָנוֹי דִתְחוּמָא מִן תְּרֵי צִיטְרוֹי מְכַוְונִין לְכַרְכּוֹר דְבַר זַעֲמָה וּלְכַרְכּוֹר דְבַר סְנִיגוֹרָא וְדִוָוקִינוֹס וְתַרְנְגוֹלָא עַד קִסְרִין מַעֲלָךְ לְאָבְלַס דְקִילְקָאֵי


From Mount Umanis you shall appoint to you (a line) as thou goest up to the entrance of Tebaria, and the outgoings of the border at its two sides, unto Kadkor Of Bar Zahama, and to Kadkol of Bar Sanigora, and Divakinos and Tarnegola unto Kesarin, where thou goest up to Abelas of Cilicia.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 34:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:9)


וְיִפּוֹק תְּחוּמָא לְקֶרֶן זְכוּתָא וּלְגַבְתָּא דְחַטְמוֹנָא וִיהוֹן מַפְקָנוֹי לְכִירַיָא דְבֵית סָכָל וְלִמְצִיעוּת דָרָתָא רַבְּתָא דִמְמִצְעָא בֵּין טִירַת עִינְוְותָא לְבֵית דַרְמָשֶק דֵין יֶהֱוֵי לְכוֹן תְּחוּם צִיפּוּנָא


And the border shall go on unto Keren Zekutha, and to Gibra Hatmona, and its outgoings shall be at Keria Bethsekel, and to the midst of the great court (darela rabtha), which is at Mizeha, between the towers of Hinvetha and Darmeshek: this shall be your northern limit.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 34:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:10)


וּתְכַוְונוּן לְכוֹן לִתְחוּם מַדִינְחָא מִטֵירַת עִינְוְותָא לְאַפְמִיאָה


And you shall appoint your eastern border from the of Hinvetba unto Apamea;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 34:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:11)


וְיֵיחוֹת תְּחוּמָא מִן אַפְמִיאָה לִדְפַנֵי מִן מַדְנַח לְעֵינְוָותָא וְיֵיחוֹת תְּחוּמָא לִמְעָרַת סַנִיאָס וּמִמְעָרַת סַנִיאָס יֵיחוֹת תְּחוּמָא לְטַוָר תַּלְגָא וּמִטַוָר תַּלְגָא יֵיחוֹת תְּחוּמָא לְעֵינָן וּמֵעֵינָן יֵיחוֹת תְּחוּמָא וְיִסַב מֵישְׁרַיָא מֵישַׁר נַחֲלֵי אַרְנוֹנָא וְיֵיתֵי לְמַדְבְּרָא וְצִינֵי טַוָר פַּרְזְלָא מֵי מַצוּתָא אַבְּלוּ וּדְמוּכָא סְמִיךְ לִגְנִיסַת כְּרַךְ מַלְכוּתְהוֹן דֶאֱדוֹמָאֵי אַחֲסָנוּת שֵׁיבַט רְאוּבֵן וְגָד וּפַלְגוּת שֵׁיבַט מְנַשֶׁה וְיֵיחוֹת תְּחוּמָא וְיַקִיף לִתְחוּם יַמָא דִגְנֵיסַר מִמַדִינְחָא


and the border shall descend from Apamea to Dophne, eastward of Hinvetha; thence the border shall go down to the cavern of Panias, and from the cavern of Panias to the mountain of snow, and from the mountain of snow to Henan, and from Henan the border shall go down and encompass the plain of the river of Arnon, and arrive at the wilderness and the palms of the mountain of iron, take in the Waters of Contention, and rest at Ginesar, a city of the kings of the Edomites, the inheritance of the tribes of Reuben and Gad, and the half tribe of Menasheh; and the border shall descend and encompass the Sea of Genesar on the east.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 34:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:12)


וְיֵיחוֹת תְּחוּמָא לְיוֹרְדְנָא וְיֶהֱווֹן מִפְקָנוֹי לְיַמָא דְמִלְחָא רֵיקָם גֵיעָה מִן דָרוֹמָא טַוְורוֹס אוּמָנוֹס מִן צִפּוּנָא יַמָא רַבָּא מִן מַעַרְבָא יַמָא דְמִלְחָא מִן מַדִינְחָא דָא תֶּהֱוֵי לְכוֹן אַרְעָא דְיִשְרָאֵל לְמִצְרָנֵי תְחוּמָהָא חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר


And the border shall descend to the Jordan, and its outgoing be at the Sea of Salt. Rekem Giah on the south, Mount Umanos on the north, the Great Sea on the west, the Sea of Salt on the east,-this shall be your country, the Land of Israel, by the extent of its borders round about.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 34:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:13)


וּפַקֵיד משֶׁה יַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְמֵימָר דָא אַרְעָא דְתַחְסְנוּן יָתָהּ בְּעַדְבָא דְפַקֵיד יְיָ לְמִתַּן לְתִשְׁעַת שִׁבְטִין וּפַלְגוּת שִׁבְטָא


And Mosheh commanded the sons of Israel, saying: This is the land which you are to inherit by lot, which the Lord hath commanded to give to the nine tribes and the half tribe.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 34:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:14)


אֲרוּם קַבִּילוּ שִׁבְטָא דִבְנֵי רְאוּבֵן לְבֵית אֲבָהַתְהוֹן וְשִׁבְטָא דִבְנֵי גָד לְבֵית אֲבָהַתְהוֹן וּפַלְגוּת שִׁבְטָא דִמְנַשֶׁה קַבִּילוּ יַת אַחְסַנְתְּהוֹן


For the tribe of the children of Reuben, according to the house of their fathers, and tribe of Gad, and the half tribe of Menasheh have received their inheritance __

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/34:15)


תְּרֵין שִׁבְטִין וּפַלְגוּת שִׁבְטָא קַבִּילוּ אַחְסַנְתְּהוֹן מֵעִבְרָא לְיוֹרְדְנָא מַדִינְחָא


__ beyond the Jordan on the eastern side.

וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:

אִלֵין שְׁמָהַת גוּבְרַיָא דְיַחְסְנוּן לְכוֹן יַת אַרְעָא אֶלְעָזָר כַּהֲנָא וִיהוֹשֻׁעַ בַּר נוּן


These are the names of the men who shall make to you the inheritance of the land: Elazar the priest, and Jehoshua bar Nun,

וְאַמַרְכּוֹל חַד אַמַרְכּוֹל חַד מִן שִׁבְטָא תִתְבְּרוּן לְאַחְסָנָא יַת אַרְעָא


and one prince from each of the tribes you shall choose to give you the inheritance of the land.

And these are the names of the men. Of the tribe of Jehudah, Kaleb bar Jephunneh;

for Shemeon, Shemuel bar Ammihud;

Benjamin, Elidad bar Kiselon;

Dan, Buki bar Jageli;

Joseph, Menasheh, Haniel bar Ephod;

Ephraim, Kemuel bar Shipbtan;

Zebulon, Elizaphan bar Parnak;

Issakar, Paltiel bar Azan;

Asher, Abihud bar Shelomi;

Naphtali, Pedahael bar Ammihud.

## Chapter 35

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:1)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה בְּמֵישְׁרַיָא דְמוֹאָב עַל יוֹרְדְנָא דִירֵיחוֹ לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh in the plains of Moab, by Jordan-Jericho, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:2)


פַּקֵיד יַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְיֶחְסוּן לְלֵיוָאֵי מֵאַחְסָנַת אֲחוּדַתְהוֹן קִירְוִין לְמִיתַב וּפַרְוִילִין לְקִירְוַיָא חֲזוּרְנוּתְהוֹן תִּתְּנוּן לְלֵיוָאֵי


Command the sons of Israel that they give to the Levites from their inheritance cities to dwell in, and suburbs (open spaces) to the cities round about shall you give to the Levites.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:3)


וִיהוֹן קִרְוַיָא לְהוֹן לְמֵיתַב וּבְדוֹרְלְהוֹן יְהוֹן לִבְעִירֵיהוֹן וּלְקִנְיָינֵיהוֹן וּלְכָל צוֹרְכֵיהוֹן


And the cities shall be for them to dwell in, and the suburbs for their cattle, their property, and all their needful things.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:4)


וּפְדוֹרְוִילֵי קִרְוַיָא דְתִתְּנוּן לְלֵיוָאֵי חֲזוֹר קַרְתָּא וּלְבַר אַלְפָא גַרְמִידֵי חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר


But of the cities which you give to the Levites the suburbs round the city shalt be one thousand cubits without the city round about.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:5)


וְתִמְשְׁחוּן מִבָּרָא לְקַרְתָּא יַת צִיטְרָא דְמַדִינְחָא תְּרֵין אַלְפִין גַרְמִידֵי וְיַת רוּחַ דָרוֹמָא תְּרֵין אַלְפִין גַרְמִידֵי וְיַת רוּחַ מַעַרְבָא תְּרֵין אַלְפִין גַרְמִידֵי וְיַת רוּחַ צִיפוּנָא תְּרֵין אַלְפִין גַרְמִידֵי וְקַרְתָּא בְּמִיצְעָא דֵין יְהֵי לְכוֹן פְּדוֹרְלֵי קִרְוַיָא


And you shall measure outside the city, on the east side, two thousand cubits; on the South two thousatid, on the west two thousand, and on the north two thousand cubits, with the city in the midst; these shall be to you the suburbs of the cities.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:6)


וְיַת קִרְוַיָא דְתִתְּנוּן לְלֵיוָאֵי יַת שִׁית קוֹרְיַין דִקְלָטִין קָטוֹלָא לְמֵיעֲרוֹק לְתַמָן קָטוֹלָא וַעֲלֵיהוֹן תִּתְּנוּן אַרְבְּעִין וְתַרְתֵּין קוֹרְיָין


And of the cities you give to the Levites, six shall be for refuges to manslayers, that the manslayer may escape thither. Beside these you shall give them forty-two other cities.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:7)


כָּל קִירְוַיָא דְתִתְּנוּן לְלֵיוָאֵי אַרְבְּעִין וְתַמְנֵי קוּרְיַין יַתְהוֹן וְיַת פְּדוֹרְוַולְהוֹן


All the cities that you give to the Levites shall be forty-eight cities with their suburbs.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:8)


וְקוּרְיַין דְתִתְּנוּן מִן אַחֲסָנַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מִן שִׁבְטָא דְעַמֵיהּ סַגִי תַּסְגוּן וּמִן שִׁבְטָא דְעַמֵיהּ זְעֵיר תִּזְעֵרוּן גְבַר אֲגַב אַחֲסַנְתֵּיהּ דְיַחְסְנוּן יִתֵּן מִקִירְוֵיהּ לְלֵוָאֵי


But when you give the cities from the inheritance of the Beni Israel, from the tribe wbose people are many you shall give many, and from the tribe whose people are few you shall diminish; every one shall give of his cities to the Levites, according to the inheritance he possesses.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:9)


וּמַלֵיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר


And the Lord spake with Mosheh, saying:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:10)


מַלֵיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן אֲרוּם אַתּוּן עָבְרִין יַת יוֹרְדְנָא לְאַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן


Speak with the sons of Israel, and say to them, When you have passed over Jordan unto the land of Kenaan,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:11)


וּתְזַמְנוּן לְכוֹן קוּרְיַין בְּשׁוּקִין וּבָתֵּי חִיזְוָתָא קִרְוִין קַלְטַן יֶהֱוְיַן לְכוֹן וְיֶעְרוֹק לְתַמָן קָטוֹלָא דְיִקְטוֹל בַּר נַשׁ בְּשָׁלוּ


you shall provide you cities with streets and houses of living (boarding houses), cities of refuge shall they be to you, that thither the manslayer may flee who hath killed a man inadvertently.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:12)


וִיהוֹן לְכוֹן קִרְוַיָא לְמִקְלַט קָטוֹלָא מִן תְּבַע אַדְמָא וְלָא יְמוּת קָטוֹלָא עַד דְיֵיקוּם קֳדָם כְּנִשְׁתָּא לְדִינָא


And they shall be to you for cities of refuge for the manslayer from the avenger of blood, that the man may not be put to death till he shall have stood before the congregation for judgment.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:13)


וְקִרְוַיָא דְתִתְּנוּן שִׁית קִרְוִין קַלְטַן קָטוֹלָא יֶהֱוְיַן לְכוֹן


And these cities which yougive shall be six cities of refuge for the manslayer;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:14)


יַת תְּלַת קִרְוַיָא תִתְּנוּן מֵעִבְרָא לְיוֹרְדְנָא וְיַת תְּלַת קִרְוַיָא תִתְּנוּן בְּאַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן קִרְוִין קַלְטַן תֶּהֱוְיַן


three you shall appoint beyond Jordan, and three in the land of Kenaan; cities of refuge shall they be.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:15)


לִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וּלְגִיוֹרָא וּלְתוֹתְבַיָא דְבֵינֵיהוֹן יֶהֱוְיָן שִׁית קִרְוַיָא הָאִילֵן לְשֵׁזָבָא לְמֵעֲרוֹק לְתַמָן כָּל דְיִקְטוֹל בַּר נַשׁ בְּשָׁלוּ


For the sons of Israel and the sojourners among you shall be these six cities of refuge, that thither whoever hath killed a man through ignorance may flee.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:16)


וְאִין בְּמָנָא דְפַרְזְלָא בְּמַשְׁהוּ מְחָהִי וְקַטְלֵיהּ קָטוֹלָא הוּא אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵיל קָטוֹלָא


But if he smote him with an instrument of iron and killed him, he is a murderer; and the murderer shall be surely put to death.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:17)


וְאִין בְּאַבְנָא מְלוֹי יְדָא דִי כְמֵסַת דִי יְמוּת בָּהּ מְחוֹהִי וְקַטְלֵיהּ קָטוֹלָא הוּא אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵיל קָטוֹלָא


Or if, filling his hand with a stone large enough to kill any one, he struck him, and killed him, he is a murderer, and the murderer dying shall die.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:18)


אוֹ בְמָנָא דְקֵיסָא מְלוֹי יְדָא דִי כְמֵיסַת דִימוּת בָּהּ מְחוֹהִי וְקַטְלֵיהּ קָטוֹלָא הוּא אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵיל קָטוֹלָא


Or if, filling his hand with an instrument of wood sufficient to kill any one, he struck him, and killed him, he is a murderer; the murderer shall be put to death.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:19)


תְּבַע אַדְמָא הוּא יִקְטוֹל יַת קָטוֹלָא כַּד יְעַרְעִינֵיהּ מִבָּרָא לְקִרְוַיָא הָאִלֵין בְּדִינָא הוּא יְקַטְלִינֵיהּ


The avenger of blood may himself kill the manslayer, if he meet him outside of these cities; he may kill him in judgment.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:20)


וְאִין בִּסְנָא הֲדוֹף וְאִיתְכַוִין וְדַחְיֵיהּ אוֹ טְלַק עֲלוֹי כְּלוֹנְסַן וְשַׁרְוָותָא וְגַלְגִיל עֲלוֹי כֵּיפִין בְּכַוָונוּת לִבָּא וְקַטְלֵיהּ


But if (the manslayer) had assaulted in enmity and intentionally with a club or staff, or thrown stones upon him with purpose of heart, and killed him;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:21)


אוֹ נְטַר לֵיהּ בָּבוּי וּמְחוֹהִי בִּידֵיהּ וְקַטְלֵיהּ קָטוֹלָא הוּא אִיתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵיל קָטוֹלָא הוּא תְּבַע אַדְמָא יִקְטוֹל יַת קָטוֹלָא כַּד אִתְחְיַיב לֵיהּ


or cherishing enmity had struck him with his hand and killed him; he is a murderer; dying he shall die. The avenger of blood may slay the homicide when he hath been condemned.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:22)


וְאִין בְּשָׁלוּ בְּלָא נְטַר לֵיהּ בָּבוּ הֲדָפֵיהּ אוֹ טְלַק עֲלוֹי כָּל מָאן וְלָא אִתְכַוִין לְמִקְטְלֵיהּ


But if in ignorance, without keeping of malice, he let any thing fall upon him, having no intention to kill;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:23)


אוֹ בְּכָל אַבְנָא דְהִיא כְּמִיסַת דִימוּת בְּלָא מִתְכַוִין וְטָלַק עֲלוֹי מִדַעַם וְקַטְלֵיהּ וְהוּא לָא סָנֵי לֵיהּ וְלָא תָּבַע בִּשְׁתֵּיהּ


or if without intention he let a stone sufficient to kill any one, or any other thing, fall upon him, and kill him, without having hated, or purposed to do him harm,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:24)


וִידוּנוּן כְּנִשְׁתָּא בֵּין מַחְיָא וּבֵין תָּבַע אַדְמָא עַל סְדַר דִינַיָא הָאִילֵין


then the congregation shall judge between him who had smitten him, and the avenger of blood, according to these judgments;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:25)


וִישֵׁזְבוּן כְּנִשְׁתָּא יַת קָטוֹלַיָא מִן יַד תָּבַע אַדְמָא וִיתוּבוּן יָתֵיהּ כְּנִשְׁתָּא לְקִרְוַיָא דְקַלְטֵיהּ דְאַפָּךְ לְתַמָן וִיתֵיב בָּהּ עַד זְמַן דִימוּת כַּהֲנָא רַבָּא דְרַבֵּי יָתֵיהּ סַגְיָא בִּמְשַׁח רְבוּתָא וּמְטוֹל דְלָא צַלִי בְּיוֹמָא דְכִפּוּרֵי בְקוֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשַׁיָא עַל תְּלַת עֲבֵירָן קַשְׁיָן דְלָא יְתַקְלוּן עַמָא בֵּית יִשְרָאֵל בְּפוּלְחָנָא נוּכְרָאָה וּבְגִילוּי עֶרְיוּתָא וּבִשְׁדִיוּת אֲדַם זַכְיָא וַהֲוָה בִידֵיהּ לְבַטְלוּתְהוֹן בִּצְלוֹתֵיהּ וְלָא צַלִי מְטוֹל כֵּן אִתְקְנַס לִמְמַת בְּשַׁתָּא הַהוּא


and the congregation shall release the manslayer from the hand of the avenger of blood, and make him return to his city of refuge whither he had fled; and he shall dwell there until the time that the high priest die, whom the multitude (sagia) had anointed with the oil of anointing;-because he did not pray on the Day of Atonement in the Holy of Holies concerning the three great transgressions, that the people of the house of Israel might not be smitten for strange worship, or impure connexions, or the shedding of innocent blood, when it was in his power to obviate them by his prayer, and he prayed not, therefore hath he been condemned to die in that year.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:26)


וְאִין מֵיפּוֹק יִפּוֹק קָטוֹלָא כָּל אֵימַת דְכַהֲנָא רַבָּא קְיַים מִן תְּחוּם קַרְתָּא דְקַלְטֵיהּ דַעֲרָק לְתַמָן


But if, while the high priest is yet alive, the manslayer goeth out indeed from the bounds, of his city of refuge whither he had fled,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:27)


וְיַשְׁכַּח יָתֵיהּ תְּבַע אַדְמָא מִבָּרָא לִתְחוּם קַרְתָּא דְקַלְטֵיהּ וְיִקְטוֹל תְּבַע אַדְמָא יַת קָטוֹלָא לֵית לֵיהּ סִדְרִין דִקְטוֹל


and the avenger of blood find him without the bounds of his city of refuge, he may kill the manslayer, without being guilty of death,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:28)


אֲרוּם בְּקוּרְיְיתָא דְקַלְטֵיהּ יְתֵיב עַד דִימוּת כַּהֲנָא רַבָּא וּמִן בָּתַר דִימוּת כַּהֲנָא רַבָּא יְתוּב קָטוֹלָא לְאַרַע אַחֲסַנְתֵּיהּ


for he should have abode in his city of refuge until the death of the high priest; but after the high priest is dead he may return to the land of his inheritance.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:29)


וְיֶהֱווֹן אִלֵין אַחְוָיָתָא לְכוֹן לִגְזֵירַת דִינִין לְדָרֵיכוֹן בְּכָל מוֹתְבָנֵיכוֹן


And these indications shall be to you a decree of judgment for your generations in all your dwellings:


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:30)


כָּל דְיִקְטוֹל בַּר נְשָׁא עַל מֵימַר סַהֲדִין דְחַמְיַין לְמִסְהַד עֲלוֹי יִקְטוֹל תְּבַע אַדְמָא אוֹ בֵי דִינָא יַת קָטוֹלָא וְסָהִיד חַד לָא יִסְהַד בְּבַר נַשׁ לִמְמָת


Whosoever killeth a man, according to the word of witnesses fit to give testimony against him, the avenger of blood, or the house of judgment, shall put him to death. But one witness only shall not testify against a man to put him to death.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:31)


וְלָא תְקַבְּלוּן פּוּרְקַן לְשֵׁיזְבָא בַּר נַשׁ קָטוֹלָא דְהוּא חַיָיב לִמְמָת אֲרוּם אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵיל


You may not take a ransom for the release of a murderer who is guilty of death, for dying he shall die.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:32)


וְלָא תְקַבְּלוּן פּוּרְקַן לִדְעָרַק לְקִרְיְיתָא דְקַלְטֵיהּ לְמֵיתוּב לְמֵיתַב בְּאַרְעָא עַד זְמַן דִימוּת כַּהֲנָא


Neither may you take ransom for him who hath fled to his city of refuge, so as that he may return to dwell in the land before the time of the high priest's decease.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:33)


וְלָא תְטַנְפוּן יַת אַרְעָא דְאַתּוּן בָּהּ אֲרוּם דַם זַכְּאַי דְלָא מִתְפְּרַע הוּא יִטוֹף יַת אַרְעָא וּלְאַרְעָא לָא מִתְכַּפֵּר עַל דַם זַכְּאַי דְאִשְׁתְּדֵי בָּהּ אֱלָהֵן בִּשְׁדִיוּת אֲדַם דְמַאן דְשַׁדְיֵהּ


Nor contaminate ye the land in which you are, because innocent blood which hath not been avenged will overflow the land, and there is no atonement made for the land upon which innocent blood hath been shed, but by the shedding of the blood of him who shed it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 35:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/35:34)


וְלָא תְסַאֲבוּן יַת אַרְעָא דְאַתּוּן בָּהּ אֲרוּם דִשְׁכִינְתִּי שַׁרְיָא בְּגַוָהּ אֲרוּם אֲנָא הוּא יְיָ דִשְׁכִינְתִּי שַׁרְיָא בְּגוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


Therefore defile not the land in which you are; for My Shekinah dwelleth in the midst of it; for I am the Lord whose Shekinah dwelleth among the children of Israel.



## Chapter 36

###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:1)


וּקְרִיבוּ לְבֵי דִינָא רֵישֵׁי אַבְהָתָא לִגְנִיסַת בְּנֵי גִלְעָד בַּר מָכִיר בַּר מְנַשֶׁה וְלִגְנִיסַת בְּנֵי גִלְעָד בַּר יוֹסֵף וּמַלִילוּ קֳדָם משֶׁה וְקָדָם רַבְרְבַיָא רֵישֵׁי אַבְהָתָא לִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


And the heads of the fathers of the family of the Beni Gilead bar Makir bar Menasheh, even the family of the Beni Gilead bar Joseph, came to the house of judgment, and spake before Mosheh and the princes, the chief fathers of the Beni Israel,


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:2)


וַאֲמָרוּ יַת רִבּוֹנִי פַּקֵיד יְיָ לְמִתַּן יַת אַרְעָא בְּאַחְסַנְתָּא בְּעַדְבָא לִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְרִבּוֹנִי אִתְפַּקֵיד מִן קֳדָם יְיָ לְמִתַּן יַת אַחְסָנַת צְלָפְחָד אָחוּנָא לִבְנָתֵיהּ


and said: The Lord commanded Rabboni {our lord} to give the land an inheritance by lot to the children of Israel, and Rabboni was commanded before the Lord to give the inheritance of our brother Zelophehad to his daughters.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 36:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:3)


וַהֲוָאָה לְחַד מִבְּנֵי שִׁבְטַיָא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לִנְשִׁין וְתִתְמְנַע אַחְסַנְתְּהוֹן מֵאַחְסָנַת אַבְהָתָנָא וְתִיתוֹסַף עַל אַחְסָנַת שִׁבְטַיָא דְיֶהֱוְיָן לְהוֹן וּמִן עֲדַב אַחְסַנְתָּנָא יִתְמְנַע


But if these marry into any of the tribes of the children of Israel, their inheritance will be withdrawn from that of our fathers, and will be added to the inheritance of the tribe which will have become theirs, and our lot will be diminished.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 36:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:4)


וְאִין יֶהֱוֵי יוּבְלָא לִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל וְתִיתוֹסַף אַחְסַנְתְּהוֹן עַל אַחְסָנַת שִׁבְטַיָא דְיֶהֱוְיָן לְהוֹן וּמֵאַחְסָנַת שִׁבְטָא דְאַבְהָתָנָא יִתְמְנַע אַחְסַנְתְּהוֹן


And at the Jubilee of the Beni Israel their inheritance will be added to that of their tribe in which they will be; and their possession will have been withdrawn from the inheritance of our father's tribe.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 36:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:5)


וּפַקֵיד משֶׁה יַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל עַל מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ לְמֵימָר יָאוּת שִׁבְטָא דִבְנֵי יוֹסֵף מְמַלְלִין


Then Mosheh commanded the children of Israel by the Word of the Lord, saying: The tribe of the Beni Joseph have said well.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 36:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:6)


דֵין פִּתְגָמָא דְפַקֵיד יְיָ לָא לְדָרַיָא דַעֲתִידִין לְמֵיקוּם בָּתַר פִּילוּג אַרְעָא אֱלָהֵן לִבְנַת צְלָפְחָד לְמֵימָר לִדְתַקִין בְּעֵינֵיהוֹן תֶּהֱוְיָן לִנְשִׁין לְחוֹד לִגְנִיסַת שֵׁבֶט אֲבוּהֶן תֶּהֱוְיַן לִנְשִׁין


This is the thing which the Lord hath commanded,-not for the generations that shall arise after the division of the land, but for the daughters of Zelophehad, saying: They may be the wives of them who are proper in their eyes, only such must be of the families of their father's tribe.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 36:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:7)


מְטוּל דְלָא תִיתְקֵף אַחְסָנָא לִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל מִשִׁבְטָא לְשִׁבְטָא חוֹרָנָא אֲרוּם גְבַר בְּאַחְסָנַת שִׁבְטָא דְאַבְהָתוֹי יִדְבְּקוּן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


That the inheritance of the children of Israel may not pass about from one tribe to another: for the children of Israel shall every one keep to the inheritance of their father's tribe.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 36:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:8)


וְכָל בְּרַתָּא יַרְתַת אַחְסָנָא מִשִׁבְטָא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל לְחַד מִזַרְעִית שִׁבְטָא דַאֲבוּהָא תְּהֵי לְאִנְתּוּ בְּדִיל דְיֵרְתוּן בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל גְבַר אַחְסָנַת אַבְהָתוֹהִי


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 36:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:9)


וְלָא תַסְחַר אַחְסָנָא מִשִׁבְטָא לְשִׁבְטָא אוֹחֲרָנָא אֲרֵי גְבַר בְּאַחְסַנְתֵּיהּ יִדְבְּקוּן שִׁבְטַיָא דִבְנֵי יִשְרָאֵל


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 36:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:10)


הֵיכְמָא דְפַקֵיד יְיָ יַת משֶׁה הֵיכְדֵין עֲבָדוּ בְּנַת צְלָפְחָד


As the Lord commanded Mosheh, so did the daughters of Zelophehad;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 36:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:11)


וַהֲוָאָה מַחֲלָה תִרְצָה וְחָגְלָה וּמִלְכָּה וְנוֹעָה בְּנַת צְלָפְחָד לִבְנֵי חֲבִיבֵיהוֹן לִנְשִׁין


and Mahalah, Thirzah, Hogelab, Milchah and Nohah, the daughters of Zelophehad, became wives of sons of their kindred;


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 36:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:12)


מִגְנִיסַת בְּנֵי מְנַשֶׁה בַּר יוֹסֵף הֲוָאָה לִנְשִׁין וַהֲוַת אַחְסַנְתְּהוֹן עַל שֵׁיבַט גְנִיסַת אֲבוּהֶן


of the family of the children of Menasheh bar Joseph were they wives, and their inheritance was with the tribe of their father's family.


###### Targum Jonathan on Numbers 36:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Numbers/r/36:13)


אִלֵין פִּקוּדַיָא וְסִדְרֵי דִינַיָא דְפַקֵיד יְיָ בִּידָא דְמשֶׁה לְוַת בְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל בְּמֵישְׁרַיָא דְמוֹאָב עַל יַרְדְנָא דִירֵיחוֹ


These are the commandments and orders of judgments which the Lord commanded the children of Israel, by Mosheh, in the plains of Moab by the Jordan near Jericho.
